[1] scOPD: Routed On-Policy Self-Distillation for LLM-Based Single-Cell Annotation cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Zhiyin Yu, Yuchen Mou, Songtao Huang, Shuaiyu Zhang, Xing Wei

TL;DR: 论文提出 scOPD，一个面向基于 LLM 的单细胞注释的 on-policy 自蒸馏框架。该方法在推理微调后，将合格 rollout 路由到基于奖励的优化，对错误 rollout 则利用成功同批轨迹提供特权 token 级监督；推理阶段利用 rollout 分歧触发约束感知的成对精炼。

Details

Motivation: 现有 LLM 单细胞注释方法主要依赖标量结果奖励，对中间生物推理监督不足；批级注释还需同时解决局部细胞身份识别和全局一对一匹配约束，单次推理也难以定位不确定预测。

Result: 在 CellPuzzles 和四个域外疾病数据集上，scOPD 一致提升多种 LLM 主干性能；使用 Qwen3-8B 时达到平均细胞级准确率 81.71% 和批级准确率 44.49%。

Insight: 创新点在于将路由式 on-policy 自蒸馏用于单细胞注释：合格轨迹走奖励优化，错误轨迹从成功兄弟轨迹获得 token 级特权监督，并在推理时用 rollout 分歧进行约束感知的成对修正。客观看，其价值在于把细粒度推理监督和不确定性感知精炼结合到结构化批级注释任务中。

Abstract: Motivation: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for single-cell annotation, but existing training methods mainly rely on scalar outcome rewards that provide limited supervision for intermediate biological reasoning. Batch-level annotation is particularly challenging because it requires jointly resolving local cell identities and a global one-to-one matching constraint, such that one local error can affect the entire assignment. Moreover, a single inference pass provides insufficient evidence for locating uncertain predictions. These limitations call for fine-grained reasoning supervision during training and uncertainty-aware refinement during inference. Results: To address these challenges, we propose scOPD, an on-policy self-distillation framework for LLM-based single-cell annotation. After reasoning fine-tuning, scOPD adaptively routes qualified rollouts to reward-based optimization, while erroneous rollouts receive privileged token-level supervision from successful sibling trajectories. During inference, rollout disagreement triggers constraint-aware pairwise refinement of uncertain assignments. Across CellPuzzles and four out-of-domain disease datasets, scOPD consistently improves the performance of diverse LLM backbones, achieving average cell-level and batch-level accuracies of 81.71% and 44.49% with Qwen3-8B. Availability and Implementation: Code and data are available at https://github.com/YuZhiyin/scOPD.

[2] RECAP: Training Long-horizon LLM Agents with Context Compaction cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Yulun Jiang, Maria Brbic, Michael Moor

TL;DR: 论文提出 RECAP，一个用于长程 LLM 智能体上下文压缩的强化学习框架。其核心思想是压缩摘要应保持原始交互历史所诱导的智能体行为，并据此设计行为失真目标来联合优化摘要生成与压缩后的后续动作。实验表明该方法在多个长程任务基准上优于仅使用任务奖励的 RL 基线。

Details

Motivation: 长程任务超出 LLM 智能体的上下文预算，需要用压缩摘要替代交互历史；但摘要会影响后续多步交互与任务成败，如何将压缩学习与智能体策略联合优化仍是难点。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-4B 上，RECAP 在 BrowseComp-plus 和 SWE-bench Verified 上分别取得 4.6% 和 4.3% 的绝对提升，稳定优于仅使用任务奖励的 RL 基线；消融实验验证了联合监督摘要生成与压缩后行为的重要性。

Insight: 创新点在于以“压缩前后行为一致性”为原则定义行为失真目标，并将该目标的梯度分解为摘要生成的失真奖励和后续动作的自蒸馏信号，从而实现对摘要与压缩后策略的联合监督。

Abstract: Context compaction enables large language model agents to tackle long-horizon tasks within limited context budgets by replacing interaction histories with compressed summaries. However, learning effective compaction jointly with the agent policy remains challenging, as summaries affect task success over many subsequent interactions. We introduce RECAP, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework based on the principle that a summary should preserve the behavior induced by the original interaction history. We formalize this principle with a behavior distortion objective that measures the divergence between the agent’s subsequent behavior under compressed and original contexts. We show that the gradient of the distortion objective decomposes into two complementary learning signals: distortion-based reward for summary generation and self-distillation for subsequent actions. Experiments on Qwen3.5-4B show that RECAP consistently outperforms the task-reward-only RL baselines on both environments, leading to an absolute improvement of 4.6% and 4.3% on BrowseComp-plus and SWE-bench Verified, respectively. Ablations further highlight the value of jointly supervising summary generation and post-compaction behavior for RECAP.

[3] RegioZH: Unveiling Comprehension Disparities across Regional Chinese Varieties in LLMs cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Weihua Zheng, Wu Kui, Thong T. Doan, Lin Jingxia, AiTi Aw

TL;DR: 论文提出RegioZH，一个包含2607道选择题的区域中文变体基准，覆盖中国大陆、台湾、香港、新加坡和马来西亚，用于评测LLM对本地化成语、俚语、语码混合、礼貌规范和社会文化表达的理解。作者在11个LLM上进行零样本评测，发现区域间准确率差异显著，并分析提示、表示、注意力和校准等层面的失败原因。

Details

Motivation: 现有评测常将中文视为单一整体，掩盖了LLM对区域中文变体的理解失败。论文旨在揭示区域用户在本地化语言和社会文化表达上的理解差异，推动语言内鲁棒性评测。

Result: 在RegioZH上，11个LLM零样本评测显示明显区域差异，台湾与新加坡平均准确率差距达29.4个百分点；对5个模型使用persona和结构化CoT提示仅带来选择性提升。分析还发现过度自信的强制选择错误，且局部词汇密度是稳定的难度预测因素。

Insight: 创新点在于构建由区域标注者编写、专家验证的区域中文变体多选基准，并系统揭示表面中文能力可能掩盖区域理解不均衡。客观看，其价值在于将“中文能力”细化为跨区域语言内鲁棒性评测，并通过提示与分析区分知识缺失、检索/框定问题和语义接地不足。

Abstract: Current evaluations often treat Chinese varieties as monolithic, obscuring failures for regional users. We introduce RegioZH, a 2,607-item multiple-choice benchmark for regional lexical reasoning across Mainland China, Taiwan, Hong Kong, Singapore, and Malaysia. Built by region-specific annotators and expert-validated, it targets localized idioms, slang, code-mixing, politeness conventions, and sociocultural expressions. Zero-shot results on eleven LLMs show large disparities, including a 29.4-point average accuracy gap between Taiwan and Singapore. Persona and structured chain-of-thought prompts for five models yield selective gains, suggesting that some errors stem from retrieval or framing rather than only missing knowledge. Representation, attention, calibration, and surface-feature analyses show a dissociation between marker attention and semantic grounding, overconfident forced-choice errors, and localized lexical density as a consistent difficulty predictor. Qualitative analysis finds terminology misunderstandings, sociocultural inference gaps, and cross-regional semantic divergence. RegioZH demonstrates that apparent Chinese proficiency can mask uneven understanding across regional varieties, motivating intra-language robustness evaluation alongside multilingual generalization.

[4] Evaluating Large Language Models on Iranian Doctoral Entrance Exams in Electrical and Computer Engineering cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Sima Akbari Neisiyani, Hossein Taghizad

TL;DR: 论文评估了GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Claude Sonnet 4.5和Grok 4在2024/2025伊朗电气工程与计算机工程博士入学考试选择题上的表现。数据集包含433道题（EE 186、CE 247），其中171道依赖图形，题目以波斯语原文和英译呈现，共得到3464次评分回答。模型以零样本、开启推理、通过Perplexity网页接口查询，并使用配对检验和按题目聚类分析。

Details

Motivation: 关注LLM在非英语、专业性强且含图形推理的研究生入学考试中的能力，尤其是电气与计算机工程博士入学考试的波斯语/英语双语场景。

Result: 准确率呈两档：GPT-5（79.9%）与Gemini 2.5 Pro（78.4%）统计上不可区分，并显著高于Grok 4（71.7%）和Claude Sonnet 4.5（71.3%）6.7–8.7个百分点。英语与波斯语准确率差异小且不显著；含图题显著更难，调整学科后优势比为0.30；EE表面劣势在控制图形题比例后消失。

Insight: 创新点在于构建并发布伊朗EE/CE博士入学考试双语评测集与题目级回答/分析代码，采用所有模型同题作答的配对统计和题目聚类推断，并系统量化图形依赖与语言翻译对LLM表现的影响。客观看，其价值主要是面向非英语专业考试的可复现评测协议和细粒度误差分析，而非提出新模型。

Abstract: We evaluate four large language models (LLMs) on multiple-choice questions from the 2024 and 2025 Iranian national Ph.D. entrance examinations in Electrical Engineering (EE) and Computer Engineering (CE). Of the 440 questions in the four official booklets, 7 were excluded because the question statement or its figure was ambiguous, leaving 433 questions (186 EE, 247 CE), of which 171 (39.5%) depend on a figure. Each question was presented in the original Persian and in an English translation, giving 3,464 scored responses. GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro, Claude Sonnet 4.5 and Grok 4 were queried zero-shot, with reasoning enabled, through a single commercial web interface (Perplexity) in October 2025, using an automated script. Because every model answers the same items, all inferences use paired tests or question-clustered models. Accuracy separates into two tiers: GPT-5 (79.9%, 95% CI 76.6–83.1) and Gemini 2.5 Pro (78.4%, 74.8–81.9) are statistically indistinguishable, and both outperform Grok 4 (71.7%) and Claude Sonnet 4.5 (71.3%) by 6.7–8.7 percentage points (Holm-adjusted $p < 0.001$). The English–Persian accuracy difference is small for every model ($-0.2$ to $+2.5$ points; exact McNemar $p \ge 0.28$). For three models the 90% confidence interval lies within $\pm 5$ points, so differences larger than this can be excluded. Questions with figures are much harder: the odds ratio is 0.30 (95% CI 0.21–0.43) after adjusting for discipline. The apparent EE deficit of two models disappears once the larger share of figure-based EE items is accounted for. We describe the dataset, the protocol and its limitations, including the uncontrolled web-retrieval and decoding settings of the interface, and release item-level responses and analysis code.

[5] AhaPro: A Process-level Rebus benchmark for probing Aha moments in LLM reasoning cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Wei Guo, Lang Qin, Yundi Gai, Qinghua Xu, Zhi Wu

TL;DR: 论文提出AhaPro，一个过程级rebus谜题基准，用于探测大语言模型推理中的Aha moments。它将思维链表示为候选答案、证据和验证状态的序列，并用分层指标评估冲突检测、状态改进及后续采纳。作者在11个模型上发现，模型常无法采纳Aha时刻发现的更优状态，且有效修正受先验推理上下文和答案选择瓶颈制约。

Details

Motivation: 现有Aha moments研究多关注反思性表达或最终修订结果，对可见推理过程中Aha时刻如何触发证据重组和答案修订刻画不足。论文希望过程级地考察模型在关键细节重新解释后如何检测冲突、改进状态并采纳新答案。

Result: 在11个模型上的评测显示，模型频繁未能采纳Aha时刻发现的更好状态，已采纳的修订也可能不改变或降低状态质量；模型能检测冲突但后续修正无效。配对干预表明先验推理上下文可能阻碍冲突检测后的有效修订，多分支搜索则显示答案选择是正确发现与成功修正之间的瓶颈。

Insight: 创新点在于用rebus谜题构建过程级Aha moments基准，并将CoT形式化为候选答案、证据与验证状态序列，以分层指标评估冲突检测、状态改进和后续采纳。客观来看，其价值在于从最终结果转向推理状态动态，揭示了“发现更优状态却难以采纳”和答案选择瓶颈等过程性问题。

Abstract: Existing research on Aha moments in large language models has largely focused on reflective expressions or final revision outcomes, leaving their role in the visible reasoning process insufficiently characterized. Rebus puzzles encode answers through character forms, spatial arrangements, and homophony. Reinterpreting key details may trigger Aha moments and shifts in the reasoning process and candidate answers. We introduce AhaPro, a process-level benchmark that uses rebus puzzles to examine how models reorganize evidence and revise answers through Aha moments. The framework represents CoT as sequences of candidate answers, evidence, and verification states, using hierarchical metrics to assess conflict detection, state improvement, and subsequent adoption. Across eleven models, we observe frequent failures to adopt better states discovered during Aha moments, alongside adopted revisions that leave state quality unchanged or reduce it. Models also detect conflicts without subsequently achieving an effective revision. Further paired interventions suggest that prior reasoning context may hinder effective revision after conflict detection, while multi-branch search highlights answer selection as a bottleneck between correct-answer discovery and successful correction.

[6] LUCID: Statistically Grounded Self-Correction for LLM Reasoning Evaluation cs.CL | cs.LG | stat.MEPDF

Satiyabooshan Murugaboopathy, Adel Daoud, Connor T. Jerzak

TL;DR: 论文提出 LUCID，一种无需重训练或访问模型权重的 LLM 推理步骤评分自校正框架。它先用少量专家标注步骤总结评分器偏差并以文本反馈提示评分器重评，再结合 Design-based Supervised Learning（DSL）进行统计校准与去偏。实验显示 LUCID 能显著缩小 GPT-5-mini 在 PRM800K 上的校准差距、降低假警报并提升错误检测 F1，且可跨模型和领域迁移；DSL 还改善了 best-of-K 选择和小预算去偏估计。

Details

Motivation: 廉价 LLM 评分器可大规模评估思维链推理，但其系统性步骤评分误差会扭曲推理审计和解答排序；论文希望在无重训练、无权重访问条件下校准二元步骤评级，使预测与专家 soundness 率对齐。

Result: 在 PRM800K 上，LUCID 一次处理将 GPT-5-mini 的绝对预测-专家率差从 7.8 降至 0.9 个百分点，假警报减半，unsound 步骤错误检测 F1 提升 32%；1000 个诊断步骤或仅总体差距反馈即可保持增益。该方法可跨模型和领域迁移，并缩小数学、科学、网页和逻辑基准中过严评分器的校准差距；DSL 的 out-of-fold soundness 概率使 best-of-K 选择匹配专家标签，双稳健估计器在小预算下优于仅专家回归拟合，并将 sound-step transition 误差降低近三分之二。

Insight: 创新点在于将提示式自校正与 Design-based Supervised Learning 结合，用少量专家标注诊断并文本化评分器偏差，实现无需重训练的统计校准和跨模型/领域迁移；同时引入前后检查跳过已校准评分器并回滚无效重评。客观看，其可借鉴之处是轻量级评分器偏差诊断、小样本专家校准、可迁移偏差画像，以及用 DSL 对 LLM 评分进行去偏和下游选择/转移估计。

Abstract: Cheap language-model raters grade chain-of-thought reasoning at scale, yet systematic step-rating errors can skew reasoning audits and solution rankings. We introduce LUCID (Loop for Unbiased Calibration via Inference-time Diagnosis), which pairs prompt self-correction with Design-based Supervised Learning (DSL). Without retraining or weight access, LUCID calibrates binary step ratings by aligning predicted and expert soundness rates. From a small expert-labeled set, LUCID summarizes rater bias in plain text and prompts the rater to re-grade unseen steps. For example, on PRM800K, GPT-5-mini grades too strictly, under-rating computation, verification, and late-trace steps most acutely. In one pass, LUCID shrinks GPT-5-mini’s calibration gap from 7.8 to 0.9 percentage points in absolute predicted-expert rate difference. LUCID also halves false alarms (sound steps rated unsound) and lifts error-detection F1 on unsound steps by 32%. LUCID retains these gains with 1,000 diagnostic steps or overall-gap feedback alone. The loop also transfers across models and domains: it applies GPT-5-mini’s bias profile to lift GLM-5.2’s PRM800K accuracy, and it narrows calibration gaps for overly strict but capable raters across math, science, web, and logic benchmarks. Because prompt feedback shifts thresholds without improving class-balanced sound/unsound discrimination, LUCID filters re-grading with pre-/post-checks that skip already calibrated raters and roll back ineffective passes. Finally, we reuse that expert set with raw corpus ratings in DSL for calibration and regression debiasing, and apply DSL to pair-labeled steps for sound-step transitions. DSL’s out-of-fold soundness probabilities lift best-of-K selection to match expert labels, while its doubly-robust estimators beat expert-only regression fits at small budgets and cut sound-step transition error by nearly two-thirds.

[7] Training decision models for simulating human respondents cs.CL | cs.LG | econ.GNPDF

Davood Wadi, Yu Ma

TL;DR: 论文研究用决策模型（System One架构）模拟人类受访者，通过对历史受访者选择微观数据进行选择训练（条件最大似然更新KEV权重），减少LLM合成选择数据在下游计量推断中的偏差。在多个新领域和新响应格式的留出研究上，处理效应误差降低22%至89%，且小型选择训练模型可优于更大的现成模型。

Details

Motivation: 现成生成式LLM作为合成受访者会产生有偏选择数据，进而偏置下游计量推断；专有模型或更高推理努力也不能可靠降低该偏差，因此需要无需目标研究数据的训练方法。

Result: 跨三个模型规模，在两个离散选择实验上训练后，于疫苗、营养标签、消费者权益和AI四个新领域及新响应格式的留出研究上，处理效应误差降低22%至89%；小型选择训练模型在四项研究中的三项优于更大的现成模型；单研究训练虽降交叉熵，但可能加剧处理效应误差。

Insight: 创新点在于将System One决策模型用于silicon sampling，并在推理前用历史选择微数据做条件最大似然选择训练；客观看，其强调训练目标应关注下游处理效应而非仅交叉熵，且选择训练可作为后推理校准的更强起点。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use large language models (LLMs) as synthetic respondents in market research, conjoint analysis, and randomized experiments. However, synthetic choice data from off-the-shelf generative LLMs bias downstream econometric inference. Neither proprietary models nor higher reasoning effort reliably reduces this bias. We investigate decision models (System One architectures) for silicon sampling. Using choice training, we update KEV model weights on historical respondent-level choice microdata by conditional maximum likelihood, before inference and without target-study data. Across three model scales, training on two discrete choice experiments lowers treatment-effect error by 22% to 89% on four held-out studies from new domains (vaccines, nutrition labels, consumer rights, and AI) and new response formats. A small choice-trained model outperforms a much larger off-the-shelf model on three of the four studies. Although training on single studies lowers cross-entropy, it can exacerbate treatment-effect error. Choice training thus complements post-inference calibration by providing a stronger starting point for estimating human preferences.

[8] SPARQL-Planned Single-Hop Traversal over a Live Knowledge Graph for Multi-Hop Question Answering cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Priyesh Vakharia, Masud Moshtaghi, Pratyay Banerjee, Nicholas Jedema, Amita Misra

TL;DR: 论文面向多跳问答，提出将稠密检索与外部知识图谱遍历交错进行：先用稠密检索定位相关区域，再由LLM以SPARQL形式规划单跳KG遍历，并解析而非执行该查询，用推理路径确认检索段落。方法在多个多跳QA基准上超过复现的HippoRAG2、T²RAG和IRCoT。

Details

Motivation: 语义检索在多跳问答中召回率和精确率不足，金标段落易被干扰项淹没；GraphRAG式多跳扩展虽提升召回，但成本高且引入额外噪声。论文希望打破召回率与噪声/成本之间的权衡。

Result: 在共享语料和reader、统一配置下，HotpotQA达到62.5/75.1 EM/F1，2WikiMultiHopQA达到73.5/81.0，MuSiQue达到37.1/50.0，三项基准均优于复现的HippoRAG2、T²RAG和IRCoT；token匹配设置下在HotpotQA和2Wiki上仍优于T²RAG。直接执行生成SPARQL而非解析会最多损失11.4 EM，主要原因是原始查询返回空结果。

Insight: 创新点在于将LLM规划的SPARQL作为单跳KG遍历的约束/确认机制而非直接执行，从而兼顾问题自适应性与可靠性；交错稠密检索和实时Wikidata遍历，在控制多跳扩展成本和噪声的同时提升多跳QA的召回与精确率。

Abstract: Semantic retrieval struggles with multi-hop question answering on both recall and precision: gold passages are buried among distractors that degrade the reader’s answer, and retrieving them requires reasoning over the query rather than matching it. GraphRAG techniques expand semantic retrieval by connecting passages to graph nodes and performing one or more hops of expansion after initial retrieval. This improves recall, but multi-hop expansion becomes expensive and brings additional noise into the retrieved set. We break this tradeoff by \emph{interleaving} dense retrieval with knowledge-graph traversal for complex multi-hop questions. Dense retrieval locates the first relevant regions of the search space at good recall, while traversal of a pre-existing external KG (Wikidata) restores precision and improves recall by confirming retrieved passages against a reasoning path through the KG. To keep traversal both question-adaptive and reliable, we have an LLM plan it as code generation task for graph traversal in SPARQL format but \emph{parse rather than execute} the query. On full 1{,}000-question splits under a shared corpus and reader, and with a single configuration applied identically to every dataset, we reach \textbf{62.5 / 75.1} EM/F1 on HotpotQA, \textbf{73.5 / 81.0} on 2WikiMultiHopQA, and \textbf{37.1 / 50.0} on MuSiQue, ahead of reproduced HippoRAG2, T\textsuperscript{2}RAG, and IRCoT on all three benchmarks. A second, token-matched setting stays ahead of T\textsuperscript{2}RAG on HotpotQA and 2Wiki while spending roughly its per-query budget. Executing the generated SPARQL instead of parsing it costs up to 11.4 EM, mostly because raw queries return empty results, not because of syntax errors.

[9] Exploring Multi-Player Social Games as Verifiable Training Grounds for Large Language Models cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.LG | cs.MAPDF

Keyu He, Michael Li, Jiarui Liu, Mingqian Zheng, Koichi Onoue

TL;DR: 论文探索用多人社交游戏作为大语言模型的可验证训练场：游戏规则提供客观胜负结果，可替代昂贵且有噪声的LLM评判器作为奖励。作者用三种社交游戏对Qwen3模型进行SFT和RLVR训练，发现模型在训练过的游戏和未见游戏上的胜率均显著提升，并表现出更强的信息共享、证据核验和投票协调等社交行为。

Details

Motivation: 现有社交智能语言模型训练常依赖LLM裁判，成本高、噪声大且易被奖励黑客攻击；多人社交游戏需要开放沟通、团队协作和隐藏角色推断，同时规则能给出客观可验证的结果，因此可作为更可靠的训练信号来源。

Result: 实验以更强的Gemma-3-27B为对手，SFT和RLVR使Qwen3-4B在三个训练游戏上的胜率从33%提升到81%，在三个额外留出游戏上从39%提升到54%；相比之下，提示等上下文策略仅提升约6个百分点。增益在1.7B、4B和32B模型规模上均成立。

Insight: 创新点在于将多人社交游戏规则产生的客观胜负作为可验证奖励，避免依赖LLM裁判，并证明这种训练能带来可泛化的社交行为。客观分析上，其价值主要在于用游戏结果提供可扩展、低成本的监督信号，同时通过留出游戏验证一定泛化性，而不仅是记忆训练游戏策略。

Abstract: The training of socially intelligent language models often relies on large language model (LLM) judges, which are expensive, noisy, and vulnerable to reward hacking. Multi-player social games offer a natural alternative: they require open-ended communication to coordinate with teammates and infer hidden roles, while their rules provide an objective outcome which can serve as a verifiable reward for training. Using three social games, we explore how much social intelligence Qwen3 models can acquire through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). Results show that against a much stronger Gemma-3-27B model, SFT and RLVR raise the win rate of Qwen3-4B from 33% to 81% on the three trained games, and from 39% to 54% on three additional held-out games. In contrast, in-context strategies such as prompting improve the win rate of Qwen3-4B by only about 6 pp across the same games we train and evaluate on. Furthermore, we show that the gains from SFT and RLVR hold across the model sizes 1.7B, 4B, and 32B. Qualitative analyses of agent behavior show signs of stronger social intelligence; for example, trained agents tend to share information more openly, check each other’s claims against public evidence, and coordinate their votes. More broadly, our results demonstrate that training LLMs on verifiable outcomes in social games can cultivate generalizable social behaviors, which offers a scalable path for building socially intelligent agents.

[10] Localizing Cross-Lingual Generalization in Parameter Space cs.CLPDF

Yeoreum Lee, Jaeseong Lee, Seung-won Hwang

TL;DR: 论文研究跨语言迁移在不同任务上表现不均的现象，提出该不对称性由参数空间几何决定：通过线性模式连接（LMC）分析英语与目标语言LoRA适配器是否共享低误差盆地，发现NLI和QA任务对大多线性连接而数学推理任务不连接，且这种不连接是局部的，可用小样本修复，并进一步将跨语言泛化分解为语言特定编码和语言无关组合两个可分别提供的组件。

Details

Motivation: 跨语言迁移在自然语言理解任务上成功但在数学推理等任务上常失败，论文旨在从参数空间几何角度解释这种任务依赖的不对称性，并寻找低资源跨语言适应的实用路径。

Result: 在三个任务（NLI、QA、Math）上评估，发现NLI和QA的模型-语言对大多线性连接而Math对不连接；Math的断开是局部的，仅用少量目标语言样本作用于英语适配器的语言编码或语义组合层即可修复；英语任务数据路由到组合层或无标注目标语言语料路由到语言编码层可分别缓解几乎所有配对的问题，其中组合层影响更大且无需目标语言数据。

Insight: 创新点在于从参数空间盆地几何（LMC）解释跨语言泛化的任务依赖性，并首次将跨语言泛化分解为语言特定编码与语言无关组合两个可分离组件，且各组件可由更廉价的信号单独提供，为低资源跨语言适应提供了理论依据和实践方法。

Abstract: Cross-lingual transfer is uneven across tasks, succeeding for natural language understanding but often failing for mathematical reasoning. We argue that this asymmetry is governed by the geometry of parameter space. Specifically, cross-lingual generalization is determined by whether English and target-language LoRA adapters share a low-error basin. To test this, we use linear mode connectivity (LMC) between English and target-language adapters, evaluating on three tasks. We find that NLI and QA model-language pairs are mostly linearly connected, whereas mathematical reasoning (Math) pairs are not, indicating that basin geometry is task-dependent. Such disconnection in Math is ``local’’ rather than global. A small target-language task sample, applied only to the English adapter’s language encoding or semantic composition layers, is enough to repair it. Cross-lingual generalization is also factorizable into language-specific encoding and language-agnostic composition, each supplied separately from a cheaper signal. English task data routed to the composition layers and an unlabeled target-language corpus routed to the language encoding layers each alleviate it on their own for nearly every pair. Composition is the more influential of these, and it needs no target-language data. Together, these results suggest that cross-lingual generalization decomposes into language-specific and language-agnostic components in parameter space. This decomposition offers a practical route for low-resource cross-lingual adaptation.

[11] Grounding Language Models in Revealed Audience Behavior cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Jiwoo Jung, Daecheol An

TL;DR: 论文研究用真实受众行为（点击、点赞、观看）微调语言模型来模拟受众对标题或帖子的反应，系统对比「提问」（prompting）与「接地」（fine-tuning on revealed behavior）两种方式，并追问提问为何、在何处不如接地。作者在四种行为上做了预注册实验，并开源代码、适配器和运行结果。

Details

Motivation: 现有用LLM模拟受众反应的工作多以人们「说的」（陈述偏好）来验证，论文主张应基于人们「做的」（真实行为）来验证，并深入分析提问式模拟失败的原因与适用边界。

Result: 同一8B模型被提问时预测读者点击正确率约50%，接地后达86.5%；最强prompting仅与文本分类器相当；接地的0.6B模型胜过所有prompted前沿模型；短文点赞上接地领先20分（200个预注册视频）；但给定读者历史时新闻点击仅持平，纯文本在观看时长上难超随机水平。

Insight: 创新点在于以真实行为记录而非陈述偏好作为验证与训练信号，并给出「why/where」的实证剖析：差距源于训练信号而非模型规模或提示方式，且接地只在文本本身携带信号时有效，界定了该范式的适用边界。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used to simulate how an audience will respond to a headline or a post, and such simulators are validated against what people say. We validate them against what people do. We call a model grounded in revealed audience behavior when it is fine-tuned on records of what audiences actually clicked, liked or watched, and we ask not only whether asking a model falls short of grounding it, as prior work found, but why and where. Why: the gap is a property of the training signal, not mainly of model size or prompting. The same 8B model is right about half the time when asked which headline readers will click and 86.5% of the time when grounded in the clicks; the strongest prompting we tried only matches a text classifier; a grounded 0.6B model beats every prompted frontier model in our runs; and whether the clicks are fitted as counts or as shares makes no detectable difference in a pre-registered test. The asked 8B model also reproduces stated-preference cues, favoring question headlines that in fact lower clicks. Where: across four behaviors, grounding wins where the text carries the signal (short-video likes, +20 points on 200 pre-registered videos), only ties an asked model given the reader’s history (news clicks), and nothing text-only gets far from chance on watch time. Code, adapters and run outputs will be released.

[12] Making Evaluation Explicit Is Not Enough: Supervising the Evaluation Process in Generative Reward Models cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Qiyuan Chen, Yuze Zhao, Jiahe Chen, Hongsen Huang, Hongxia Xu

TL;DR: 论文指出生成式奖励模型虽然会先生成评语、评分标准或中间分析再给出偏好判断，但仅用最终偏好做监督可能导致中间评估不可靠，形成事后合理化。为此提出STEP-GRM，对基于评分标准的GRM中间评估过程进行直接监督：除偏好监督外，还提供评分标准构建的结构引导和准则级判断的局部监督。实验表明该方法在标准奖励建模基准上表现较强，并提升中间评估的一致性与正确性，其奖励信号还能带来更好的下游策略优化效果。

Details

Motivation: 现有生成式奖励模型强调显式评估，但仅用最终偏好结果监督时，模型可能给出正确偏好却产生薄弱或无依据的中间判断，使评估过程不可靠。论文旨在解决显式评估不等于可靠评估过程的问题。

Result: 在标准奖励建模基准上取得较强性能，同时提升中间评估的一致性和正确性；其产生的奖励信号还能改善下游策略优化，说明可靠评估过程可带来超越奖励准确率的实际收益。

Insight: 创新点在于不局限于最终偏好监督，而是直接监督基于评分标准的中间评估过程，包括评分标准构建的结构引导和准则级局部监督。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是将过程监督引入生成式奖励模型，并验证评估过程可靠性对下游策略优化的实际价值。

Abstract: Generative reward models (GRMs) make language-model evaluation more explicit by generating critiques, rubrics, or intermediate analyses before predicting a final preference. However, explicit evaluation does not necessarily imply a reliable evaluation process: under outcome-only supervision, a model may reach the correct final preference while producing weak or unsupported intermediate judgments, turning the evaluation into a post-hoc rationalization. We introduce STEP-GRM, a framework that directly supervises the intermediate evaluation process of rubric-based GRMs. In addition to conventional preference supervision, STEP-GRM provides structural guidance for rubric construction and local supervision for criterion-level judgments, encouraging the model to learn not only the correct final preference but also a more reliable process for reaching it. Extensive experiments show that STEP-GRM achieves strong performance on standard reward-modeling benchmarks while improving the consistency and correctness of intermediate evaluations. Moreover, reward signals produced by STEP-GRM lead to better downstream policy optimization, demonstrating that more reliable evaluation processes can translate into practical gains beyond reward-model accuracy.

[13] RightDiff: Learning Generative Reward Models to Judge by the Right Differences cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Qiyuan Chen, Hongsen Huang, Jiahe Chen, Renjie Hua, Hongxia Xu

TL;DR: 论文提出RightDiff，将成对偏好评估结构化为一次自回归生成中的证据驱动评估轨迹：显式发现候选响应间的对比差异，区分实质与非实质因素，并诱导成对特定评分规则后进行裁决。训练上通过响应隔离提示合成演示数据，并用差异对齐奖励、预算约束的一对一匹配和乘性门控优化奖励模型。

Details

Motivation: 现有生成式奖励模型多依赖查询级通用评分规则或无约束理由，忽视了成对偏好由响应特定差异驱动；仅优化结果正确性会诱发冗长、格式等表面捷径和理由漂移。

Result: RightDiff在RewardBench和RM-Bench上取得SOTA偏好建模性能，超过所有开源奖励模型并领先专有LLM评判器；下游可为Arena-Hard和AlpacaEval上的DPO提供可靠监督，并有效抵抗长度捷径。

Insight: 创新点在于将成对评估显式结构化为差异发现、实质/非实质分类、成对规则诱导和最终裁决的统一生成轨迹；训练上以响应隔离提示合成数据避免事后合理化，并用差异对齐奖励结合预算约束匹配与乘性门控提升偏好监督质量。

Abstract: Generative reward models typically rely on generic query-level rubrics or unconstrained rationales, overlooking the fact that pairwise preference is driven by response-specific differences. Moreover, optimizing solely for outcome correctness encourages models to exploit superficial shortcuts such as verbosity and formatting, triggering rationale drift. To address this, we propose RightDiff, which structures pairwise evaluation into an evidence-grounded evaluation trajectory executed in a single autoregressive generation. RightDiff explicitly discovers contrastive differences between candidates, categorizes them into material or immaterial factors, and induces pair-specific rubrics before final adjudication. To train this process without post-hoc rationalization, we synthesize demonstration data via response-isolated prompts and optimize the reward model with a difference-alignment reward regularized by budget-constrained one-to-one matching and multiplicative gating. RightDiff achieves state-of-the-art preference modeling performance on RewardBench and RM-Bench, surpassing all open-source reward models and leading proprietary LLM judges. Downstream, it provides reliable supervision for direct preference optimization on Arena-Hard and AlpacaEval while effectively resisting length shortcuts.

TL;DR: 论文提出 SkillCore，一套数据构建流程与监督微调方案，旨在将通用可迁移技能内化到 LLM agent 中。方法将真实 agent 轨迹中的技能加载脚手架移除，但保留任务上下文和后续动作，从而监督模型直接完成任务而不依赖外部技能内容；同时混入少量保留技能调用轨迹的样本，以保留对真正外部任务特定技能的调用能力。

Details

Motivation: 运行时技能能为代码审查、调试等任务提供过程性指导，但大多数用户不会主动调用技能，收益只覆盖少数人；作者认为这类通用流程应成为模型自身能力而非可选的外部附加组件。

Result: 在无技能的留出评测上，SkillCore 相比基座 Qwen3-8B 和 Qwen2.5-72B 分别提升任务完成质量 54.4% 和 24.8%，并在单独探测任务上保留了任务特定技能调用能力，在 SWE-bench Verified 上保持通用编码性能。

Insight: 创新点在于通过移除脚手架的数据变换来监督模型直接执行任务，从而内化通用技能，同时用少量技能原生轨迹混合训练以保留对外部信息的调用；从客观看，其可借鉴之处是把 agent 轨迹改造成“去技能化”的训练信号，在提升自主能力与保留外部技能调用之间做平衡。

Abstract: Runtime skills can improve agents’ problem-solving capabilities by providing procedural guidance for tasks such as code review and debugging. However, these benefits reach only a minority of users, as most do not proactively invoke skills in practice. We argue that such generalizable procedures should become part of the model’s capabilities rather than remain optional external add-ons, making them directly available without explicit skill loading. To this end, we present SkillCore, a data pipeline and supervised fine-tuning recipe for internalizing generalizable skills. We transform real agent trajectories by removing skill-loading scaffolding while preserving the task context and subsequent agent actions, thereby supervising direct execution without external skill content. To preserve access to task-specific skills that provide genuinely external information, we mix these scaffold-removed trajectories with a small proportion of skill-native trajectories that preserve supervision for skill invocation. On a held-out skill-free evaluation, SkillCore improves task completion quality over the base Qwen3-8B and Qwen2.5-72B models by 54.4% and 24.8%, respectively, while preserving task-specific skill invocation on a separate probe and general coding performance on SWE-bench Verified.

Details

Motivation: 现有OCR和文档理解系统把人工标注当作视觉噪声，而非语义编辑操作，无法输出符合用户修正意图的文本；金融文档中此类人工修订常见且重要。

Result: 在IntentBench上，微调后的3B和7B模型F1分别为0.81和0.83，显著超过GPT-4.1的0.626和LLaMA-3.2-Vision-90B的0.50，表明小规模领域专用模型在该任务上可优于大通用模型。

Abstract: Human annotations on documents (such as arrows, carets, strikethroughs, and insertions) express corrective intent yet existing OCR and document understanding systems treat these marks as visual noise rather than semantic edit operations. We introduce intent-aware OCR, the task of generating corrected transcriptions by jointly reasoning over original document content and visual intent cues (handwritten or digital) that convey edit intent. To support this setting, we present DocIntent, a multimodal dataset of 1,600 financial documents with over 5,700 annotated visual intent cues, each paired with layout-aware, intent-resolved OCR targets. We further introduce IntentBench, a 216-document benchmark designed to evaluate model robustness under diverse real-world annotation conditions. Leveraging DocIntent, we fine-tune DocIntentOCR, a suite of 3B and 7B parameter vision-language models, under direct intent supervision on corrected transcriptions. On IntentBench, the fine-tuned 3B and 7B models achieve F1-scores of 0.81 and 0.83, respectively, substantially outperforming commercial closed-source (e.g., GPT-4.1 (0.626)), and open-source models (e.g., LLaMA-3.2-Vision-90B (0.50)) demonstrating that intent-aware supervision enables compact domain-specific models to outperform substantially larger general-purpose systems on this task. This work establishes intent-aware OCR as a novel human-in-the-loop document understanding task and provides foundational resources (dataset, benchmark, models) to advance research in interactive document processing.

[17] BCP: A Lightweight Boundary-Controlled Framework for Online Sign Language Recognition and Translation cs.CLPDF

Motivation: 在线手语识别和翻译需要随视频流增量输出手语词或文本，但强离线预训练模型无法直接在线部署，现有在线方法通常需在模拟流式前缀上重训整个模型，成本高。论文旨在低成本复用离线预训练模型实现在线输出。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新点包括统一轻量框架、冻结预训练模型、基于CTC对齐无人工标注监督的边界适配器，以及推理时Wait-K前缀投票的自适应突发提交策略。客观来看，其创新在于将离线模型与在线提交策略解耦，以极低参数成本实现离线到在线的转换。

Abstract: Online sign language recognition (SLR) and translation (SLT) incrementally produce gloss sequences or natural language text from sign video as it arrives, a capability essential for real-time communication and live captioning. However, powerful pretrained offline models cannot be directly deployed in this setting, while existing online methods typically retrain the entire model on simulated streaming prefixes constructed from offline videos, incurring high costs. To address this challenge, we propose Boundary-Controlled Prefix Commit (BCP), a unified lightweight framework that converts pretrained offline methods into online counterparts for sign-to-gloss (S2G), sign-to-text (S2T), and sign-to-gloss-to-text (S2G2T). Specifically, BCP freezes the pretrained model and introduces a lightweight boundary adapter that decides when to commit, supervised by Connectionist Temporal Classification (CTC) alignments from a pretrained SLR model, without any human annotation. At inference, a Boundary-Controlled Wait-K Output Policy then decides what to commit via prefix voting, yielding adaptive burst commits while preserving the output progress of the base Wait-K schedule. Experiments on PHOENIX-2014T and CSL-Daily across four offline methods show that BCP, with less than 0.1% additional trainable parameters per offline method, achieves a strong quality-latency trade-off on S2T and remains competitive on S2G and S2G2T.

Abstract: We introduce reinforcement learning from collective rewards (RLCR), a recipe for jointly training language models to act as agent organisations under latency constraints. Abstract: Changes in image viewpoint and question formulation can alter latent knowledge representations in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, output correctness and overall representational drift provide limited insight into how these changes affect model decisions. We therefore evaluate latent knowledge stability across visual and linguistic elicitation conditions from two complementary perspectives: answer preference and internal support. Specifically, we devise the margin retention score (MRS), which measures the first-order projected degradation of the initial answer’s logit margin over competing options across conditions and assesses how changes in latent knowledge representations affect this margin. We also present latent knowledge support-structure stability (KSS), which evaluates reference-correct items by comparing internal support profiles constructed using the margin gradient under the reference condition, which jointly reflects changes in how internal support is distributed and changes in total support magnitude. We further construct MMStableBench, comprising 19,452 knowledge items (facts), each probing the same latent knowledge across varied image viewpoints, semantically equivalent question formulations, and answer-option permutations. Experiments across 15 MLLMs show that MRS and KSS reveal latent knowledge instability not captured by accuracy, while an MRS-inspired regularizer further improves the generalization of edited latent knowledge.

Abstract: Deep search is a critical capability for agents tackling complex, open-ended tasks, where solving a problem requires iterative information gathering, query reformulation, and evidence-based verification rather than a single retrieval step. To address these requirements, we present Bathys 9B and 27B deep search agents built around a training framework that spans verifiable question synthesis, post-training, and online reinforcement learning (RL) under context compression. Our high-quality data construction ensures both search depth and reliability: each clue is reformulated as an indirect reference requiring independent retrieval, and retained evidence chains enable verification that the clues jointly determine a unique answer. Curated trajectories underpin a pipeline of supervised fine-tuning (SFT), Kahneman-Tversky Optimization (KTO), and Compaction-Managed Group Sequence Policy Optimization (CM-GSPO): SFT provides a stable foundation for tool use over long horizons and query reformulation; KTO uses binary feedback on trajectories to improve the selection of answers grounded in evidence; CM-GSPO optimizes execution sub-trajectories with length-normalized likelihood ratios on the recorded rollout contexts and reweights sub-trajectory advantages to correct the extra weighting introduced by independently averaging sub-trajectory losses. Abstract: Answers from generative AI chatbots built on large language models (LLMs) now arrive at very different speeds: reasoning models deliberate before replying, while response caches can return a stored answer at once. Blind preference votes, which rank these models, are read as judgments of their text, and if delivery matters, faster answers are expected to gain. Research on the labor illusion and on how text appears on screen suggests instead that an answer which unfolds as it is written is credited over the same answer shown at once. We test these accounts with three sources of variation in natural use. First, compar:IA, a French government chatbot arena, answered repeated prompts, by a random draw, either with a fresh answer streamed as it was written or with a stored earlier answer from the same model, shown at once. Within the same prompt, a replay lowered an answer’s chance of winning a decisive blind vote by 25.8 percentage points. The same stored text won 57% of its decisive votes when streamed and 29% when replayed, and voters called it less complete, more superficial and more often incorrect when replayed. Second, provider congestion slowed streamed answers. In 324,029 conversations, slower answers earned no detectable net credit in the vote, but a slower first exchange made visitors less likely to ask a follow-up question. Third, in 4,378,514 WildChat replies from everyday ChatGPT use, replies slowed by congestion made users less likely to continue. Delivery moved blind votes, in the direction opposite to the one the arena’s designers expected, while waiting cost follow-up questions that a comparison between two answers cannot register when both are slow. The results bear on how AI services cache and stream answers, how they budget latency, and how preference leaderboards evaluate models.

[24] FOL-Proof: Verifying LLM Text-to-Logic Translations via Deterministic Semantics cs.CL | cs.LOPDF

Yisrael Clark, Guy Katz

TL;DR: 论文提出 FOL-Proof，一个用于验证 LLM 将自然语言翻译为一阶逻辑（FOL）是否语义忠实的“语义防火墙”流水线。该方法先利用 English Resource Grammar 和 Minimal Recursion Semantics 等语义分析工具确定性地生成候选翻译池，再用 SMT 求解器检查 LLM 生成公式与各候选读法是否逻辑等价；忠实则接受，否则拒绝或可选修复，不确定时保守弃权。

Details

Motivation: LLM 越来越多地被用于把自然语言陈述翻译成 FOL 以增强推理，但 LLM 容易产生语义不忠实的公式，从而污染下游推理。论文旨在为 LLM 生成的 FOL 翻译提供独立、确定性的语义验证，避免错误翻译进入推理流程。

Result: 论文在 ProofWriter、RuleTaker、PrOntoQA 以及新提出的对抗基准 LongFaithCheck 上评估 FOL-Proof，实验显示其达到接近完美的选择性准确率，并显著优于直接使用 LLM 评判器和往返翻译基线。

Insight: 创新点在于将确定性语义分析生成的候选读法与 SMT 逻辑等价检查结合，形成可接受、拒绝、修复和弃权的语义防火墙。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是引入独立语义来源与保守弃权机制，在不完全或不确定验证时避免误拒，提高翻译验证的可靠性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to translate natural-language statements into first-order logic (FOL) to improve reasoning. However, LLMs are prone to errors, and so a semantically unfaithful FOL formula could corrupt downstream reasoning. We present FOL-Proof, a semantic firewall pipeline that validates LLM-generated FOL translations against independently derived linguistic interpretations. FOL-Proof operates by deterministically obtaining a pool of candidate translations via semantic analysis tools, such as the English Resource Grammar and Minimal Recursion Semantics; then applying an SMT solver to check each derived reading for logical equivalence to the LLM-generated formula. If the translation is faithful it is accepted; otherwise, it is rejected, or optionally repaired via repeated invocations to the LLM. The procedure is conservative: incomplete search, unsupported constructions, or inconclusive checks yield abstention rather than false rejection. We evaluated FOL-Proof on established benchmarks (ProofWriter, RuleTaker and PrOntoQA), and also on a new adversarial benchmark (LongFaithCheck). Our experiments show that FOL-Proof attains near perfect selective accuracy, while direct LLM judges and round-trip baselines show substantially worse performance.

TL;DR: 论文提出CoT-Proxy方法，用于从强教师模型蒸馏长程策略。标准微调需要模仿教师的思维链（CoT），但闭源教师的CoT常被隐藏。作者发现可以完全省略CoT，仅用教师动作训练学生（推理块留空），并加入KL损失防止漂移。令人意外的是，这种空推理块训练出的策略能迁移到推理时开启思考的模式，学生用自己的风格推理但像教师一样行动，称为跨格式迁移。进一步引入CoT-proxies（教师推理的简短摘要）可提升效果，且方法适用于闭源教师。

Details

Motivation: 强教师模型的思维链常因闭源API而不可见，标准蒸馏需要同时模仿CoT和动作，导致无法应用于闭源教师。论文旨在解决仅利用教师可见动作（无需隐藏CoT）来蒸馏长程策略的问题。

Result: 仅蒸馏Qwen3.6-27B的动作，使Qwen3-4B在AppWorld成功率从7%提升到18%，Qwen3-32B从12%提升到25%；加入CoT-proxies后Qwen3-4B达到35%。蒸馏GPT-5.6 Luna的可见输出和API返回的推理摘要，使Qwen3-4B在AppWorld从7%提升到48%，在SWE-bench Verified从3%提升到28%。

Insight: 创新点包括：发现仅用教师动作（空推理块）蒸馏即可获得长程策略，并意外实现跨格式迁移（训练时空推理块，推理时开启思考仍能受益）；提出CoT-proxies作为教师推理的简短摘要替代隐藏CoT，使方法适用于闭源教师。客观分析，其创新在于揭示了动作蒸馏与推理格式解耦的可行性，并提供了无需完整CoT的实用蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Reasoning models become long-horizon agents through reinforcement learning or, more cheaply, through fine-tuning on a stronger teacher’s trajectories, which contain the teacher’s chain-of-thought as well as its actions. The standard fine-tuning approach imitates both, yet the strongest teachers are closed models whose chain-of-thought is hidden behind an API. We show that the chain-of-thought can be left out altogether and that the long-horizon policy can be distilled from the teacher’s actions alone. The student is trained on these trajectories with its reasoning block left empty, while a KL loss on a replay buffer of its own generations limits drift from its initial model. Surprisingly, the policy learned from these trajectories with an empty reasoning block carries over to inference with thinking enabled, where the student reasons in its own style while acting like the teacher, an effect we call cross-format transfer. Distilling the actions of Qwen3.6-27B raises the task success of Qwen3-4B on AppWorld from 7% to 18% and that of Qwen3-32B from 12% to 25%. We further introduce CoT-proxies, short summaries of the teacher’s reasoning placed after the empty reasoning block, which raise Qwen3-4B further, to 35%. Since neither actions nor CoT-proxies need the hidden chain-of-thought, the recipe applies unchanged to closed teachers. Distilling GPT-5.6 Luna from its visible outputs and the reasoning summaries its API returns raises Qwen3-4B from 7% to 48% on AppWorld and from 3% to 28% on SWE-bench Verified.

cs.CV [Back]

[26] PEACE-TEST-PROTOCOL: Polarization-Keyed Examination Papers and Mandatory Issued Eyewear that Displace AI Glasses and Extend Universal Design cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.CRPDF

Dohyeon Kwon

TL;DR: 论文提出PEACE-TEST-PROTOCOL考试防作弊协议：由考试机构统一发放眼镜，试卷通过圆偏振手性编码文本，裸眼和未加滤波的相机关看为均匀灰色，只有佩戴发放的圆偏振分析眼镜才能阅读。该方案把智能眼镜形态反过来用于考场，并兼容处方镜片、低视力辅助MR以及盲文/音频/屏幕阅读器等无障碍形式。论文给出亮度与对比度预算、偏振光学建模和仿真实验，但明确所有结果均为仿真，尚无原型、物理测量或真人实验。

Details

Motivation: 相机AI眼镜可把试卷页面直接发给远程视觉语言模型，传统设备检测主要针对“持有设备”，难以从根本上阻止作弊。论文试图改变眼镜所有权：让考场中面部位置被权威发放并回收的眼镜占据，同时让试卷只有在配套偏振眼镜下才可读。

Insight: 创新点在于把防AI眼镜作弊从“检测设备/检测模型”转为“权威发放眼镜+偏振编码试卷”的系统级物理层方案，并兼顾处方镜片、低视力与盲人考生等通用设计。客观看，其价值在于提出可分析的光子预算与偏振泄漏边界，但密钥本身不保密、滤波隐蔽相机仍可能存在，且缺少真实原型验证是主要局限。

Abstract: Camera-equipped AI glasses turn an examination page into a query for a remote vision-language model. Device detection targets possession, and our companion work SEOYUN targets the model. PEACE-TEST-PROTOCOL instead changes who owns the eyewear. Every candidate wears authority-issued glasses for the whole session, and the question paper is printed so that it can be read only through them. As in cinema stereoscopy, a patterned laminate encodes the text in the handedness of circular polarization rather than in brightness. The page looks uniformly grey to the naked eye and to unfiltered cameras, and the issued circular analyzer reveals the text. Compulsory issued eyewear turns the smart-glasses form factor against itself. The face position is occupied by a device the authority supplies and collects, and a camera anywhere else needs its own analyzer. Spectacle wearers receive prescription inserts in the issued frame, low-vision candidates may use an offline assistive mixed-reality tier, and blind candidates keep braille, audio, and screen-reader formats. We derive the luminance and contrast budget and show why circular keying tolerates head tilt. A simulation study supports the analysis. A spectral Mueller model confirms tilt invariance and invisibility to linear-polarization cameras, but a 1 degree axis misregistration leaks a few percent of contrast. A capture-and-OCR experiment on English and Korean text shows that video averaging can recover leakage too weak to read in a single frame, so leakage must be bounded against the adversary’s total photon budget. Data from 151 observers and 28 cameras show that spectral (metameric) keys are visible to all of them. The key carries no secrecy, and filtered covert cameras remain possible. All results are simulations; no prototypes, physical measurements, or human-subject results are reported.

Abstract: Visual creation demands expressive imagery together with precise control over text, layout, and structure. We introduce Nemotron-Labs-VisualCreation, an agentic framework that connects these capabilities through Pixel as Code. Raster images are represented by learned discrete visual tokens, while structured artifacts use explicit programs. A shared generation-and-rendering interface connects these representations to visual feedback across images, vector graphics, webpages, and documents. A Nemotron-native harness coordinates planning and raster candidate selection, with targeted repair for editable structured and hybrid artifacts. To support this interface, we develop Nemotron-Labs-Image-Tokenizer, the autoregressive raster generator Nemotron-Labs-Image-30B-A3B, and the structured-code model family Nemotron-Labs-VisionCode. On approximately 178K images spanning six visual domains, our tokenizer improves reconstruction PSNR from 26.08 to 29.57 dB over Emu3.5 IBQ. It also provides 7.13x faster encoding and 10.93x faster decoding at batch size one on A100. In standalone evaluation, one Nemotron-Labs-VisionCode variant surpasses Kimi K3.1 Pro on the aggregate across Chart2Code, MMSVG, Design2Code, and Image2Struct. At the system level, the Nemotron-native configuration raises GenEval2 Soft-TIFA GM from 31.71 to 52.56 using the same raster generator. [28] Enhancing Sparse-View Gaussian Splatting with Feed-Forward Priors via Spectral Fusion and Distillation cs.CVPDF

Weikai Lin, Jun Dai, Sheng Zhao, Junjie Zhao, Yuhao Zhu

TL;DR: 论文提出一种即插即用方法，用预训练前馈新视角合成模型增强稀疏视角高斯泼溅。作者发现前馈先验在低频更可靠、高斯在高温更可靠，因此用一参数低通滤波器自适应融合两者，再将融合图像作为教师蒸馏回高斯模型，从而在推理时不引入前馈模型开销。

Details

Motivation: 稀疏训练视角下的高斯泼溅在远离训练视角区域重建质量下降；前馈新视角合成模型虽能借助强先验补全场景，但渲染速度慢十倍以上，且域外场景易产生高频伪影。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数据集和定量数值，仅声称该方法能提升多种稀疏视角高斯方法的重建质量，同时不牺牲渲染速度，并验证其分析适用于不同先验模型。

Insight: 创新点在于从频域分析前馈先验与高斯的互补可靠性，将最优融合权重建模为一参数低通滤波器，并通过蒸馏把融合教师信息回灌到高斯中，实现推理阶段去除慢速前馈模型、保持高斯渲染效率的即插即用增强。

Abstract: Gaussian splatting from sparse training views renders high-quality images near training views, but reconstruction quality degrades at distant regions, which are undersampled by the training set. Feed-forward novel view synthesis (NVS) models use strong learned priors to resample the scene from substantially different viewpoints, but they take more than ten times as long to render and produce high-frequency artifacts on out-of-domain scenes. We propose a plug-and-play method that enhances an existing sparse-view Gaussian model with a pretrained feed-forward NVS model. We observe that the two are complementary in the frequency domain: the prior is more accurate at low frequencies, and the Gaussians at high frequencies. The reliability of the prior relative to the Gaussians is inversely proportional to the image frequency, so the optimal fusion weight is a first-order low-pass filter with one parameter that can be fitted. Our key idea is then to intelligently fuse the reliable part of each of the two methods. To remove the slow feed-forward model at render time, we use these fused images as teachers and distill them back into Gaussians, balanced with supervision from input views. Our method improves the quality of various sparse-view Gaussian methods without sacrificing rendering speed, and our analysis works on different prior models. Project page: https://linwk20.github.io/spectral-fusion-gs/

Details

Motivation: 龙卷风时空演化对理解与防灾很重要，但从稀疏单侧视频重建实验室龙卷风高度欠约束：外观视角依赖、运动非刚性且未观测体积存在多解。论文旨在利用物理先验缓解这些不适定问题。

Result: 在留出相机视图上计算新视角合成精度，Tornado4D相比逐帧3DGS和基于视频的4DGS有显著提升。

Abstract: Characterizing how tornadoes evolve over space and time is critically important for understanding and preparing for these highly destructive weather events. Towards this end, we develop algorithms capable of forming time-resolved 3D reconstructions of laboratory smoke tornadoes from sparse one-sided videos. Even in a controlled environment, reconstruction is severely underconstrained: appearance is view dependent, motion is non-rigid, and the unobserved volume admits many geometries. We present Tornado4D, a physics-guided 4D Gaussian framework capable of reconstructing laboratory tornadoes from sparse, one-sided videos. Tornado4D uses knowledge of the enclosure geometry to introduce rotational airflow field priors that constrain how the 3D structure evolves over time. These rotational priors allow us to reconstruct otherwise unobserved structure. We evaluate reconstruction accuracy by computing novel-view synthesis accuracy on a held out camera view: Tornado4D outperforms frame-by-frame 3DGS and video-based 4DGS by a considerable margin.

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models effectively learn action policies from robot demonstrations, but struggle to generalize to changes in object positions and task goals. General multimodal foundation models (MFMs) offer complementary scene understanding and spatial reasoning, but remain slow and unreliable for direct fine-grained control. To understand how MFMs can improve VLA spatial generalization, we conduct a systematic capability analysis across multiple VLA architectures using atomic-stage interventions, revealing that many failures stem from poor target grounding and global spatial routing, while local manipulation skills remain largely usable. Based on these findings, we propose MFM-VLA, a selective collaboration framework in which an MFM provides target grounding and spatial guidance, controllers execute free-space motion, and the VLA retains control over fine-grained manipulation. We fine-tune the VLA with action-chunk-level dense geometric captions and perturbed-state recovery data to improve local guidance following and recovery from unfamiliar handoff states. A development-calibrated capability router selects between direct VLA execution and MFM-assisted execution to avoid unnecessary intervention. Across LIBERO-PRO, ALOHA-Sim-PRO, RoboCasa365, and real-world chemistry tasks, MFM-VLA improves success rates by 14.9, 74.0, 3.6, and 30.9 percentage points, respectively, over the corresponding base VLA. Abstract: Recent advances in video generation have made automated advertising production increasingly feasible. Crucially, product research and creative design are closely intertwined, as the information gathered about a product determines which features can be faithfully demonstrated and how they should be presented. In this paper, we introduce AdProducer, the first end-to-end agentic framework for advertising video creation, transforming concise creative briefs into finished advertisements. [32] Lit Up but Not Needed: Scoped Lesions Show Where Multimodal Mixture-of-Experts Depend on Their Experts cs.CVPDF

Zhixuan Ge, Anqi Li, Kexin Geng, Wei Qiu

TL;DR: 论文将认知神经科学中的“损伤实验”引入多模态 MoE 视觉语言模型，指出路由器激活图并不等于专家必要性图。作者在 Qwen3-VL、Kimi-VL 和 Gemma-4 中识别出视觉专属专家，并通过全局与按图像/文本位置限定的 scoped lesion 检验模型真正依赖哪些专家。结果表明视觉专属专家大多可移除而不显著掉点，真正影响图像侧性能的是双用途专家，且读图中文字是各模型都依赖图像位置专家的能力。

Details

Motivation: 现有工作常把 MoE 路由激活图直接解读为模态专属专家，但激活不等于必要性；论文要回答多模态 MoE 是否真的依赖这些看似模态专属的专家，并为专家选择、剪枝和跳过提供更可靠的依据。

Result: 在 Qwen3-VL 中，视觉专属专家占 3% 到 12%，接收约四分之一图像 token 路由，但全部损伤后在八个感知 benchmark 和自由格式 OCR 上平均损失不到 1 个百分点，且不超过同规模随机损伤。按位置损伤显示，Qwen3-VL 图像侧全专家损伤约 3 点，文本侧约 45 点；Kimi-VL 图像侧依赖约 2 到 3 点，Gemma-4 约 26 点。

Insight: 创新点在于用 scoped lesion 区分图像位置与文本位置的专家必要性，挑战“激活图即模态专家图”的直觉结论。论文进一步揭示视觉专属专家多由负载均衡制造、接收信息 patch 但输出普通且可被文本训练招募，提出专家选择、剪枝和跳过应跟随损伤实验而非路由激活图。

Abstract: Cognitive neuroscience learned that an activation map is not a map of necessity. Regions that light up during a task are not always needed for it, and only lesions reveal which ones are. Mixture-of-experts (MoE) vision-language models come with an activation map for free, since the router records which experts respond to image and text tokens, and recent work reads modality-specialized experts off this map. We bring the lesion. In Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B, Kimi-VL-A3B and Gemma-4-26B-A4B, 3 to 12% of the experts are vision-exclusive: image tokens route to them at up to five times the uniform rate and text tokens almost never. In Qwen3-VL they receive a quarter of all image-token routing, and lesioning all of them costs less than one percentage point on average over eight perception benchmarks and free-form OCR, no more than lesioning a random set of the same size. In Kimi-VL and Gemma-4 the lesion also costs no more than a random set. Scoped lesions, which remove an expert set only at image positions or only at text positions, then show where the models do depend on their experts. In Qwen3-VL, silencing every expert at every image position costs 3 points, against 45 at text positions, where the dependence sits in dual-use experts that text-only tasks need as well. Across models the image-side dependence is 2 to 3 points in Qwen3-VL and Kimi-VL and 26 in Gemma-4. Reading text in images is the one ability that needs image-position experts in every model. The exclusive experts receive informative patches, produce outputs of ordinary size, and stay inert under sensory deprivation: text-only training recruits them only when load balancing forces it. In Kimi-VL the balancing bias itself manufactures most of them. Load balancing draws the modality map in the part of the computation the models depend on least. Expert selection, pruning and skipping should follow the lesion, not the map.

[33] Spec$^2$R: Recovering Emergent Properties in Supervised Vision Transformers cs.CVPDF

Adam Wróbel, Jacek Tabor, Bartosz Zieliński, Marcin Przewięźlikowski

TL;DR: 论文研究监督式视觉Transformer（ViT）为何缺乏自监督ViT中常见的对象对齐图块表示和注意力图等涌现属性。作者通过谱分析发现，监督ViT的MLP层会向图块表示引入高频变化，破坏其空间组织；据此提出轻量级谱正则化方法Spec$^2$R，微调预训练监督ViT的MLP层，以恢复对象对齐的图块表示和注意力图，同时提升对抗鲁棒性并保持有竞争力的分类准确率。

Details

Motivation: 监督式ViT虽然性能强，但表示质量不同于自监督ViT，通常缺乏对象对齐图块表示和注意力图等涌现属性，即使使用强数据增强也难以获得。论文旨在理解这种差异的来源，并据此恢复监督ViT的对象级结构。

Result: Spec$^2$R能够恢复对象对齐的图块表示和注意力图，提升对抗鲁棒性，同时保持有竞争力的分类准确率；摘要未给出具体benchmark数值或SOTA对比。

Insight: 创新点在于通过谱分析定位监督ViT与自监督ViT的关键差异：监督ViT的MLP层引入高频变化并破坏图块表示的空间组织；进而提出轻量级谱正则化方法，仅微调MLP层即可恢复涌现属性并改善鲁棒性。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是从频域角度解释表示差异，并以低成本后训练方式控制ViT的对象级结构。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) have achieved strong performance across computer vision tasks, but the quality of their learned representations differs drastically depending on the training paradigm. While self-supervised ViTs often demonstrate emergent properties, such as object-aligned patch representations and attention maps, supervised ViTs typically lack these properties, even when trained with strong data augmentations. Understanding the origin of this difference is important for explaining and controlling the object-level structure. To investigate it, we perform a spectral analysis of supervised and self-supervised models, and identify a key distinction: MLP layers in supervised ViTs introduce high-frequency variations into patch representations, disrupting their spatial organization. Motivated by this finding, we propose Spec$^2$R, a lightweight spectral regularization method that fine-tunes the MLP layers of pretrained supervised ViTs. Spec$^2$R recovers object-aligned patch representations and attention maps, while improving adversarial robustness and preserving competitive classification accuracy.

[34] GoldiBench: Right Tools, Wrong Amount – Benchmarking Agents on Effect Judgement in Professional Editing cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.SEPDF

Wang Yang, Xinpeng Li, Yanyan Zhang, Chaoda Song, Shouren Wang

TL;DR: 论文提出 GoldiBench，一个面向专业图像/视频编辑软件智能体的基准，包含 500 个任务，覆盖 darktable、RawTherapee、GIMP、Inkscape 和 Blender。每个任务给定 START 图像和由隐藏程序渲染的 TARGET 图像，要求智能体通过写程序或操作 GUI 达到目标效果。基准将任务分别以“工具和数值”“仅工具”“仅目标”三种形式给出，以区分工具操作能力与效果量判断能力。

Details

Motivation: 真实编辑请求通常只描述想要的效果，而不给出具体工具和参数，因此智能体必须判断每种工具需要用多少量。现有基准要么直接给出工具和数值，要么用 VLM 评判且对半强度/三倍强度结果误判较多，无法衡量这种效果量判断能力。

Result: 在 25 个开放、GUI 和闭源模型上，智能体在被告知数值时工具操作表现很好，4B 以上模型用程序可达 99-100%；但仅给工具时效果量判断大幅下降，最佳模型用程序仅通过 19%，用 GUI 仅通过 33%，扩大模型规模也不能弥补。提供 TARGET-START 的测量差异可使通过率翻倍以上，但以图像形式展示该差异对开放模型帮助不大；允许四次渲染时智能体几乎总在第一次后停止，强制比较后强模型通过率可翻倍。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于用隐藏程序固定编辑量并重建渲染结果，从而可客观评估“效果量判断”，并通过三种任务设定分离工具操作与效果判断。其客观分析表明智能体并非不知道工具，而是不会读取、度量或主动检查所需编辑量，这为后续智能体设计提供了重要诊断方向。

Abstract: Real editing requests describe a look, not the steps to reach it, so an agent that serves them must judge how much of each tool the look takes. Existing benchmarks do not measure this: their tasks either state the tools and values, or leave the amount open and are scored by VLM judges that pass more than half of half- and triple-strength results. We introduce GoldiBench, a benchmark of 500 editing tasks in five professional applications (darktable, RawTherapee, GIMP, Inkscape, Blender), solvable by writing a program or by operating the GUI. Each task pairs a START image with a TARGET rendered from a hidden program, so the amount is fixed by a known target; the agent’s edit is re-rendered and compared with TARGET, and the same task is posed with the tools and values, the tools only, or the goal only, separating operation from effect judgement. Across 25 open, GUI and closed models, agents operate well (99-100% with programs from 4B up when told the values) but fall off a cliff when they must judge the amount: given only the tools, the best model passes 19% of the tasks with programs and 33% through the GUI, and scale does not close the gap. Manuals barely help; the measured TARGET-START difference more than doubles the pass rate, but the same difference shown as an image does not for open models. Allowed four renders, agents almost always stop after the first; forced to compare, the strong models double their pass rate. Agents know the tools, but neither read nor check the amount.

[35] Adapt, Label, Distill: Foundation-Model Supervision for Efficient Object Detection cs.CVPDF

Zhengye Yang, Anthony Wang, Yuyang Li, Mehmet Kerem Turkcan, Beizhan Yan

TL;DR: 论文提出 Adapt-Label-Distill 流程：先用少量目标域标注微调视觉基础模型 SAM3 作为教师，再为剩余未标注图像生成伪标签，并以置信度引导方式蒸馏训练 YOLOv11 紧凑检测器。方法在多个目标域验证有效性，并展示学生模型在边缘设备上的实时部署优势。

Details

Motivation: 专用域目标检测面临框标注稀缺、部署需要紧凑实时模型的问题，通用检测器迁移效果差；大视觉基础模型可提供离线监督，但其零样本预测与目标域对齐不足。

Result: 在六个目标域和三种学生容量上，部分域显著提升、部分域有限或负迁移；YOLOv11m 相比 STAC 风格自训练最高提升 15.3 AP，在 Garbage 达 51.6 AP（全监督 54.1 AP）；Jetson AGX Orin 上学生模型达 188 FPS，而 SAM3 仅 2.9 FPS。

Abstract: Object detectors often transfer poorly to specialized domains, where box annotations are scarce and deployment requires compact, real-time models. Large vision foundation models can provide offline supervision, but their zero-shot predictions may be poorly aligned with the target domain. We present an adapt-label-distill pipeline that uses limited target annotations to specialize a foundation teacher and transfer its supervision to compact detectors. We adapt SAM3 on the labeled subset, generate pseudo-labels for the remaining unlabeled images, and train YOLOv11 students with confidence-guided supervision. Teacher confidence weights pseudo-label losses, while zero-gating retains uncertain hypotheses during assignment without applying their associated training losses. Experiments across six target domains and three student capacities show substantial gains in several domains, while revealing limited or negative transfer in others. Teacher comparisons under a common hard-label training recipe support the choice of SAM3, and comparisons with an EfficientTeacher adaptation provide additional evidence against an online SSOD baseline. With YOLOv11m, our pipeline improves over STAC-style self-training by up to 15.3 AP and reaches 51.6 AP on Garbage, compared with 54.1 under full supervision. On NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, the deployed student achieves 188 FPS versus 2.9 FPS for SAM3, benefiting from foundation-model supervision without incurring the teacher’s deployment cost. Code is available at https://github.com/zk2172-columbia/ProjectAdaptLabelDistill_ObjectDetection.

[36] Anchoring VAE Fine-Tuning to Its Latent Diffusion Prior cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Rafael Bischof, Dominik Narnhofer, Julius Erbach, Michael Kraus, Konrad Schindler

TL;DR: 论文提出 Diffusion Prior Anchoring (DPA)，在微调 VAE 时利用预训练潜在扩散模型的先验进行锚定，通过 score-distillation 更新将 latent 引导到扩散模型能准确去噪的高密度流形上。该方法旨在解决冻结 VAE 编码器难以处理非 RGB 输入、丢失显著细节且受三通道限制的问题，并在多种图像/视频模型和任务上验证有效。

Details

Motivation: 现有迁移学习方法通常微调扩散主干但冻结 VAE 编码器以保持 latent 分布，然而冻结编码器可能遗漏非 RGB 输入的显著细节，且无法适配超过三通道的输入。论文希望允许 VAE 微调以适配新任务和输入，同时避免 latent 偏离预训练扩散先验。

Result: 在 FLUX 3、LTX-Video、Wan2.2 以及单目深度估计、MRI 重建、风场模拟、表面法线估计任务上，DPA 相对冻结 VAE 降低预测误差 7.5%，相对仅解码器微调降低 4.9%，相对无锚定 VAE 微调降低 4.0%。目标数据越远离 RGB 训练分布增益越大，从单目深度的 3.3% 到复值 MRI 的 7.2%；在四通道 VAE 编码深度和表面法线时，深度估计误差降低 9.3%。

Insight: 创新点是将 VAE 微调锚定到预训练扩散模型的 score/去噪流形，通过 score-distillation 正则约束 latent 不偏离扩散先验。客观来看，核心在于把“冻结编码器保分布”转变为“可微调编码器加先验锚定”，并支持多通道、剪枝等修改后的 VAE 适配非 RGB 任务。

Abstract: Pretrained latent diffusion models are effective priors for transfer learning, from geometry estimation to physics simulation. Existing approaches fine-tune the diffusion backbone but freeze the encoder of the VAE to preserve the latent distribution. However, a frozen encoder may miss salient details in non-RGB inputs and is limited to three channels. We introduce Diffusion Prior Anchoring (DPA), a regularizer applied during VAE fine-tuning that uses a score-distillation update to steer latents toward the high-density manifold on which the pretrained diffusion model denoises accurately. We validate DPA across three image and video models (FLUX 3, LTX-Video, Wan2.2) and four tasks: monocular depth estimation, MRI reconstruction, wind-flow simulation, and surface normal estimation, where DPA lowers the prediction error by 7.5% relative to the frozen VAE, 4.9% to decoder-only fine-tuning, and 4.0% to unanchored VAE fine-tuning. Its gain grows the further the target data lies from the RGB training distribution, from 3.3% on monocular depth estimation to 7.2% on complex-valued MRI. DPA also applies to modified VAEs, including pruned and multi-channel variants. With a four-channel VAE that encodes depth and surface normals, it lowers depth estimation error by 9.3%.

In this work, we propose GeoMVGen, a framework for joint multi-view RGB and pointmap video generation in robotic manipulation. Details

Abstract: Image restoration remains a fundamental challenge in low-level vision, requiring methods that handle diverse degradations while preserving scene structures and details. We revisit classical Retinex theory and propose a Generalized Retinex formulation that extends the illumination-reflectance decomposition into a Retinex-inspired content-environment factorization prior for image restoration. The key idea is to restore images using two learned factors: a content-dominant response (generalized reflectance) capturing scene properties and an environment-side response (generalized illumination) encoding task-dependent variations associated with the observed degradations. Based on this formulation, we develop GRUN (Generalized Retinex Unfolding Network), a MAP-guided deep unfolding framework that alternates reflectance restoration, illumination estimation, and progressive degradation inference across K stages with degradation-conditioned proximal operators. Motivation: 退役数据中心硬件常可翻新或转售，但视觉分拣不仅需要识别物体类别，还要区分外观相似部件、在损坏和遮挡下判断成色，并读取印刷零件编号。现有公开电子垃圾数据集主要覆盖消费设备，仅约 10% 图像标注为数据中心部件，且缺少成色和文本标签、重复图像比例高，难以支撑该任务。

Result: 在近 1 万张人工标注测试集上，使用 E-Waste-Bench 训练的 ConvNeXt-T 达到 64.3% top-1，而仅用公开数据集为 31.5%；在作为退役硬件流转代理的市场和拍卖照片上准确率降至 42.1%。实验还显示近重复泄漏在模型生成测试标签下会虚高 top-1 约 3.0 个百分点，但在人工标签下影响小于 0.1 个百分点。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于构建面向数据中心硬件视觉分拣的多任务大规模基准，联合标注类别、粒度、成色和印刷文本；采用配方爬虫与开放权重视觉语言模型智能体协同扩充数据并偏向欠代表类别；同时使用近重复分组划分和人工标注测试集，揭示模型生成标签基准中的重复泄漏问题及真实市场场景的域偏移。

Abstract: Retired data-center hardware can often be refurbished or resold, but triage demands more than object categories. A model must tell visually similar parts apart, judge condition under damage and occlusion, and read the printed part number. We audit nine public e-waste collections. They cover mostly consumer devices (only 10% of images are labeled as a data-center part), label neither condition nor printed text, and contain 42% exact or augmented duplicates. We introduce E-Waste-Bench, 328K photographs drawn from 762K deduplicated images and labeled for class (24 classes), granularity, condition and printed text. Images come from those collections and the web, where a recipe-based crawler works beside an agent built on an open-weight vision-language model. The agent vets new sources and reads sites the crawler cannot parse, and both are steered toward under-represented classes. Training labels are model-made, splits follow near-duplicate groups, and human annotators label a test set of nearly 10,000 images. We benchmark classification, condition, detection, text perception and cross-dataset transfer. On this test set, a ConvNeXt-T trained on E-Waste-Bench reaches 64.3% top-1, against 31.5% with the public collections alone. Near-duplicate leakage inflates top-1 by 3.0 points under model-made test labels but by less than 0.1 under human labels, so model-labeled benchmarks need group splits. Accuracy falls to 42.1% on marketplace and auction photographs, the closest proxy for hardware leaving service. Data and code will be released.

[41] NOBODY Was There: Bridging the Human-VLM Gap in Unwarranted Person Attribution Bias cs.CVPDF

Parsa Madinei, Ziqi Wen, Miguel Eckstein

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）在描述无人物图像时，倾向于将可见动作或事件归因于图像中并不存在的人，并将此称为无依据人物归因偏差。作者发现25个SOTA VLM在无人物场景的caption中提及人物的频率约为人工caption的4倍，且人类和VLM都更偏好避免此类归因的描述。为此提出偏好数据集NOBODY，通过DPO对齐开放VLM，并发布NOBODY-Bench用于评估该偏差及与人类caption/偏好的一致性。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注人类与VLM在图像描述推断上的系统性差距：VLM更容易对画面中不存在的人进行无依据归因，而现有人类偏好数据未能有效纠正这一行为。

Result: 在25个SOTA VLM上，无人物场景caption提及人物的频率是人工caption的4倍；经NOBODY上DPO对齐后，开放VLM在人物归因上与人类caption的一致率达到或超过人与人之间的一致率，而9个现有公开偏好数据集几乎不改变该行为，人类评审也显著更偏好对齐后的caption。

Insight: 创新点在于明确定义并量化了VLM的无依据人物归因偏差，构建针对性偏好数据集NOBODY和评测基准NOBODY-Bench，并证明定向偏好对齐可有效缩小人机差距；可借鉴之处是针对特定系统性偏差设计偏好数据和基准，而非依赖通用偏好数据。

Abstract: Describing an image requires not only recognizing what is visible but deciding what should be inferred. We investigate a systematic gap between humans and VLMs in their tendency to attribute visible actions or events to people who are not present in an image. We term this behavior unwarranted person attribution bias and find that, across 25 SOTA VLMs, captions of person-absent scenes mention a person four times as often as human-written captions of the same images. Through a preference study, we further show that both humans and VLMs prefer captions that avoid such attributions. Motivated by this, we introduce NOBODY, a preference dataset designed to bridge the gap between humans and VLMs in unwarranted person attribution. Through direct preference optimization (DPO), we align open VLMs on NOBODY and show that their agreement with human captions on person attribution reaches or exceeds inter-human agreement, whereas nine existing public preference datasets leave this behavior largely unchanged. Furthermore, we show that human judges preferred the aligned VLM captions significantly more than those of the baseline model. Alongside NOBODY, we release NOBODY-Bench, which evaluates VLMs for unwarranted person attribution bias and their alignment against human captions and preference. Sadiq O. Macaulay, Dipon Ghosh, Hadfield Simon

TL;DR: 论文提出 EventSSM，一种面向异步事件相机的因果空间状态建模框架，用于事件关联的度量深度估计。该方法用按时间顺序到达的事件包更新循环记忆，保持持续的空间上下文与物理时间状态，并借助标定相机位姿和可微渲染将异步深度预测几何变换到参考时间戳以接受帧级监督。

Details

Motivation: 事件相机输出异步亮度变化，但度量深度监督通常只在稀疏参考时间戳可得；将事件聚合成帧式表示虽简化学习，却会破坏原始事件流的细粒度时间结构，因此需要直接利用异步事件流并桥接异步预测与帧时刻监督。

Result: 在基准评测中，EventSSM 在多种深度误差指标上优于已有事件深度估计方法；消融进一步分析了跨模态初始化、时间循环、状态、读出、监督组件及事件包细分的影响。

Insight: 创新点在于以按时间排序的事件包驱动循环空间状态，实现因果、持续空间上下文与物理时间状态保留；并通过相机位姿几何变换与可微渲染在参考时间戳施加监督，从而连接异步事件预测和帧级深度标签。

Abstract: Event cameras report asynchronous brightness changes, yet metric-depth supervision is typically available only at sparsely sampled reference timestamps. Aggregating events into frame-like representations simplifies learning but can obscure the fine-grained temporal structure of the original event stream. We introduce EventSSM, a causal spatial-state framework for event-associated metric-depth estimation using a recurrent memory updated by chronological event packets. To bridge asynchronous predictions and frame-time supervision, predicted event depths are geometrically transported using calibrated camera poses and differentiably rendered at reference timestamps. Benchmark evaluations demonstrate improvements over established event-depth methods across multiple depth-error measures. Controlled ablations further examine cross-modal initialization, temporal recurrence, individual state, readout, and supervision components, and packet subdivision. Together, these findings establish EventSSM as an effective framework for causal event-associated metric-depth estimation, combining persistent spatial context with physically timed state retention.

[43] BEARING: Who Is Talking to Whom? Grounding Acoustic Direction in Vision for Online Multi-Party Conversation cs.CV | cs.CL | cs.SDPDF

Nader Lobandi, Lombe Chileshe, Mohammad Mehdi Hosseini, Mohammad H. Mahoor

TL;DR: 论文提出BEARING，面向在线多人对话场景，从视频与音频中判断谁在说话以及在对谁说。方法以麦克风阵列测得的声源方向为核心几何信号，通过无标签在线校准将声学方向注册到相机图像，并维护人脸、声音和方向的无注册在线登记。注册方向被用作视觉语言模型的视觉提示、与视觉活跃说话人检测进行可靠性加权融合的输入，以及用于模型对齐的可验证奖励。

Details

Motivation: 现有系统多在离线单通道片段上处理说话人与受话人判断，并依赖自评估奖励进行对齐，难以满足在线多人对话中的几何一致性和可验证性需求。论文试图利用相机旁麦克风阵列测得的声源方向作为跨模态锚点，解决在线场景下谁在说话、对谁说以及声源与视觉位置对应的问题。

Result: 在三个AMI站点和egocentric EasyCom的9,498条带受话人标注话语上，方向提示将说话人准确率从54%提升到74%，受话人准确率约提升2个点；在AVDIAR共址说话者场景中，视觉活跃说话人检测保持95%，而仅用方向降至59%。可验证奖励在GRPO、reward-ranked fine-tuning和DPO下优于自评估；前瞻窗口从5秒缩至0.5秒在AMI上损失4.5个DER点，在线学习注册在前两分钟有性能下降后超过仅视觉方法。

Insight: 论文的核心创新是以声源方向这一可测量几何信号作为视觉与声学之间的锚点，并将其同时用于视觉提示、多模态融合和可验证奖励设计。客观来看，其创新点在于将方向注册、在线人脸声音登记和可验证对齐结合为统一的在线框架，并通过显式前瞻参数和奖励约束提升多人对话中说话人与受话人判断的可解释性与可靠性。

Abstract: Understanding a group conversation means deciding, from video and audio, who is speaking and to whom. Leading systems treat these tasks offline on single-channel clips and align models with a self-evaluation reward. We propose BEARING, a geometry-first alternative built on one measured signal: the direction from which a microphone array beside the camera hears a sound. A label-free calibration learned from the stream registers this direction into the camera image, and an online registry of faces, voices and directions needs no enrollment. The registered direction serves as (i) a visual prompt for a vision-language model, a marker on the speaker or an arrow toward an off-screen source; (ii) one input of a reliability-weighted fusion with visual active-speaker detection, for speakers in the same direction from the array; and (iii) a verifiable reward for alignment: the named speaker must sit where the sound comes from, the confidence must match that check, and always answering “group” earns nothing. Every decision is online, with the lookahead as an explicit parameter. On 9,498 addressee-labelled utterances from three AMI sites and egocentric EasyCom, the direction prompt raises speaker accuracy from 54% to 74%; addressee accuracy moves by about two points, because telling one addressee from the group depends on the words more than on where people are. For co-located speakers on AVDIAR, visual active-speaker detection holds 95% where direction alone falls to 59%. The verifiable reward beats self-evaluation under GRPO, reward-ranked fine-tuning and DPO; shrinking the lookahead from 5 s to 0.5 s costs 4.5 DER points on AMI, and learning the registration online costs a dip in the first two minutes before the system overtakes vision alone. Code and evaluation protocols will be released.

TL;DR: 论文提出 LeanWAM，一种潜在世界动作模型，利用冻结视频生成器并通过两阶段训练将未来表征压缩为少量 token，再蒸馏到轻量策略中，使其在生成动作的同时预测紧凑未来表征。该方法仅 1.4B 参数且无大规模具身预训练，在多个机器人操作基准上优于强 VLA 和 WAM 基线，并显著提升推理速度。

Details

Motivation: 现有世界动作模型通常适配大型预训练视频生成器来联合预测未来观测和机器人动作，训练与部署成本高；同时尚不清楚策略真正需要哪些未来表征、这些表征能压缩到何种程度。论文的探测实验表明，互补的未来表征可压缩为少量 token 并保留大部分动作解码性能。

Result: LeanWAM 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus 和 RoboTwin 2.0 上超过强 VLA 与 WAM 基线；在 A100 GPU 上预测一个动作块仅需 25.1 ms，比代表性 WAM 快 12.3 到 41.5 倍。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) typically adapt large pretrained video generators to jointly predict future observations and robot actions, incurring substantial training and deployment costs. However, it remains unclear which future representations a policy actually needs and how compactly they can be represented. Our probing study shows that complementary future representations can be compressed into a few tokens while retaining most of their action-decoding performance. Motivated by this finding, we introduce LeanWAM, a latent WAM that transfers video priors from a frozen video generator to a lightweight policy via two-stage training. In the first stage, a Future Extractor compresses observed transitions into 32 predictive tokens. These tokens condition the frozen generator and are jointly shaped by action, RGB, semantic, and geometric supervision, while a compact readout of the generator’s hidden states supplies complementary world tokens. The second stage distills both representations into the policy, which learns to predict them from current observations while generating actions. With 1.4B parameters and no large-scale embodied pretraining, LeanWAM outperforms strong vision-language-action (VLA) and WAM baselines on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin~2.0. It predicts an action chunk in 25.1,ms on an A100 GPU, 12.3–41.5$\times$ faster than representative WAMs. These results demonstrate that compact predictive supervision can transfer video priors to robust and efficient control.

Abstract: Harnesses for vision-language models (VLMs) have grown with the models’ capabilities, notably for images far larger than those seen in training, where downscaling loses small details. Most of them wrap the VLM in a fixed procedure, such as a zoom tree, that decides in advance where it looks and how the evidence is combined. These procedures make up for the weaknesses of older models by keeping them on a task-specific path, but with frontier models that reliably plan and use tools, they can lower accuracy. We propose RVLM-X, a task-generalizable recursive VLM harness that runs unchanged across photographs, charts and maps. Following the recursive inference of Recursive Language Models, the VLM programmatically controls its own visual context in a code REPL that holds the full-resolution image and calls itself recursively on regions that it chooses. With a cost-efficient frontier model as the base of every method, RVLM-X is more accurate than a single call on all 7 public benchmarks and has the highest pooled accuracy. Because existing benchmarks mostly test whether a model finds one small detail, we also introduce MapGenBench, procedurally generated maps whose questions combine fine-detail perception with multi-step reasoning. There, RVLM-X answers 71.4% of held-out questions against 55.5% for the strongest baseline. Overall, our findings suggest that as VLMs plan and use tools more reliably, letting the model write its own visual search works better than fixing one in advance.

[47] Dreamcatcher: Remembering What Surprises a World Model in Video Streams cs.CVPDF

TL;DR: 论文提出 Dreamcatcher，一个面向流式视频问答的免训练在线视觉记忆框架。它利用独立预训练且冻结的视频世界模型的预测误差生成时空“惊讶”信号，指导在线分段与冻结 Video-LLM 原生视觉 token 的保留，并在查询时用证据门控筛选相关片段。方法在多个流式与离线长视频基准上验证，能在显著降低输入 token 的同时取得有竞争力的准确率。

Details

Abstract: As live video streams grow longer, repeatedly encoding and processing their full histories becomes increasingly costly. Answering questions as they arrive therefore calls for visual memory built in one pass before future questions are known. Existing methods mainly keep tokens that differ from earlier ones or match surrogate queries fixed in advance. Without modeling world dynamics, these criteria struggle to tell subtle surprising details from expected change. We introduce Dreamcatcher, a training-free framework and, to our knowledge, the first to use predictive signals from an independently pretrained, frozen video world model for online memory formation in streaming video question answering. Conditioned on recent history, the world model predicts incoming features using learned world priors. At query time, an evidence gate admits a segment from a similarity-based shortlist only when the Video-LLM judges that it adds evidence for the question beyond the current view. We evaluate Dreamcatcher on three streaming and three offline long-video benchmarks. On OVO-Bench, Dreamcatcher achieves the highest overall accuracy among the compared training-free methods for each of four frozen Video-LLMs, reaching 78.9% with Qwen3.8-27B. With the three Qwen Video-LLMs, it also reduces average input tokens for answer generation by more than 80% relative to their base models at 1 fps.

Motivation: 纯手部文本驱动运动生成受限于数据：现有数据集常把手视为刚性末端执行器，缺少细粒度描述，或规模不足，难以支持稳健泛化。
