Insight: 创新点包括大规模野外文本-手部运动配对数据、灵巧度过滤聚焦复杂双手运动、从全局语义标签到原子手指状态的五级分层描述，以及适配高自由度手部运动学的393维双手表示。

Abstract: While text-driven body motion generation has matured, pure-hand synthesis remains bottlenecked by data. Existing datasets often treat hands as rigid end-effectors, lack fine-grained captions, or are too limited in scale to support robust generalization. We introduce RoMo Hands, an in-the-wild, text-paired dataset of ~542K sequences (884 hours, 95.5M frames at 30 fps) which is an order of magnitude larger than prior work. To ensure high-quality supervision, a stringent dexterity filter discards low-articulation sequences, focusing the distribution on complex bimanual movement. Each clip is paired with five captions at increasing granularities from global semantic tags to atomic finger states, organized under a hierarchical taxonomy. To bridge the gap between body and hand architectures, we propose a novel 393-D bimanual representation that adapts redundant body-motion paradigms to the high-DoF kinematics of hands. Systematic benchmarking of diffusion, masked-token, and autoregressive generators reveals distinct trade-offs under these hand-specific adaptations, including a sharp divergence between semantic alignment and kinematic plausibility. Our dataset, representation, and benchmark establish a rigorous foundation for the next generation of dexterous motion synthesis. Project page: https://romodataset.github.io/romo-hands/.

[49] VISOR: A Vision-Language Selector for Multimodal Time-Series Forecasting cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Seunghan Lee, Jun Seo, Junhyeok Kang, Jaehoon Lee, Sangjun Han

TL;DR: 论文提出 VISOR，一个面向多模态时间序列预测的视觉-语言选择器框架。它先用无文本随机预测器采样多个候选未来，再由视觉语言模型将候选未来可视化为单张图表图像，并结合文本信息选择最合适的一个未来，而不是对候选做均值化。

Details

Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型常将预测分布压缩为中位数或均值，导致多样未来被拉平成平缓曲线；现有多模态时序模型主要通过时序-语言对齐引入文本，而对齐能力弱于视觉-语言对齐。

Result: 在覆盖十个数据集的三个基准上，VISOR 在点误差和波动率误差上与时间序列基础模型及多模态时序预测模型相比取得最佳平均排名。

Insight: 创新点在于将多模态时序预测转化为候选未来选择问题，利用 VLM 的视觉-语言对齐能力基于文本选择候选，并采用兼顾逐点误差与路径形状的 shape-aware 目标训练选择器，以避免预测路径过于平缓。

Abstract: Recent time-series foundation models (TSFMs) forecast a distribution over futures, and multimodal time-series (TS) forecasting further exploits other modalities to improve forecasting accuracy. However, existing works have two limitations: 1) TSFMs reduce the predictive distribution to its median or mean, flattening diverse futures into a calm line, and 2) multimodal TS forecasting models incorporate text into the forecaster through TS-language alignment, which is substantially weaker than vision-language alignment. To address these limitations, we propose the VIsion-language SelectOR (VISOR), a multimodal TS forecasting framework that selects one plausible future instead of averaging across candidates, leveraging the alignment capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) to select the most suitable candidate based on textual information. VISOR consists of two components: 1) a text-free stochastic predictor that samples diverse candidate futures, and 2) a VLM selector that reads the candidates as a single chart image and selects one based on the text. To avoid overly calm forecasting paths, we train the selector with a shape-aware target that evaluates each candidate based on both point-wise error and path shape. Across three benchmarks covering ten datasets, VISOR achieves the best average ranking in both point and volatility errors compared with TSFMs and multimodal TS forecasting models. Code: https://github.com/seunghan96/VISOR.

TL;DR: 论文提出 SceneMemBench，用于评估交互式视频世界模型中的场景记忆能力：即用户离开后返回时能否恢复已观察场景。基准从“已见视角回忆 vs 新视角恢复”和“受控诊断 vs 端到端交互”两个维度评测，并引入基于共享真实观察历史的 Continued Rollout 与完整模型交互的 Full Rollout。基准包含 33 个 Unreal Engine 场景中的 600 条受控轨迹和 60 个 Full Rollout 案例，覆盖 8 个代表性开源与闭源模型。

Motivation: 现有记忆评估多关注已见视角重访和完整 rollout，忽视跨视角场景恢复，且容易将记忆能力与轨迹跟随误差、累积生成退化混在一起。论文希望更纯粹、系统地诊断交互式视频世界模型的场景记忆。

Result: 实验表明新视角恢复通常比已见视角回忆更具挑战性；模型在受控历史下的表现画像可能与完整交互中明显不同，二者揭示了单独使用已见视角重访或端到端 rollout 会掩盖的互补失败模式。

Insight: 创新点在于将场景记忆拆分为已见视角回忆与新视角恢复，并区分受控诊断和端到端交互；Continued Rollout 从共享真实观察历史出发，有助于解耦记忆评估与轨迹跟随误差及累积生成退化，并按主体一致性、外观保真度和几何一致性评测场景恢复。

Abstract: Interactive video world models generate navigable worlds frame by frame, but a persistent world requires previously observed scenes to remain recoverable as users move away and return. Existing memory evaluations, however, often focus on seen-view revisits and full rollouts, leaving cross-view scene recovery underexplored while entangling memory with trajectory-following errors and accumulated generation degradation. We introduce SceneMemBench, a benchmark for scene memory in interactive video world models. It evaluates two complementary dimensions: seen-view recall versus novel-view recovery, and controlled diagnosis versus end-to-end interaction. We introduce Continued Rollout from a shared ground-truth observation history, alongside Full Rollout over complete model-generated interactions. SceneMemBench contains 600 controlled trajectories across 33 Unreal Engine scenes and 60 Full Rollout cases, covering eight representative open- and closed-source models. We evaluate scene recovery along subject consistency, appearance fidelity, and geometric consistency. Results show that novel-view recovery is generally more challenging than seen-view recall, while model profiles under controlled histories can differ markedly from those observed in full interaction. Together, these findings reveal complementary failure modes obscured by seen-view revisits or end-to-end rollouts alone.

Motivation: 现有世界动作模型在每个动作前生成未来帧，导致闭环控制推理过慢；同时多数策略仅依赖短观测历史，难以利用长期上下文。论文旨在解决实时动作解码与长历史、大规模预训练结合的问题。

Result: Fast模式与同checkpoint的IDM推理性能相当，每16步动作块在单RTX 5090上仅需60 ms，比IDM快7.6倍，并支持无暂停实时分块。G0.7在五个仿真基准套件上平均70.8分，Galaxea R1 Pro/R1 Lite零样本抓放成功率48%/54%，视频条件in-context学习ID/OOD成功率96.7%/93.3%，七个真实后训练任务平均进度86.9%。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) adapt pretrained video generators for robot control by jointly predicting future camera frames and actions, transferring physical priors learned from video into manipulation policies. However, generating future frames before every action makes test-time inference too slow for closed-loop control, while most existing policies remain restricted to short observation histories. Building on Fast-WAM, which showed that video prediction shapes policy representations during training so that future frame synthesis can be skipped at deployment, we present G0.7, a generalist world action model scaled along two axes: extending the context window from a single observation to up to five minutes of history, and scaling pretraining to a large-scale corpus of robot teleoperation and human egocentric (ego) video. Across public simulation suites and real-robot deployments, direct action decoding (Fast mode) matches imagine-then-act (IDM) inference from the same checkpoint while running in 60 ms per 16-step action chunk on a single RTX 5090 (7.6x faster than IDM) and supporting pause-free real-time chunking. Details

Motivation: 现有视线目标估计方法大多逐帧处理，不建模视线如何在动态场景变化下发生转移；论文希望利用世界模型对场景动态的潜在编码能力来检测并预测视线转移。

Result: GWM 在 VideoAttentionTarget 和 ChildPlay 上取得 SOTA 的视线转移检测结果，四类 AP 比最强基线高约 10 点；视线目标估计与最强图像模型相当，并在未来视线定位和转移预测上优于对比基线。

Abstract: Where a person looks indicates their focus of attention, and how their gaze shifts between targets reveals how that attention responds to a changing scene. Existing gaze target estimation methods largely operate frame by frame and do not model these shifts. Gaze shifts are often driven by dynamic changes in the scene, which is the kind of information world models are designed to encode. This relationship is subtle, however, since an event far across the frame may produce only a slight movement of the person’s head or eyes. We therefore introduce the Gaze World Model (GWM), which co-trains latent scene prediction with person-conditioned gaze supervision on a frozen V-JEPA 2.1 encoder; in our ablations, the latent prediction objective improves both shift detection and gaze localization. Given a video clip and the person’s head box, GWM estimates gaze locations and detects gaze shifts in the observed clip, and forecasts future gaze and shifts from predicted scene features. To train and evaluate these tasks, we augment VideoAttentionTarget and ChildPlay with event-level gaze-shift annotations. GWM achieves state-of-the-art shift detection on these benchmarks, about 10 points higher in four-class AP than the strongest baseline, while remaining comparable to the strongest image-based models in gaze target estimation, and outperforms the compared baselines on future gaze localization and shift forecasting.

[53] Rethinking Video Agents: When and Why Video Harnesses Work cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.MMPDF

Luoyuan Zhang, Xuanyu Zheng, Yancheng Long, Haoyang Xu, Bin Wen

TL;DR: 论文系统研究了视频智能体中的观察框架（video harness）何时以及为何能提升视频理解：固定观察策略会预先限制证据，而自适应观察允许模型随时间与空间主动寻求更多证据。作者基于 Video-Relook 框架分析不同模型和任务下的性能与交互轨迹，发现自适应观察在细粒度动作理解上有显著收益，并可扩展到 Video-MME-v2，但收益高度依赖模型能力和任务需求。

Details

Motivation: 传统视频理解依赖固定观察策略，证据在推理前已被确定，可能限制模型挖掘潜力；尽管智能体式观察框架允许模型主动请求观察，但“能请求”不等于“能有效利用”，因此需要研究其生效条件。

Result: 在 Video-Relook 上，自适应观察对细粒度动作理解带来显著提升，并在 Video-MME-v2 上也有收益；但同一观察接口下不同模型收益差异很大，同一模型在不同任务上也会出现大幅提升、有限增益甚至性能回退。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新在于系统性揭示视频观察框架的有效性取决于“信息性观察机会”与“模型将交互转化为可靠答案修订的能力”之间的对齐，并通过交互轨迹分析证据寻求与修订可靠性。客观来看，其可借鉴点是将观察自主性与任务需求、模型能力匹配，并强调感知与推理协同设计，而非简单赋予模型主动观察接口。

Abstract: Video understanding depends not only on how models reason about visual evidence, but also on which evidence they observe. Fixed observation policies determine this evidence in advance; agentic harnesses allow models to seek further observations as their understanding develops. This shift creates an opportunity to realize capabilities that fixed inputs leave untapped, yet the ability to request observations does not ensure their effective use. We investigate when and why video harnesses improve understanding, examining how task demands and models’ capacity for interaction jointly shape their benefits. Using Video-Relook as an experimental framework, we let models adapt their temporal and spatial observations from a common default configuration, and analyze performance and interaction trajectories across models and tasks. Adaptive observation yields substantial gains on fine-grained action understanding, with benefits also extending to Video-MME-v2. Even under the same observation interface, gains differ substantially across models; interaction traces reveal corresponding differences in evidence seeking and the reliability of answer revision. The benefits also vary across tasks for the same model, ranging from substantial improvements to limited gains and regressions. Together, these findings support an account in which harness effectiveness depends on the alignment between opportunities for informative observation and the model’s ability to turn interaction into reliable revisions. They motivate video harnesses that coordinate perception and reasoning, matching observational autonomy to task demands and model capabilities.

Abstract: Training capable omni video agents requires more than diverse question-answer pairs: agents must learn to reliably find, connect, and correct task-relevant evidence in omni-setting. However, we argue that existing data pipelines provide limited control over task-relevant evidence and weak supervision for these capabilities. In this work, we present Open OmniCoach, a new framework for evidence-seeking omni video agents. It contains a high-quality dataset, OmniCoach-240K, and a new agentic reinforcement learning (RL) method, Step-Level Evidence-Guided Policy Optimization (SEPO). For the former, OmniCoach-240K comprises 248.2K interaction trajectories, including 230.7K multi-span trajectories and 17.4K reflection trajectories. We design a three-stage data pipeline by leveraging both fine-grained omni-evidence and the agent’s own errors. Meanwhile, compared with other RL baselines, SEPO provides turn-level credit based on improvements in evidence coverage and temporal localization. Applying both trajectory data and the SEPO method, we obtain Open OmniAgent models. The resulting models outperform the baselines across multiple video and audio-visual understanding tasks by significant margins, demonstrating the effectiveness of the Open OmniCoach framework. TL;DR: 论文提出 Loupe，一个面向知识密集型视频生成的智能体，通过检索并验证文本与视觉证据，构建有事实依据、包含参考图和时间关系的视频提示。作者进一步提出基于评分标准的步骤信用机制 RBC，并派生 RBC-SFT 与 RBC-GRPO，在视频渲染前评估搜索与规划中间决策，从而降低视频级反馈成本并缓解轨迹级信用分配粗糙的问题。论文还构建了 Search-VBench 基准，实验显示其在多个视频生成器上提升生成质量与事实准确性。

Abstract: Knowledge-intensive video generation remains challenging because prompts often omit the facts, appearances, and temporal relationships required for accurate generation. While search-based workflows can retrieve missing evidence, manually designed strategies limit adaptive planning, and conventional training suffers from costly video-level feedback and coarse trajectory-level credit assignment. In this paper, we present Loupe, a video generation agent that retrieves and verifies textual and visual evidence to construct evidence-grounded temporal video prompts with reference images. We further introduce Rubric-Based Credit (RBC), which leverages prompt-specific factual, visual, and temporal requirements to evaluate intermediate search and planning decisions before video rendering. Building on RBC, RBC-SFT reweights assistant-token learning by step quality, while RBC-GRPO incorporates step-level credits alongside a final planning reward, without rendering videos during RL. To address the lack of dedicated search-oriented evaluation, we construct Search-VBench, which contains 150 held-out prompts across seven categories. Experiments show that Loupe improves all four evaluation dimensions across three video generators, raising the overall score of MiniMax-H3 from 32.80 to 70.67 and outperforming an untrained search workflow. Ablation experiments show that full RBC training outperforms standard SFT followed by trajectory-level GRPO by 5.27 points. Human evaluations and results on Sci-VBench and KIVI-Bench further demonstrate improved generation quality, scientific grounding, factual precision, and helpfulness. Our project page is available at https://zengkaiya.github.io/Loupe/.

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的持续学习问题，即在学习新操作技能的同时保留对旧指令的响应能力。针对有限经验回放下旧指令可能错误触发新学竞争目标的结构化失败模式，提出跨指令锚定（XIA），在每个任务边界冻结上一策略作为教师，用当前观测配旧指令进行响应保持，从而在不增加专家回放数据的情况下扩展监督。

Motivation: 现有经验回放（ER）在每任务仅保留少量历史专家帧时，许多旧指令响应缺乏直接监督，导致灾难性遗忘以及旧指令触发新学竞争目标的问题。论文旨在有限回放预算下更好地保持指令条件化的旧行为。

Result: 在多种VLA架构和三个LIBERO套件上，XIA在相同历史专家数据预算下相比ER取得更高成功率和更低遗忘；RoboCasa和真实世界实验进一步支持其有效性。摘要未明确宣称达到SOTA。

Insight: 创新点在于用上一策略作为教师，将旧指令与当前观测配对进行响应匹配，把监督从存储的历史专家样本扩展到更广的旧指令响应空间。客观上，其价值在于将指令条件行为保持与专家帧回放解耦，缓解有限回放下旧指令与新目标之间的干扰。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models need to continually acquire new manipulation skills while retaining the ability to follow previously learned instructions. Experience replay (ER) can mitigate catastrophic forgetting, but retaining only a small number of historical expert frames per task leaves many old-instruction responses without direct supervision. Under limited replay, we observe a structured failure mode in which an earlier instruction can trigger a subsequently learned, competing goal. To address this gap, we propose Cross-Instruction Anchoring (XIA), which supplements expert replay with instruction-conditioned response preservation. At each task boundary, XIA freezes the previous policy as a teacher and matches its responses to old instructions paired with current observations. This extends supervision beyond stored historical examples without requiring additional expert replay data. Experiments across multiple VLA architectures and three LIBERO suites demonstrate improved success rates and reduced forgetting compared with ER under identical historical expert-data budgets. Additional RoboCasa and real-world experiments further support the effectiveness of XIA.

Abstract: GUI grounding requires resolving fine visual details within the context that gives them meaning. Abstract: For endoscopic robotic surgery, accurate dense depth perception serves as a foundational capability, which enables navigation, instrument localization, and tissue interaction. However, endoscopic video lacks reliable metric depth, while metric measurements obtained via tracking, time-of-flight sensing, or instrument contact only cover a small fraction of pixels in each frame, rendering sparse depth completion an urgent and unsolved challenge. In this work, we propose Temporal Anchor Densification with Reliability-Aware Memory (TANDEM), a causal sparse depth completion framework for endoscopic video. It adopts a lightweight design and leverages the video stream: past measurements re-projected into the current view recover the metric evidence unavailable within a single sparse frame. To keep this evidence interpretable, accurate, and efficient, an explicit memory scores each re-projected measurement with analytic re-observation, age, and motion-parallax factors without overriding current measurements. Abstract: Shape decomposition represents 3D objects as a compact set of geometric primitives for interpretable modeling and simulation. Within this framework, PhysDec-Loop is an agentic pipeline that reasons about part structure and materials, using a reference 3D point cloud for geometric feedback and physical verifiers to guide iterative refinement. Through self-evolution, we further develop another agentic pipeline, PhysDec-Evolved, which learns from prior corrections to predict primitive decompositions from images without point-cloud feedback or a correction loop at inference. Abstract: Interactive video world models enable action- and camera-controlled exploration, yet struggle to maintain consistent spatial geometry and appearance when revisiting previously explored locations. Historical KV caches help auto-regressive video diffusion models maintain long-term consistency, but their retrieval and attention computation introduce substantial latency as history grows. We propose DraftKV, a training-free framework that retrieves historical KV caches based on geometric grounding and query-conditioned relevance, while combining CPU offloading with tile-wise streaming attention to reduce GPU memory usage and inference latency. Theoretically, we show that draft relevance reproduces the mean branch of full-resolution block relevance exactly, so its deviation is confined to the $β$-weighted max branch, and we bound retrieval utility loss in terms of the measured Top-K selection overlap. We further show that draft relevance cannot distinguish blocks with similar pooled descriptors regardless of camera pose, motivating geometric grounding before refinement. Details

Motivation: 现有视频生成视觉质量高但物理动态不合理；潜在世界模型的预测一致性可能反映场景易预测性而非物理合理性，需要内容匹配的参考来准确评估生成动态的物理合理性。

Result: 在多个基准上评估，SPAR提升了文本到视频生成和图像到视频延续的物理合理性，使生成动态更符合真实世界事件的物理规律（未明确说明是否达到SOTA）。

Abstract: Generating videos with physically plausible dynamics remains challenging despite advances in visual quality. Latent world models offer predictive signals for evaluating physical plausibility, but higher agreement between predicted and observed features may reflect easier-to-predict scene content rather than more plausible dynamics. We propose \textbf{S}elf-Referenced \textbf{P}hysical \textbf{A}lignment \textbf{R}ewards (\textbf{SPAR}), a framework that uses world-model predictions for inference-time physical alignment. SPAR compares each video’s prediction error with that of static clips constructed by repeating its own frames, providing a content-matched reference for evaluating generated dynamics. To account for the uneven spatial and temporal distribution of physical interactions, we introduce a unified \textbf{spatiotemporal reward refinement} strategy. \textbf{Spatial refinement} emphasizes localized prediction discrepancies, while \textbf{temporal refinement} moderates brief error peaks and supplements prediction errors with agreement between predicted and observed feature changes. Evaluations across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that SPAR improves the physical plausibility of both \textbf{text-to-video generation} and \textbf{image-to-video continuation}, bringing generated dynamics into closer alignment with the physical regularities governing real-world events. [64] Visual Thinking: Training-Free Active Perception for Counting and Localizing Hard-to-Find Objects cs.CVPDF

TL;DR: 论文提出 Visual Thinking，一个无需训练的主动感知框架，将视觉语言模型（VLM）与 Segment Anything Model 3（SAM 3）结合，通过迭代循环让 VLM 生成更丰富的短名词短语，再由 SAM 3 完成计数与定位。该方法针对小目标、遮挡目标和原始文本描述难以捕捉的目标，在多个计数与定位基准上优于 SAM 3，并以更低时延达到与 Claude Opus 5.5 高推理强度相当的性能。

Details

Motivation: 视觉基础模型能基于文本提示精确分割，但难以恢复小目标、遮挡目标或文本描述不佳的目标；VLM 能利用图像上下文提出更丰富的描述，却在精确分割和定位上较弱。论文旨在融合二者优势，解决复杂场景中难找目标的计数与定位问题。

Result: 在 CARPK、FSCD-147、PixMo-Count 和 OmniCount（Fruits）上评估计数与定位。相比 SAM 3，Visual Thinking 在 FSC-147 上将计数 MAE 降低约 18，在 CARPK 上降低约 8；在 FSCD-147 和 CARPK 上框 AP 提升超过 12。固定分块或不分块时，性能与 extra-high reasoning effort 的 Claude Opus 5.5 相当，单桌面 GPU 约 5.91 秒/图，而 Claude API 约 46.53 秒/图。

Insight: 创新点在于无需训练的迭代主动感知闭环：VLM 基于图像、候选裁剪和历史生成短名词短语，控制器按嵌入相似度、熵验证优先级和新颖性排序；SAM 3 检测后跨迭代关联目标，并用偏向窄提示的加权均值更新置信度，同时将高置信检测作为正视觉示例并引入图像分块恢复小目标。客观看，其价值在于以提示重写、排序和跨迭代置信融合有效组合现成基础模型，避免额外训练并兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: Vision foundation models can precisely segment objects based solely on a text prompt. However, they struggle to recover objects that are small, occluded or whose appearance is poorly captured by the original textual description. Vision language models can examine the image and its context, they can propose richer descriptions of the objects to be detected. But these models struggle with precise segmentation and localization. We introduce Visual Thinking, a training-free framework that combines a vision-language model with Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) through an iterative active perception loop. At each iteration, the VLM proposes short noun phrases using the image, candidate crops and history from past iterations. A controller ranks these phrases using embedding similarity between the original prompt and the proposal, an entropy-based verification priority and the novelty of the prompt. Next, SAM 3 uses prompts to detect objects in the image. We associate detections across iterations and update each object’s confidence score using a weighted mean of scores obtained from SAM 3 with a larger weight for detections using narrower prompts. We supply confident detections as positive visual exemplars for SAM 3, and incorporate image tiling to recover small objects in cluttered images. We evaluate counting and localization on CARPK, FSCD-147, PixMo-Count, and OmniCount (Fruits). Compared with SAM 3, Visual Thinking reduces the counting mean absolute error (MAE) by about 18 on FSC-147 and about 8 on CARPK. It improves box average precision (AP) by more than 12 on FSCD-147 and CARPK. Visual Thinking, with fixed or no tiling, performs on par with Claude Opus 5.5 at extra-high reasoning effort, while running on single desktop GPU in about 5.91 seconds per image, compared to about 46.53 seconds per image for Claude through its API.

[65] InCue: Making Better Use of What Multimodal LLMs Already See at Test Time cs.CVPDF

Shuwei Wu, Siyue Yu, Longin Jan Latecki

TL;DR: 论文提出InCue，一种面向多模态大语言模型的测试时框架。作者发现，即使MLLM回答错误，其内部注意力等线索仍常指向相关区域；而常用的“放大裁剪重呈现”方法带来的收益，很大部分并非来自新细节，而是来自让模型关注已看到的证据。InCue将视觉注意力与预测熵梯度作为内部线索并耦合成空间目标，仅优化现有视觉token上的偏移来引导注意力，无需额外视觉输入。

Details

Motivation: 现有测试时方法常通过放大裁剪相关区域来补充细节，但论文发现这种重呈现的收益很大程度上来自引导模型关注已持有的证据，而非新增视觉信息，因此希望在不增加视觉输入的情况下更充分利用模型测试时已看到的内容。

Result: 在匹配视觉token预算下，InCue达到SOTA，持续优于近期测试时方法；在V*上使四个MLLM最高提升8.9%，在其困难样本上最高提升23.4%，并可将增益迁移到八个通用基准。

Insight: 创新点在于将视觉注意力与预测熵梯度统一为内部线索，耦合成空间目标，并通过仅优化现有视觉token的偏移来引导注意力；客观看，其价值在于揭示重呈现收益的“注意力引导”本质，并提出无需额外视觉输入、轻量且可迁移的测试时优化方案。

Abstract: Even when multimodal large language models (MLLMs) answer incorrectly, their internal cues often point to the relevant region. A widely used remedy re-presents the cue-selected region as an enlarged crop to supply finer detail. However, we find that much of its gain requires no new detail: it largely survives when the crop comes from the already-seen low-resolution input, and partly even when the region’s cached tokens are merely copied. Part of re-presentation’s gain thus comes from drawing attention to evidence the model already holds. We propose InCue, a test-time framework that makes better use of this evidence without additional visual input. InCue treats visual attention and predictive-entropy gradients as Internal Cues, couples them into a spatial target, and optimizes only an offset on the existing visual tokens to steer attention toward it. Under matched visual-token budgets, InCue achieves state-of-the-art performance, consistently outperforming recent test-time methods and improving four MLLMs by up to 8.9% on V$^*$ and 23.4% on its hard samples. Its gains carry over to eight general benchmarks. Code will be available at https://github.com/paul-swu/InCue.

[66] Describe, Don’t Decide: Correcting Answer Bias in Multimodal LLMs cs.CVPDF

Shuwei Wu, Longin Jan Latecki

TL;DR: 论文研究多模态大模型在图像中比较两个实体时容易答错的问题，发现答案偏差主要发生在模型结合图像做决策的阶段，而不是视觉感知阶段，因为模型对实体的描述往往已包含正确答案。为此提出免训练的D^3方法：先分别描述每个实体，再在不看图像的情况下基于描述做决策，最后与模型直接答案融合。

Details

Motivation: 多模态大模型在图像实体比较任务中存在答案偏差，论文试图定位偏差来源并纠正它，提升比较类问题的回答准确率。

Result: D^3在十个benchmark、八个7-9B多模态模型共79个模型-基准组合上均带来提升；在GQA留出比较问题上，平均准确率从72.4%提升到83.2%，单模型最高提升21.5%，超过Qwen2.5-VL-32B直接回答的82.8%。

Insight: 创新点在于发现并解耦“描述”和“决策”：让模型先独立描述实体，再脱离图像基于描述做判断，并与直接答案融合；方法无需训练，强调通过推理流程设计缓解图像在场时的答案偏差。

Abstract: Comparing two entities in an image, multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) often answer wrongly. We find that the answer bias enters not when the model sees, but when it decides with the image in view: the model’s own descriptions of the two entities often already hold the right answer. We therefore propose D$^3$ (Describe, Don’t Decide): the model describes each entity alone, decides from these descriptions without the image, and merges this decision with its direct answer. Without any training, D$^3$ improves all 79 model-benchmark pairs across ten benchmarks and eight 7-9B MLLMs. On held-out GQA comparison questions, the mean accuracy of these models rises from 72.4% to 83.2% (up to +21.5% per model), higher than that of Qwen2.5-VL-32B answering directly (82.8%). Code will be available at https://github.com/paul-swu/describe-dont-decide.

TL;DR: 论文提出一个在灵长类自由观看自然场景时，从神经活动中重建完整自然图像的框架。研究记录了五只猴子LGN和V1的高密度电极活动，并跨眼动、试次和会话整合神经证据；先给出带1/f先验的线性方法，再将其扩展为将脉冲活动和注视信息token化后输入Transformer与扩散模型的深度学习解码器。

Details

Motivation: 以往从脑活动重建图像多要求被试保持眼球固定，条件不自然；而现实中灵长类会不断眼动，且感受野仅覆盖图像局部。论文希望在自由观看条件下重建自然图像，并借此检验何种自然图像先验更符合大脑的先验。

Result: 论文在五只猴子的LGN和V1高密度电极记录上验证方法，并宣称在fMRI、EEG、MEG和电生理等多种脑记录模态的自然图像重建任务上达到新的SOTA。重建质量主要取决于观看时间内累积的神经证据量、眼动追踪准确性和图像先验选择。

Insight: 创新点在于面向自由观看的自然视觉重建框架，跨眼动、试次和会话聚合局部感受野证据，并将脉冲活动与注视信息token化后结合Transformer和扩散模型进行全场景重建；客观上看，将脑活动重建从固定注视推广到自然眼动条件，并系统分析证据量、眼动精度和先验的影响，具有较强方法学价值。

Abstract: Reconstructing images from brain activity has two broad goals. The first is to build brain-computer interfaces. The second is to understand how the brain sees. The eyes deliver incomplete, noisy input, so the brain must fill in the gaps with its expectations of what natural scenes look like. This filling-in relies on the brain’s prior over natural images. Image reconstruction lets us test which natural image prior best matches the brain’s natural image prior. However, most previous work on image reconstruction relies on an unnatural condition: subjects must hold their eyes still. In reality, primates constantly move their eyes to see the world. We introduce a framework for reconstructing images from neural activity while a primate freely views a static natural scene. We record from five monkeys with high-density electrode arrays in the lateral geniculate nucleus (LGN) and primary visual cortex (V1). The recorded receptive fields cover only a small part of the image. Thus, we combine neural activity across eye movements, trials, and sessions to reconstruct the full image. We first present a simple linear method with a 1/f prior. We then scale this approach up with modern deep learning. We train a decoder by tokenizing spiking activity and measured gaze; these tokens are fed into a transformer and diffusion model to reconstruct the full scene. Three factors determine reconstruction quality: the amount of neural evidence combined over viewing time, the accuracy of eye tracking, and the choice of image prior. Our method sets a new state-of-the-art performance in reconstructing natural images from brain activity, across brain recording modalities: fMRI, EEG, MEG and electrophysiology.

Details

Motivation: 现有针对 SAM2 的攻击多聚焦局部掩码退化和短期时序特征破坏，未充分探索长期动态与竞争物体干扰；随着 SAM2 在下游视频理解与跟踪中的应用扩展，理解其对抗扰动鲁棒性变得重要。

Result: 在 Visual Object Tracking 和 Video Object Segmentation 的视频级与单次扰动实验中，方法显著提升攻击效果，视频扰动下 DRE 达最近基线的 4.4 倍、NRE 达 7.3 倍，并在黑盒设置下跨模型规模和跟踪器变体有效迁移。

Abstract: Promptable video trackers such as SAM2 achieve accurate and robust performance through strong temporal consistency, making them important foundations for downstream video understanding and tracking applications. As their use continues to expand, understanding their robustness to adversarial perturbations becomes increasingly important. Existing attacks on SAM2 largely focus on local mask degradation and short-term temporal feature disruption, leaving its longer-term dynamics and competing object interferences (Distractors) insufficiently explored. We observe that SAM2 itself exposes useful distractor cues when tracking becomes uncertain. Based on these observations, we propose DistractAttack, a white-box attack that uses background distractors to disrupt target tracking persistently. Its core procedure, Counterfactual Distractor Search (CDS), propagates clean and attacked trajectories in parallel to discover plausible competing regions and identify those that become increasingly effective under perturbation. The discovered evidence is accumulated over time to weaken target support and promote recurrent alternatives or disappearance predictions. Extensive experiments under video-level and one-shot perturbations across Visual Object Tracking and Video Object Segmentation demonstrate strong attack effectiveness, raising DRE to $4.4\times$ and NRE to $7.3\times$ their nearest baselines under video perturbation, while transferring effectively across model scales and tracker variants in black-box settings. [69] DynaCue: Comparing Motion and Timing Between Groups of Videos cs.CVPDF

Hao Chen

TL;DR: 论文提出 DynaCue，一种用于比较两组视频运动与时间特性的两样本置换检验方法。它不预先依赖单一线索，而是融合光流局部变化、帧连续性和外观评分来检测组间差异。实验表明其能识别受控干预及真实生成视频中与时间一致性/运动偏好相关的差异，并在多个数据集上显著优于其他方法。

Details

Motivation: 现有时间一致性评分可能无法反映运动偏好差异，例如 VideoScore 虽能区分部分 Pika 视频，却无法检测 VideoGen-RewardBench 中偏好视频与同提示替代视频的差异。论文希望在不预先选择单一指标的情况下，稳健比较视频组在运动和时间上的差异。

Result: 在受控实验中，DynaCue 检测到影响播放连续性、位移或节奏的五种干预带来的组差异；在三个真实生成视频数据集上，较小样本量下重采样比较的检测率至少为80%，而其他方法至少在一个数据集上检测率不超过60%。

Insight: 创新点在于将视频组比较建模为两样本置换检验，并融合运动、帧连续性和外观等互补测量；同时先扣除片段通常活动再测光流局部变化，并用帧差和冻结计数捕捉中断。客观而言，其价值在于减少单一评分偏差，把“偏好/时序一致性”评估转化为更稳健的统计组间差异检测。

Abstract: A score that distinguishes videos with different temporal-consistency ratings can miss differences associated with motion preference. VideoScore’s temporal consistency score distinguished higher- from lower-rated Pika videos in 95% of comparisons with 15 videos per group, yet could not detect the difference between preferred videos and their same-prompt alternatives in any of the 200 comparisons of 41 VideoGen-RewardBench pairs. We introduce DynaCue, a two-sample permutation test that combines complementary measurements of motion, frame continuity, and appearance to compare groups of videos without choosing a single cue in advance. DynaCue measures local changes in optical flow both before and after accounting for each clip’s usual activity, captures interruptions through frame differences and freeze counts, and incorporates scores for motion and appearance. In controlled experiments, DynaCue detected group differences across five interventions affecting playback continuity, displacement, or rhythm. In real-world evaluations of generated videos where viewers rated temporal consistency or chose which of two videos they preferred for its motion, DynaCue detected group differences in at least 80% of resampled comparisons on each of the three datasets at the smaller sample sizes studied. Every other method evaluated on all three had a detection rate of 60% or less on at least one dataset.

[70] Learning Agent States for Latent Action Modeling cs.CVPDF

Shaoan Xie, Fan Feng, Peiyuan Zhu, Peter Spirtes, Kun Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出 Agent-State LAM，用于从无标注视频中学习潜在动作模型。该方法联合学习紧凑的智能体状态表示，并用该状态建模帧间转移；同时引入空间匹配时间判别 SMTD，引导表示捕捉智能体不断变化的物理状态，而非背景和持久外观等无关视觉上下文。

Details

Motivation: 现有潜在动作模型依赖用于动作推断和转移预测的表示空间，但该空间容易将智能体的物理状态与背景、持久外观等视觉上下文纠缠在一起，导致推断出的潜在动作包含与动作无关的信息。

Result: 在干扰控制任务和六个真实机器人视频数据集上，论文方法在动作恢复、下游控制和世界模型预测方面均取得改进；摘要未明确给出具体数值或 SOTA 声明。

Insight: 创新点在于联合学习紧凑智能体状态表示与潜在动作建模，并提出 SMTD：通过冻结局部视觉特征进行空间灵活匹配，按最强局部匹配为帧打分，使背景和持久外观因跨帧仍可匹配而降低判别信息，从而促使表示聚焦智能体动态物理状态。

Abstract: Latent action models (LAMs) learn action-like latents from unlabeled video by coupling frame-pair inference with latent-conditioned prediction, enabling policy and world-model pretraining. However, what the latent must explain depends on the representation space used for action inference and transition prediction. When this space entangles the agent’s evolving physical state with visual context, such as background and persistent agent appearance, inferred latents can encode information unrelated to the agent’s actions. To address this challenge, we propose Agent-State LAM, which jointly learns a compact agent-state representation and latent actions modeling its transitions. The compact state aggregates dense visual features into a shared space for both action inference and forward prediction. To guide what this state retains, we introduce spatially matched temporal discrimination (SMTD), which trains the state to distinguish its source frame from nearby frames through spatially flexible matching with frozen local visual features. Rather than comparing global frame representations, SMTD scores each frame by emphasizing its strongest local feature matches across spatial locations. This scoring allows background and persistent agent appearance to remain matched across frames despite displacement, making these shared cues less informative for identifying the source frame and encouraging the representation to capture the agent’s evolving physical state. Extensive experiments on distracting control tasks and six real robot video datasets demonstrate improved action recovery, downstream control, and world-model prediction. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/Mid-Push/Agent-State-LAM.

Details

Motivation: 医疗专科 VLM 在各自领域表现强，但训练数据常为私有、模型架构不统一，分别部署多个大模型成本高昂，因此需要一种无需访问专家训练数据和统一架构的模型整合方案。

Result: 在超声、病理、OCT、眼底和牙科五个专科上，基于 90 多个公开数据集构建 914k 无标注提示和 47k 留出测试项，并在 132k 测试项上统一评测；参数为五专家总参数 10% 的单模型保留 94% 问答性能，2% 参数版本保留 89%。

Abstract: Specialist medical vision-language models (VLMs) are strong within their specialties but hard to unify: their training data are often private, and independently developed specialists cannot be assumed to share an architecture. Deploying these large models separately is costly. We present HuluOPD, an on-policy distillation recipe that unifies heterogeneous specialists into one lightweight VLM using only unlabeled prompts, without requiring reference answers or the specialists’ training data. The lightweight model answers each prompt, and the specialist of the prompt’s modality supervises its response; a continuation-aligned cross-tokenizer loss makes this possible even when they differ in architecture and tokenizer. We apply HuluOPD to five specialists, for ultrasound, pathology, optical coherence tomography, fundus photography, and dental imaging. To distill and evaluate them, we build HuluOPDCorpus, 914k unlabeled prompts, and HuluOPDBench, 47k held-out test items, from over 90 public datasets, and score all models under one protocol on 132k test items, including third-party benchmarks. A single model with 10% of the five specialists’ combined parameters retains 94% of their question-answering performance, and one with 2% retains 89%. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of unifying heterogeneous medical specialist VLMs without requiring their training data or a shared architecture.

Details

Motivation: 双时相卫星影像理论上需要比较灾前与灾后场景，但VLM可能仅依赖灾后图像或提示中的时间框架就给出变化答案。论文旨在检验模型是否真正进行时序证据 grounding，而非只看答案准确率。

Result: 在DisasterM3上，真实前后图对仅比重复灾后图控制高0.5个百分点，置信区间包含0，反转顺序仅变化-0.2个百分点；但真实图对比使用其他事件灾前图高2.5个百分点且显著。状态转移和多跳问题仍较难，光学与SAR输入表现不同。

Insight: 创新点在于提出受控诊断协议，通过多种输入扰动区分模型是否依赖预期时序证据，并主张地球观测时序基准应报告答案对证据的依赖性而非仅准确率。客观看，其价值主要是揭示轻量VLM时序grounding不足，并提出temporal scene graphs作为可检验的后续方向。

Abstract: Bi-temporal satellite imagery is central to disaster assessment: deciding what was damaged requires comparing a pre-event scene with its post-event counterpart. A vision-language model (VLM) can nonetheless appear to answer change questions while relying mainly on the post-event image or on the prompt’s temporal framing. We present a controlled diagnostic of temporal grounding and apply it to one lightweight VLM, InternVL3-2B, on DisasterM3: 1,659 optical and SAR image pairs from 41 events and nine disaster types, in event-disjoint partitions, with 9,031 reasoning questions. We compare post-only input, the true pre/post pair, the post image repeated twice, reversed image order, and an unrelated pre-event image. The true pair scores $+0.5$ accuracy points above the repeated-post control (95% paired bootstrap CI $[-0.2, +1.2]$), and reversing the order changes accuracy by $-0.2$ points ($[-0.8, +0.5]$). Under this protocol we therefore find no measurable benefit from the content of the true pre-event image and little sensitivity to temporal order. The model is not blind to the first image, however: the true pair scores $2.5$ points above a pair whose pre-event image comes from another event ($[+1.6, +3.4]$). State-transition and multi-hop questions remain hard, and optical and SAR inputs show different profiles. We outline temporal scene graphs as a testable next step, not a validated solution, and argue that temporal benchmarks for Earth observation should report whether answers depend on the intended evidence, not only how often they are right.

Motivation: 在线3DGS-SLAM地图优化通常只使用定位关键帧和不确定的前端深度进行训练与几何正则，未充分考虑3DGS地图高模型复杂度与稀疏视图造成的过拟合；同时新增视图和高斯都受实时预算限制。

Result: 摘要未报告具体定量指标或benchmark，仅宣称所提设计能在实时预算内同时改善在线地图的外观和几何质量。

Insight: 创新点包括：渐进接纳非关键帧并按各组帧提供的监督分配更新预算的视图管理；根据图像细节进行边缘引导的高斯放置以控制模型复杂度；利用累积渲染证据且无额外渲染成本的自参考不透明度正则化，抑制地图派生表面前方持续贡献的高斯。

Abstract: The realistic rendering of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has drawn interest from real-time applications that require maps to be built online. This led to coupling 3DGS with SLAM, providing camera poses and initial Gaussians for the map. However, the map is still optimized with designs inherited from conventional SLAM, which train only on keyframes selected for localization and regularize geometry with uncertain frontend depth. These designs overlook the model complexity of a 3DGS map: with millions of parameters but few views of each region, the map overfits to sparse inputs. Resolving this is challenging, as every view and Gaussian added to the map competes for the same real-time budget. We close this gap from both sides. To add views while keyframes preserve the structure of the map, we progressively admit non-keyframe images as mapping updates complete and allocate the limited updates across different groups of frames according to the supervision each group provides. To control the model complexity of the map, edge-guided initialization places new Gaussians where image detail requires them. To complement the uncertain frontend depth, Self-Referenced Opacity Regularization (SOR) uses accumulated rendering evidence, at no extra rendering cost, to suppress the opacity of Gaussians that persistently contribute in front of a map-derived surface reference. Together, these designs improve both the appearance and the geometry of online maps within the real-time budget.

[74] DeCo-Attention: Decompose to Compensate for Low-Precision Attention cs.CVPDF

Yuhang He, Harrison Zhang, Fanrong Li, Chang Liu, RuQing G. Xu

TL;DR: 论文提出 DeCo-Attention，一种面向视频扩散 Transformer 低精度自注意力的算法与系统协同设计。它针对 FP4 计算 QK 时量化误差被 softmax 指数放大的问题，通过在每个头上做特征分解找到若干主方向，并把轻量补偿项作为额外通道附加到 Q 和 K 上，使 FP4 矩阵乘在计算注意力分数时自行补偿舍入误差。方法无需训练，并结合 softmax 优化，将 FP4 计算收益转化为实际加速。

Details

Motivation: 视频扩散 Transformer 中数万 token 的自注意力因计算量随序列长度平方增长而主导生成时间；GPU 的 FP4 矩阵乘虽可加速，但 Q、K 的 4-bit 舍入误差较大，且 softmax 中的指数会放大量化后 QK 乘积误差，成为注意力误差的主要来源。

Result: 在 Wan2.2 上，QK 使用 FP4、其余部分在 GB300/VR200 用 FP8、在 RTX PRO 6000 用 FP4 时，注意力 kernel 相比 BF16 分别加速 2.13x、2.49x、2.82x，整体生成分别加速 1.55x、1.68x、1.80x。四个视频生成模型上，FP8 配置的 LPIPS 与已有 8-bit QK 方法相当，全 FP4 配置的 LPIPS 低于已有全 4-bit kernel。

Insight: 创新点在于无需训练的误差补偿：按头特征分解寻找 Q/K 舍入误差的主方向，将轻量补偿项作为额外通道并入 Q、K，使 FP4 矩阵乘在计算注意力分数时同时修正自身舍入误差；并协同优化 softmax 以把 FP4 计算优势转化为实际端到端加速。

Abstract: Self-attention over tens of thousands of tokens dominates the generation time of video diffusion transformers, because its cost grows quadratically with sequence length. The FP4 matrix products of modern GPUs can speed up attention, but the 4-bit rounding of the queries Q and the keys K introduces large errors. We show that the exponential in softmax amplifies the error of their product QK, which therefore dominates the attention error. We propose DeCo-Attention, a co-design of algorithm and system for low-precision attention. DeCo-Attention is training-free and compensates the rounding error of Q and K along a few principal directions found by a per-head eigendecomposition. The lightweight compensation terms are appended to Q and K as extra channels, so the FP4 matrix product corrects its own rounding error while it computes the attention scores. Once the products run in FP4, the softmax bounds the kernel, and we optimize the softmax so that the gain of FP4 computation translates into realized speedup. With QK in FP4, we implement DeCo-Attention with the rest of attention in FP8 on GB300 and VR200 and in FP4 on RTX PRO 6000. On Wan2.2, these kernels run 2.13x, 2.49x, and 2.82x faster than BF16 attention, and the whole generation runs 1.55x, 1.68x, and 1.80x faster. On four video generation models, the FP8 configuration achieves LPIPS comparable to prior 8-bit QK methods, and the fully FP4 configuration lower LPIPS than prior fully 4-bit kernels.

Abstract: Surgical scene understanding is constrained by data scarcity and long-tail class imbalance, as annotating surgical videos demands scarce clinical expertise while rare procedural events remain underrepresented. We tackle this through data generation and propose SurgGenesis, a generative surgical world model that learns future surgical state transitions in a latent space from visual observations and textual procedural descriptions, serving as a scalable surgical data engine. To adapt a large-scale video diffusion model to the data-scarce surgical domain without catastrophic forgetting, we introduce a three-stage progressive LoRA strategy that incrementally injects endoscopic visual priors, procedural semantics, and spatial motion cues. Rather than judging generation by visual fidelity alone, we validate SurgGenesis along two axes that separate a world model from a generic generator: (i) future-awareness, by comparing predicted futures against the real future through a GT-latent prediction gap and temporal-horizon analysis, and (ii) action-faithful usefulness, by checking that generated clips contain the commanded surgical triplet and that using them to rebalance long-tail events and drive downstream understanding (segmentation, triplet recognition, 3D reconstruction) yields action-specific gains. Abstract: Spatial reasoning in hospitals requires tracking look-alike equipment, interpreting patient-relative directions, and judging passage widths, yet existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of these abilities. We introduce Clinical3D-Bench, a simulated benchmark with 3,368 questions of eight types on egocentric walkthroughs of 373 hospital-room layouts. Layouts follow hospital design guidelines, answers are computed from the 3D scenes, and paired scene states test whether models change their answers when relevant evidence changes. Videos are photorealistic, and ten physicians screened the layouts. Among 35 multimodal large language models (MLLMs), the best open and proprietary models reach 45.7% and 53.6% macro accuracy, against 40.8% for an answer prior and 79.0% for physicians on a subset, and 27 of 32 open models score below 40% on two-way patient-relative directions. The proposed GeoJeV instead lets the MLLM compute with 3D: it reconstructs a scene memory from each video, selects 3D operations and their arguments from the MLLM’s question state, and adds their evidence to its option scores. [77] Rethinking Post-Training Quantization for Large Vision-Language Models cs.CVPDF

Minchan Kang, Kyeonghye Park, Seoyoung Cho, Junhee Lee, Daeshik Kim

TL;DR: 论文重新审视大视觉语言模型（LVLM）的训练后量化（PTQ），指出低比特权重-激活量化性能下降主要源于逐通道权重与逐 token 激活共享量化范围时，少数离群值主导范围，导致大多数数值分辨率不足甚至被映射为零。作者提出 Rotate-and-Refine Quantization（RRQ），结合局部 Hadamard 变换、分组量化和高效的组感知执行，作为简单有效的 LVLM PTQ 基线。

Details

Motivation: 现有 PTQ 虽不断改进校准策略，但在低比特权重-激活量化下仍出现显著性能退化；论文试图定位量化误差集中位置并分析权重/激活分布，以解决离群值主导量化范围、普通数值分辨率不足的问题。

Result: 在 8 个多模态任务和多种量化设置下，RRQ 显著优于基线，并降低推理时间和内存占用；W4A4 下，8B 模型保留超过 96% 全精度性能，32B 模型超过 99%，而基线不足 8%。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) compresses large vision-language models (LVLMs) by calibrating quantization parameters on a small dataset without retraining. However, despite increasingly sophisticated calibration strategies, prior methods suffer substantial performance degradation under low-bit weight–activation quantization. To understand this degradation, we locate where quantization error is concentrated and examine the corresponding weight and activation distributions. We observe that a few outliers dominate the ranges shared by per-channel (PC) weights and per-token (PT) activations, leaving insufficient resolution for most values and mapping many of them to zero. These findings motivate a shift in focus from calibration refinement to the underlying quantization representation. We therefore present Rotate-and-Refine Quantization (RRQ) as a simple, effective baseline for LVLM PTQ, combining local Hadamard transforms with group quantization and efficient group-aware execution. Across 8 multimodal tasks and diverse quantization settings, RRQ substantially outperforms baselines while reducing inference time and memory usage. At W4A4, it retains over 96% of full-precision performance on 8B models and over 99% on a 32B model, compared with less than 8% for these baselines. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/kmc3661/RRQ

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents translate screenshots and user instructions into executable actions. Most existing approaches rely on vision-language models to generate actions as text, often leading to substantial inference latency due to autoregressive decoding. To address this inefficiency, we propose GUI-PRISM, a hybrid action decoder that directly predicts action types and structured arguments while retaining language generation for open-ended text. Unlike existing spatial decoding methods that primarily focus on target localization, GUI-PRISM extends direct prediction to action types and their associated arguments, enabling complete GUI action prediction. Specifically, GUI-PRISM employs field-specific queries to extract action-relevant semantic and spatial information from a shared multimodal feature bank. It further models dependencies among action components through conditional queries. Gated residual cross-attention refines these predictions using fine-grained multimodal information. Abstract: Text-to-scene methods turn a short instruction into a furnished 3D room, but the room that a particular person wants rarely comes across fully in words: many preferences surface only when the person sees a concrete room and can point to what they like and dislike in it. Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents translate user instructions and screenshots into executable actions. Abstract: Coronary computed tomography angiography (CCTA) depicts coronary anatomy and cardiac fat, but segmentation alone does not ensure anatomically consistent measurements. Motivation: 解决流式视频生成场景切换中的冲突：既要保留切换前主体，又要清除缓存中持续存在的旧场景物体；现有 KV flush 或 sink 重算不足以移除旧场景内容。

Abstract: Switching scenes while preserving the original subject in streaming video generation exposes a conflict: cached history helps maintain the subject but also sustains old-scene objects that should disappear. Through controlled interventions on text conditioning, denoising steps, the local window, and the attention sink, we identify how cached visual history keeps this obsolete content. When the original frames remain in the cache, KV flush and sink recomputation still leave old-scene objects in the generated videos. Replacing this history with target-scene history largely removes those objects, but also changes the subject, reducing face similarity to the pre-switch subject. We then propose Molt Forcing, a training-free method that rebuilds scene history from a cold-start target scene while selectively retaining subject tokens from the original cache. Across 16 scene families, Molt Forcing removes old-scene objects at least as reliably as an Echo-Forcing hard cut while keeping the subject much closer to its pre-switch appearance.

Abstract: In embedding-based video anomaly detection (VAD), normal clips can have high similarity to anomalous descriptions, an anomaly bias that leads to false positives. Methods such as PRISM mitigate this bias by measuring deviations from a normality reference along anomaly directions. Both the normality reference and the anomaly directions are derived from text and shared across videos, although normal activity and the appearance of anomalies vary across scenes. The resulting scores are also sensitive to prompt selection. To address these limitations, we propose Visual Intrinsic Distribution Estimation (VIDE), a training-free framework that adapts text-derived scoring to each test video using frozen clip embeddings. VIDE estimates normal scene content from clips with low initial anomaly scores and uses this estimate to partially correct the textual reference. It then rescales category responses, penalizes clips with strong responses across categories, and refines the anomaly directions using high-scoring clips. VIDE averages scores across prompt views to reduce sensitivity to prompt selection and fuses scores from two frozen encoders. With both encoders, VIDE improves AUC over the best PRISM result by $1.5$, $1.9$, and $3.5$ points on UCF-Crime, XD-Violence, and MSAD, respectively, and AP by $6.5$ points on XD-Violence and $9.3$ points on MSAD. VIDE yields the highest AUC among the compared training-free methods on all three benchmarks and the highest reported AUC among all compared methods on XD-Violence and MSAD. Our code is included in the supplementary material and will be made publicly available.

Abstract: First-order reinforcement learning, such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), is widely used to post-train Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for multimodal reasoning. However, it incurs heavy memory and system overhead from backpropagation, optimizer states, and a reference model. Forward-only zeroth-order (ZO) methods, such as Evolution Strategies (ES) and its top-k variant Augmented Random Search (ARS), avoid these overheads via reward-guided weight perturbations, yet remain unexplored for VLM reasoning. We find that directly applying standard ES or vanilla ARS to VLMs suffers from two fundamental failure modes: (i) patch-grid overfitting, fitting fragile grid-specific visual token artifacts while ignoring spatial invariants, and (ii) catastrophic forgetting, degrading general perception and out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks due to unbounded high-rank parameter and policy drift. We propose ARS-VLM, a visually aligned Augmented Random Search framework for forward-only VLM post-training. First, ARS-VLM extracts a contrastive visual basis from bilinearly paired cross-grid views, concentrating perturbations on shared visual signals while suppressing patch-shift artifacts. Second, ARS-VLM anchors shallow visual-fusion layers with a soft subspace prior while freeing deep reasoning layers, restraining policy drift without a reference model. Third, ARS-VLM scores perturbations on paired views to discard view-fragile directions during top-k selection. We evaluate ARS-VLM on Qwen3-VL-4B and Qwen3-VL-8B on multimodal reasoning tasks. On 8B, ARS-VLM reaches 61.0% validation accuracy in 20 steps versus 150 for GRPO (7.5x fewer optimization steps) and avoids the late OOD collapse of vanilla ARS and ES. Moreover, ARS-VLM achieves 75.41% single-view micro accuracy across seven OOD vision reasoning benchmarks, compared with 75.31% for full-parameter GRPO, using forward-only post-training.

Abstract: Training pixel diffusion models remains less straightforward, owing to the ad hoc training recipes and architecture designs, along with the sensitivity to hyper-parameter choices. We show that strong denoisers can be built by refining existing pixel-native designs from the literature. Specifically, we revisit the long skip connection in U-Net and find that the optimization benefit commonly attributed to it is not necessary for its denoising benefit. We further find that the conventional linear combination of features from the skip and main backbone is inadequate for regimes of various noise levels. Motivated by these observations, we introduce two core modifications. First, we adopt asymmetric long skips, which propagate fine-grained information from input embeddings to decoder layers. Second, we introduce adaGLU, a SwiGLU-like MLP block, in decoder layers to adaptively filter information from the embeddings before being added to main-branch features. When trained from scratch, it scales smoothly from small-scale baselines up to 1.6B parameters, achieving 1.62 and 1.70 FID on ImageNet-256 and -512, respectively. Abstract: Existing clip-based soccer commentary models often produce delayed, repeated, or poorly synchronized narration on untrimmed full-match videos. They may describe nearby actions while failing to mention key events such as goals, cards, substitutions, and offsides correctly and on time. In this paper, we present SoccerNarrate to bridge low-latency streaming narration with event-grounded professional soccer commentary across data, training, and evaluation. First, we construct SoccerNarrate-Data, a large-scale time-anchored dataset with word-level ASR alignment, quality filtering, and entity calibration. Second, we perform macro-window preference alignment (MWPA), which aligns the model with complete event-level commentary semantics by comparing causal multi-step rollouts from the same streaming prefix while keeping second-level inference unchanged. Abstract: MediaStormBench is a multimodal benchmark for real-world film and television production, with each subset drawn from MediaStorm’s day-to-day work. We release its first subset, MediaStormBench-CameraLog (CameraLogBench), on a common foundational task in footage ingest and initial color grading: identifying the Log curve of a clip without metadata. Cameras record Log footage by compressing a wide range of scene light into code values with a vendor-defined logarithmic curve, and color can be restored correctly only once that curve is known. The curve, however, is recorded only in file metadata, easily lost in transcoding and team hand-offs. A colorist then has to try candidate curves one by one against a waveform scope, and a wrong choice shifts the tones and colors of the whole clip. Recovering the curve from the image alone is challenging, because vendor curves cluster tightly in code value and are coupled with unknown exposure. CameraLogBench is built on professionally shot footage and tests whether multimodal models can identify the curve, with a probe that removes the correct option to expose guessing from priors. Because every candidate curve is strictly defined by a published vendor formula, we derive four controls from one implementation of these formulas: graded negatives, synthetic frames of identical scenes, in-context specification digests, and a training-free formula baseline, which together decouple semantic priors from photometric measurement. Across 11 models, real-footage accuracy barely exceeds always guessing the most frequent curve; when the correct option is removed, models almost never abstain, revealing heavy reliance on scene and brand priors. Supplying the vendor specification sharply improves recognition on calibrated charts but hardly helps on natural images without a photometric reference, exposing a gap between reading a specification and measuring pixels.

Abstract: People reason with pictures as well as words. Interleaved multimodal reasoning gives models a similar way to solve problems by creating intermediate images and using them to guide further reasoning. Training models with this ability usually relies on task-specific demonstrations, which are difficult to collect. We introduce ThinkMorph-SSL, a two-stage training recipe that uses images themselves as a source of supervision. The first stage, pretext reasoning, converts visual pretext tasks, such as image colorization and denoising, into instruction-free interleaved traces. Models describe a corrupted or transformed image, generate a restored or canonical image, and interpret the result. The second stage uses automatically constructed demonstrations for question-driven instruction tuning. At the same instruction-tuning budget, models trained with pretext reasoning outperform those trained with instruction tuning alone. Abstract: Solar filaments are cool, dense chromospheric plasma structures held by magnetic field lines above polarity inversion lines. Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic scenes from RGB video requires reasoning about both 3D geometry and motion across viewpoints, yet existing methods either split reconstruction and tracking into fragmented pipelines or face prohibitive dense spatiotemporal decoding costs. We introduce MoGe-4D, a feed-forward framework that unifies geometry and motion prediction via ordered queries of paired allocentric and egocentric dynamic point maps. Each query maps source pixels to a target time simultaneously in both camera frames, resolving relative camera pose in closed form while flexibly serving depth, dynamic registration, and dense 3D tracking. For efficient dense querying, MoGe-4D decouples scene modeling from query decoding: a high-capacity context encoder caches coarse global spatiotemporal features once, while a lightweight query encoder rapidly decodes dense point maps at flexible resolutions. TL;DR: SplatTok 是一种数据驱动的3D高斯泼溅压缩编解码器：用transformer将现有splat编码为紧凑的、空间锚定的场景级潜在token，再由在数千个真实场景上训练的共享解码器合成一组全新的标准高斯基元，不要求与原基元对应。表示通过渲染监督而非基元重建来学习；压缩时仅对场景码做量化感知潜在搜索，网络保持冻结。

Details

Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅虽能实时渲染但存储开销巨大；现有压缩方法要么编码给定splat的基元，要么为每个场景单独优化紧凑表示，忽略了“不同基元排列可渲染出几乎相同视图”这一自由度。

Result: 在标准benchmark上，仅用输入splat的渲染做潜在搜索时，以小4～6.5倍的文件达到FCGS的PSNR，压缩率约75～110倍；结合训练图像时，以小13～41%的文件超过逐场景方法HAC++的PSNR；单一在DL3DV上训练的模型可泛化到含数百万高斯点的未见场景，并支持多种目标码率而无需重训。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian splats enable real-time rendering but require substantial storage. Existing compression methods either encode the primitives of a given splat or optimize a compact representation independently for each scene. Yet a Gaussian splat is only one realization of a scene: different arrangements of primitives can produce nearly identical rendered views. We exploit this freedom on the decoding side with SplatTok, a learned scene-resynthesis codec. Trained across thousands of real-world scenes, a transformer encodes an existing splat into compact, spatially anchored latent tokens, and a shared decoder synthesizes a new set of standard Gaussian primitives without preserving correspondence to the input. The representation is learned through rendering supervision rather than primitive reconstruction. At compression time, a quantization-aware latent search refines only the scene code, keeping the networks frozen, and closes most of the encoder’s remaining error. On standard benchmarks, using only renders of the input splat for the latent search, without the original training images, SplatTok matches the PSNR of the feed-forward codec FCGS with 4 to 6.5 times smaller files, compressing input splats about 75 to 110 times. With the training images, it exceeds the PSNR of the per-scene method HAC++ with 13 to 41% smaller files. A single model trained on DL3DV generalizes to unseen scenes with millions of Gaussians and supports multiple target rates without retraining.

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）在物体被移动后仍伸向训练位置的问题，发现模型存在场景捷径和指令捷径，并提出“Seeing Is Not Reaching”现象：视觉定位信息虽已存在，但常被指令到动作的直接捷径覆盖。作者通过分别屏蔽图像介导的定位通路和直接的指令到动作通路来验证机制，并提出无需训练、无需外部目标位置的Pathway-Separated Guidance（PSG），增强视觉定位并抑制捷径。

Details

Motivation: VLA被认为应继承视觉语言模型的视觉定位能力，但实际在目标移动后常按训练位置行动。论文旨在区分视觉定位是未被使用还是被捷径覆盖，并解决指令/场景捷径压制定位的问题。

Result: 在六个VLA上识别出场景捷径和指令捷径；在π0.5中，即使物体名称注意力跟随移动目标，指令捷径仍持续存在。PSG在LIBERO-PRO Object的位置扰动设置下将目标到达率从22.0%提升到52.6%，任务成功率从16.6%提升到40.8%，并基本保持原任务性能。

Insight: 创新点在于揭示VLA中视觉定位被直接指令到动作通路覆盖的机制，并通过通路分离验证定位与捷径的作用；PSG以无需训练和外部目标位置的方式放大定位、抑制捷径，为提升VLA鲁棒性提供了可借鉴的干预思路。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are expected to inherit visual grounding from vision-language models, yet often reach for an object’s training location after it has been moved. Across six VLAs, we identify two shortcuts: a scene shortcut, where the arm follows the scene’s original task despite a new instruction, and an instruction shortcut, where the instruction retrieves the requested object’s training location. In $π_{0.5}$, the instruction shortcut persists even though object-name attention follows the moved target. We call this Seeing Is Not Reaching. To determine whether this grounding is unused or overridden, we separately mask an image-mediated grounding pathway and a direct instruction-to-action pathway. Masking the grounding pathway eliminates reaches to moved targets, whereas masking the direct pathway increases them. Thus, grounding already guides action but is often overridden by the instruction shortcut, which acts through the direct pathway. We introduce Pathway-Separated Guidance (PSG), which amplifies grounding and suppresses the shortcut without training or external target locations. On LIBERO-PRO Object under Position perturbations, PSG raises the target-reaching rate from 22.0% to 52.6% and task success from 16.6% to 40.8%, while largely preserving performance on the original tasks.

Abstract: Geospatial search is fundamental to resource surveys and environmental monitoring, requiring the discovery of relevant evidence across large geographic areas and multiple spatial scales. Existing active perception methods adaptively select observation locations and scales to improve search efficiency, yet their search trajectories become increasingly long and costly as the search area expands. A key limitation is that final-answer correctness alone provides little guidance on which observations to retain and when to stop, hindering efficient wide-area search. To address this challenge, we propose EvoSearch, a self-evolving active perception framework that co-evolves the perception policy and exploration program to compress search trajectories while preserving answer quality. Specifically, quality-cost comparisons between completed branches from shared evidence train the policy to acquire useful observations, avoid redundant ones, and stop when sufficient evidence is available. Execution feedback from the updated policy then guides exploration-program revision, adapting experience generation to the policy’s changing observation needs. We further introduce GeoSearchBench, a benchmark for evaluating search quality and visual processing efficiency through relational search and region search. Motivation: 现有潜在动作预训练方法将视觉转换建模与动作推断耦合，直接将对齐观测或转换线索映射为潜在动作，导致“刻画转换的关系结构”和“抽象为紧凑动作表示”两个任务纠缠，限制了动作表示的质量和泛化性。

Abstract: Learning latent actions from unlabeled video sequences offers a promising way to acquire reusable action representations without relying on embodiment-specific control annotations. However, existing latent-action pretraining approaches commonly couple the modeling of visual transitions with action inference, directly mapping paired observations or derived transition cues into latent action codes. This formulation leaves two distinct tasks entangled: characterizing the relational structure of an observed transition and abstracting it into a compact action representation. We propose RELACT, a relation-first framework that explicitly separates transition relation modeling from predictive action abstraction. RELACT first constructs a structured relation representation $R$ that captures how visual elements and their interactions evolve across observations. It then infers a compact, state-conditioned stochastic latent action $U$, whose predictive effects are learned through action-conditioned latent dynamics. The resulting action representation can be grounded in native control spaces for model-based planning or used to supervise downstream embodied policies. Extensive experiments across in-domain and out-of-domain image-goal navigation, goal-conditioned manipulation, and downstream policy adaptation demonstrate improved predictive action representations and consistent gains in planning and control, supporting the effectiveness and generality of relation-first latent action pretraining.

[100] Relational Bottleneck Models cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Shin’ya Yamaguchi, Kosuke Nishida

TL;DR: 论文提出关系瓶颈模型（RBMs），一种无需训练、即插即用的框架，可将任意现成LVLM转化为内在可解释模型。RBMs包含结构化理由生成（SRG）和理由接地解码（RGD）两阶段：前者用主谓宾三元组建模视觉概念间语义关系，后者通过重要性加权专家乘积聚合理由条件下一词元分布，并严格从解码上下文中排除图像输入，从而保证理由与输出之间的因果依赖。

Details

Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）虽可解释，但依赖任务特定训练且使用孤立视觉概念，难以捕获高阶视觉语义关系；LVLM的零样本Chain-of-Thought推理又缺乏生成理由与最终输出间的严格因果依赖，影响忠实性。论文旨在同时实现高性能与高忠实可解释推理。

Result: 论文在多种基准和多种LVLM骨干上进行广泛零样本评测，表明RBMs无需任务特定微调即可获得优于CBM基线的可解释性，并提升任务性能；摘要未给出具体定量数值。

Insight: 创新点在于将结构化关系三元组作为可解释瓶颈，并通过排除图像输入的理由接地解码与重要性加权专家乘积策略，强制理由到输出的因果依赖；整体以训练-free、plug-and-play方式增强现成LVLM的可解释性与忠实性。

Abstract: Achieving both interpretable and high-performance inference is a key challenge in deep learning. While Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) provide inherent interpretability, they are constrained by task-specific training and rely on isolated visual concepts that fail to capture higher-order visual semantic relationships. Conversely, Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) demonstrate zero-shot capabilities through Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. However, standard CoT lacks a strict causal dependency between the generated rationale and the final output, compromising faithfulness. To bridge this gap, we propose Relational Bottleneck Models (RBMs), a novel training-free framework that transforms any off-the-shelf LVLMs into inherently interpretable models in a plug-and-play manner. RBMs consist of two stages: (i) Structured Rationale Generation (SRG), which explicitly models semantic interactions between visual concepts as subject-predicate-object triplets, and (ii) Rationale-Grounded Decoding (RGD), which enforces a strict causal dependency between the rationales and the final output. RGD aggregates rationale-conditional next-token distributions via an importance-weighted product-of-experts strategy, strictly excluding the image inputs from the decoding context. Extensive zero-shot evaluations across diverse benchmarks and LVLM backbones demonstrate that RBMs achieve superior interpretability while improving task performance over CBM baselines, entirely without task-specific fine-tuning.

Abstract: Dynamic Gaussian splatting is usually initialised either with random points or with a structure-from-motion (SfM) cloud of the first frame. Neither says anything about what moves, where and when. We introduce ST-SfM, a sparse spatio-temporal SfM for synchronised, calibrated multi-camera video that splits the scene into two branches. Static structure is detected once per camera on a temporal-median image, gated for temporal stability and matched along epi-polar lines. Motion is detected as space-time interest points (STIPs) in the video volume, described with HOG3D and matched only between candidates that agree both epipolarly and in time. Union-find tracks that refuse inconsistent merges are triangulated with RANSAC over all view pairs, giving static 3D points and dynamic 4D points (x, y, z, t) with sub-frame times. We use ST-SfM derived sparse 4D cloud to initialise 4D Gaussian splatting. In comparison with random initialisation on N3DV scenes we get improvements in PSNR, SSIM and LPIPS.

We introduce $ε$-Seg++, which places a semantic expert at every level of the hierarchy and consolidates their predictions with a parameter-free product of experts. We further enable 3D inputs through a leakage-free sub-volume splitting protocol, which allows 2D and 3D models to be compared under identical sparse supervision. Because validation labels are just as scarce as training labels, we treat checkpoint selection as part of the method and average the top-ranked checkpoints, a second ensemble, this time across training time. On the BetaSeg FIB-SEM dataset, the multi-level experts improve 2D mean Dice by about 0.1 at 2,500 training labels. With up to 600 training labels, the 2D model matches or exceeds 3D $ε$-Seg at about one eleventh of its prediction cost. Above roughly a thousand labels, 3D inputs dominate, and at matched annotation cost 3D $ε$-Seg++ reaches a mean Dice of 0.905 versus 0.880 for D3PM, while predicting between 5.4 and 27 times faster (with our checkpoint ensemble or a single model, respectively). Checkpoint ensembling matches single-best selection on average and markedly improves the worst case when the validation set is small.

Zichen Zou, Xiaosong Jia, Wen-Ming Chen, Zuxuan Wu, Yu-Gang Jiang

Abstract: Multi-view feed-forward models show strong scalability, but their reliance on costly geometric annotations limits data scaling. Existing 3D self-supervised approaches reduce this reliance through cross-view completion (e.g., CroCo, MuM, Muskie) or novel-view synthesis (e.g., RayZer, E-RayZer), typically learning image representations and cross-view interactions jointly. We argue that learning single image representations from scratch is unnecessary for 3D pretraining methods, as doing so fails to benefit from the continuing advances in 2D foundation models (e.g., DINO, CLIP, frontier VLMs). We introduce MaskedVGGT, which freezes a strong pretrained 2D encoder and pretrains only an added, randomly initialized multi-view transformer, focusing on learning interactions across views. MaskedVGGT pretrains the multi-view transformer through masked self-distillation without annotations: a student predicts targets at masked locations based on complete anchor views and heavily masked remaining views, while an exponential-moving-average teacher provides prediction targets based on unmasked inputs. In experiments across model sizes up to 1B parameters, MaskedVGGT pretraining followed by fine-tuning outperforms single-stage supervised VGGT-$Ω$ training with the same training data, model architecture, and total number of optimization updates. MaskedVGGT also outperforms E-RayZer, CroCo, MuM, and Muskie, providing evidence for the effectiveness of building multi-view pretraining on strong 2D representations. Finally, extending pretraining with additional unlabeled videos further improves generalization, and the learned representations transfer to diverse downstream tasks. These results demonstrate an effective division of learning: reuse strong image representations from 2D pretraining and learn cross-view interactions from unlabeled multi-view data without relying on geometric annotations.

Abstract: Reference-guided photographic color grading helps users realize their aesthetic intent by selectively drawing on the color and tonal characteristics of a reference photograph. [108] Query-Guided Visual Minimization for Selective Disclosure to Cloud VLMs cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.CRPDF

TL;DR: 论文研究面向云托管视觉语言模型（VLM）的查询引导视觉最小化问题：在图像发送到云端前，由本地可信变换保留与查询相关的视觉内容，同时尽量减少查询不需要的敏感信息泄露。作者提出 QGVM 框架，结合查询定位与独立敏感项清单，在类型、实例和文本内容层面判断披露必要性，并为黑盒下游 VLM 生成最小化图像。实验从任务效用、可直接恢复的敏感信息和推断式地理定位泄露等维度评估该方法。

Insight: 创新点在于将查询定位与独立敏感项清单结合，在类型、实例和文本内容层面判断披露必要性，并面向黑盒 VLM 渲染最小化图像。其核心思想是区分视觉相关性与披露必要性，使敏感内容仅在查询需要时保留，否则抑制。客观来看，该工作把查询条件化的隐私保护引入云 VLM 图像传输场景，并提供了任务效用、敏感信息恢复和推断泄露的多维评估。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted vision-language models (VLMs) require sending images beyond the user’s local trust boundary, potentially exposing sensitive information the query does not need. We study query-guided visual minimization: a trusted local transformation preserves query-relevant visual content and reduces unnecessary disclosure before transmission. Unlike query-agnostic anonymization, it preserves the same sensitive content when the query requires it and suppresses it otherwise. We introduce QGVM, a framework that combines query grounding with an independent sensitive-item inventory, determines disclosure necessity at the type, instance, and text-content levels, and renders a minimized image for a black-box downstream VLM. We evaluate QGVM on complementary benchmarks measuring task utility, directly recoverable sensitive information, and inferential geolocation leakage. With OpenAI GPT-6.1 Sol, QGVM achieves 85.3% DoxBench answer correctness versus 77.5% for PGP, while a separately prompted Sol geolocation attacker succeeds within 1 km on 5.8% of QGVM images versus 25.7% of unprotected images. On Scene-Privacy, QGVM reduces face re-identification from 27.7% (PGP) to 4.5%. On held-out BIV-Priv-Seg, unnecessary private-text recovery falls from 33.9% (PGP) to 15.3%. To verify that disclosure is query-dependent, we evaluate paired queries that either require or do not require the same sensitive information. QGVM achieves 69.6% correct disclosure switching, compared with 29.1% for grounding-only cropping. These findings highlight the value of distinguishing visual relevance from disclosure necessity, while revealing the privacy-utility trade-offs of selective visual disclosure.

[109] CREDIT: Compositional Reliability Learning for Noisy Composed Image Retrieval cs.CVPDF

Tiancai Zhou, Yongxiang Li, Suimei Liu, Zhong Yuan, Dezhong Peng

TL;DR: 论文关注带噪声三元组对应的组合图像检索（CIR），即参考图像、修改文本与目标图像之间的语义对应不可靠。作者提出CREDIT框架，通过证据不确定性学习显式量化三元组对应可靠性，并用扩散引导的参考表示细化提升组合查询特征鲁棒性。实验表明该方法在多种设置下持续优于现有方法。

Details

Motivation: 现有CIR方法通常用固定决策规则隐式判断三元组对应是否正确，但在噪声三元组对应（NTC）下语义可靠性并非二值，确定性置信度容易造成过度自信监督并传播噪声语义。论文旨在显式建模对应不确定性并抑制噪声监督传播。

Result: 摘要未给出具体基准和数值，仅定性报告CREDIT在多种设置下持续优于现有方法，说明其在不同噪声场景中具有较好的有效性和鲁棒性。

Insight: 创新点在于将三元组对应可靠性建模为不确定性估计问题，而非简单二值判定，并结合证据学习与文本条件扩散细化参考表示。客观看，其可借鉴之处是将不确定性感知监督与生成式特征细化结合，用于抑制噪声多模态检索训练中的错误语义传播。

Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) aims to retrieve a target image that satisfies user intent based on a multimodal query consisting of a reference image and a modification text. However, real-world training data are often corrupted by Noisy Triplet Correspondence (NTC), where the semantic correspondence among the reference image, modification text, and target image becomes unreliable. Existing methods implicitly assume that the correctness of triplet correspondence can be determined by fixed decision rules. However, under NTC, the authenticity of semantic correspondence is difficult to determine accurately, and different triplets exhibit varying degrees of semantic reliability rather than binary correctness. Consequently, deterministic confidence is insufficient to faithfully characterize correspondence reliability, often leading to overconfident supervision for unreliable triplets and progressively reinforcing noisy semantics during compositional representation learning. To address this challenge, we propose a Compositional Reliability learning framework with Evidential uncertainty and DiffusIon-guided refinemenT (CREDIT), which explicitly quantifies the uncertainty of triplet correspondence to estimate its reliability and suppress the propagation of noisy supervision. CREDIT consists of two complementary components: The Evidential Compositional Uncertainty Learning (ECUL) module explicitly models correspondence uncertainty via evidential learning, enabling uncertaintyaware reliability estimation to suppress noisy supervision. Building upon the reliable supervision provided by ECUL, the Diffusion-enhanced Reference Refinement (DRR) module further refines reference representations through textconditioned diffusion, producing more robust compositional query features. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CREDIT consistently outperforms existing methods under various settings.

TL;DR: 论文审计了19个多图多模态基准约33k个样本，发现24.2%的题目仅凭单张图像或问题本身即可作答，并将多模态大模型依赖单图作答的现象称为“单图捷径”。作者据此构建了需要真正跨图推理的ASTERISM基准（18.7k样本）、训练集ASTERISM-Train和人工校验的困难子集ASTERISM-Lite，并提出跨图像信用分配方法CICA，以在RLVR训练中减少对单图捷径的奖励。

Details

Motivation: 现有多图基准声称评估模型跨图像整合证据的能力，但很多题目存在单图可解捷径，导致评测和强化学习奖励可能高估模型的多图推理能力。论文旨在审计并修正这一问题，构建真正需要多图证据的基准和训练方法。

Result: 在ASTERISM-Lite上最强闭源多模态大模型得分为68%；现有RLVR目标对ASTERISM分数提升很小。作者提出的CICA在三个开源多模态大模型上使ASTERISM分数提升2.6到6.0分，超过通用和视觉专用RL目标在同数据上增益的两倍。

Abstract: Multi-image benchmarks aim to test whether a multimodal large language model (MLLM) combines evidence across images, but many of their items can be answered from a single image. Across about 33k items from 19 public benchmarks, our audit and a two-stage single-image filter find that one image or the question alone settles 24.2% of them. On these items, MLLMs recover from one image a median 78% of the accuracy that all images add over the text alone, so these items reward answering from one image, which we call the single-image shortcut. MLLMs take this shortcut even on items where one image is not enough. On such items, most of their wrong answers repeat an answer they give from one image, at a rate well above chance. We build ASTERISM, to our knowledge the largest audited multi-image benchmark, from these 19 benchmarks by keeping their items that need more than one image. Its 18.7k items, 2.0k of them corrected or rewritten, share one format, one scorer and seven task categories. We also release ASTERISM-Train with 49k training items and ASTERISM-Lite, a hard subset whose items we manually verify one by one. On ASTERISM-Lite, the strongest proprietary MLLM scores 68%. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), now common in MLLM post-training, rewards the shortcut as well, because a correct answer read off one image earns as much reward as one that combines images. In line with this, existing RLVR objectives add little to the ASTERISM score. To stop rewarding the shortcut in training as ASTERISM does in evaluation, we propose cross-image credit assignment (CICA), an extension of RLVR that moves the credit toward the tokens that need more than one image. On three open-source MLLMs, CICA raises the ASTERISM score by 2.6 to 6.0 points, more than twice the gain of each general and vision-specific RL objective trained on the same data.

[112] Get the Next Moment Right: Short-Term Event Planning for Physically Plausible Video Generation cs.CVPDF
