TL;DR: 论文提出STEP，一个无需训练的闭环短程事件规划框架，用于提升视频生成的物理合理性。它将生成过程分解为多个短片段，每轮从已生成帧感知场景状态、预测下一物理事件并渲染控制片段、引导生成下一短片段，并通过验证事件是否发生来决定是否重采样。

Details

Motivation: 现有视频生成方法通常预先预测整段视频的运动轨迹来引导生成，长程预测误差会累积并导致后续帧物理不合理。论文希望仅依赖可靠的短程预测，并在生成过程中闭环校正。

Result: STEP在Physics-IQ Verified和PhyGenBench上提升了三个冻结视频生成器的物理合理性，且无需训练即可应用于新的生成器；摘要未给出具体数值或SOTA声明。

Insight: 创新点在于将物理事件规划与视频生成解耦为短程闭环流程，每轮基于实际生成帧进行场景 grounding、事件预测、控制生成和验证重采样，从而避免长程轨迹预测的误差累积，并保持 training-free 的通用性。

Abstract: Video generation models synthesize realistic imagery yet still struggle to produce physically plausible evolution. Existing methods guide generation with a motion trajectory predicted for the entire clip in advance, so prediction errors accumulate and degrade later frames. We propose STEP(Short-Term Event Planning), a training-free closed-loop framework that guides generation one short clip at a time. Each round consists of four steps: grounding measures the scene state from the latest frames, event planning predicts the next physical event and renders it into a control clip, latent-guided generation produces the next short clip under this control, and verification checks whether the event occurs and resamples the clip if it does not. Because each round is grounded in the frames actually generated, control relies only on reliable short-range predictions. STEP improves the physical plausibility of three frozen generators on Physics-IQ Verified and PhyGenBench, and can be applied to new generators without training.

Abstract: Diagnosing with X-rays often requires reasoning about the 3D locations of abnormalities and their spatial relationships to surrounding anatomical structures, yet such reasoning remains challenging due to the loss of spatial information in 2D projections. To address this challenge, we propose Dreaming in CT (DiCT), a three-stage progressive CT-to-X-ray reasoning transfer framework that leverages a CT vision-language model (VLM) to guide an X-ray VLM. Through this CT-guided transfer, the student acquires CT-informed 3D reasoning capabilities, enabling it to reason directly from 2D X-rays without explicit CT reconstruction at inference time. The framework begins with instruct the dreamer, which equips the X-ray student with initial CT-oriented reasoning capabilities and establishes a structured interface that distinguishes X-ray observations from CT imagination. Building on this structure, visualize the dream introduces CT-guided on-policy distillation (OPD), where the CT teacher selectively guides the X-ray student’s reasoning trajectories to transfer 3D anatomical knowledge. Finally, manifest the dream adapts the acquired reasoning capabilities from digitally reconstructed radiographs (DRRs) to real clinical X-rays. Experiments across two VLM backbones on 3D-RAD and ReXVQA demonstrate DiCT’s effectiveness across radiological reasoning tasks, achieving a 1.66 percentage point improvement over a CT reconstruction-based baseline on 3D-RAD Abnormality Identification with 12.77$\times$ lower inference computation. Our code is available at https://github.com/HongYep/DiCT.

[114] LRQ-Cache: Compressing Feature Caches in Video Diffusion via Low-Rank Approximation and Quantization cs.CVPDF

Ruizhe Yao, Zhen Huang, Songpan Gao, Danyi Liu, Zijian Cao

TL;DR: 论文针对视频扩散Transformer（DiTs）中时序缓存带来的巨大持久化存储开销问题，提出了一种免训练的缓存压缩方法LRQ-Cache。该方法利用缓存特征中跨token共享的少量通道模式所体现的冗余，通过低秩近似与量化相结合，对每个缓存特征用一小部分因子表示并在存储前量化、复用时重建，从而在不改变现有缓存机制的前提下大幅压缩缓存状态，使预测类缓存能放入单张GPU并加速生成。

Details

Motivation: 视频扩散模型推理昂贵，时序缓存虽能跨去噪步复用中间特征加速，但保留稠密特征张量造成随视频长度、分辨率和缓存覆盖率增长的持久存储开销（每视频达109-346 GiB），超出单GPU显存，只能CPU卸载（变慢）或多GPU分片（缓存而非计算决定GPU数量）。论文希望直接压缩缓存状态本身。

Result: 在HunyuanVideo和Wan2.1上的三种缓存机制中，LRQ-Cache将预测类缓存的持久状态减少42-71倍，降至每视频2.3-4.9 GiB；TeaCache的VBench指标与原缓存相差在0.25个百分点以内，Wan2.1预测缓存的较大下降（最高3.5个百分点）在更高秩下会缩小；原本超过80GB H100显存的缓存现可放入单卡，生成速度比CPU卸载的原缓存快2.7-5.9倍。

Insight: 创新点在于发现缓存特征的能量大量集中于少数跨token共享的通道模式这一冗余结构，并据此将低秩近似与量化结合用于缓存压缩；方法免训练、即插即用，不改变原有缓存机制，仅压缩存储状态，思路简洁且工程实用性强。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers (DiTs) achieve high-fidelity generation, but inference is expensive because every denoising step evaluates a large transformer over all spatiotemporal tokens of the video. Temporal caching accelerates them by reusing intermediate features across denoising steps. However, retaining dense feature tensors introduces a persistent storage cost that grows with video length, resolution, and cache coverage, reaching 109-346 GiB per video for the predictive caches we evaluate. Such states exceed the memory of a single GPU, so native caches must either be offloaded to the CPU, which slows generation, or be sharded across several GPUs, e.g., with sequence parallelism, so that the cache rather than the compute sets the number of GPUs. We instead shrink the cached state itself, exploiting its redundancy: a few channel patterns shared across tokens capture much of the energy of each cached feature. Based on this observation, we propose LRQ-Cache, a training-free method that combines low-rank approximation and quantization for feature-cache compression. LRQ-Cache represents each cached feature with a small set of factors, quantizes them before storage, and reconstructs the feature at reuse time, leaving cache mechanisms unchanged. Across three caching mechanisms on HunyuanVideo and Wan2.1, LRQ-Cache reduces the persistent state of predictive caches by 42-71x, to 2.3-4.9 GiB per video. VBench stays within 0.25 pp of the native cache for TeaCache, and the larger drops of the Wan2.1 predictive caches, up to 3.5 pp, shrink at higher ranks. As a result, predictive caches that exceed the memory of a single 80 GB H100 GPU fit on it with LRQ-Cache, making generation 2.7-5.9x faster than with CPU-offloaded native caches.

Abstract: Human gaze provides a rich window into attention and intent, making its understanding essential for applications ranging from social signal analysis to human-robot interaction. Existing methods typically employ highly customized architectures relying on auxiliary inputs like head crops or depth maps, causing cascading errors and inference overhead. Trained on single domains and confined to isolated gaze following with heatmap outputs, they lack flexibility and generalization for diverse interactive scenarios. To bridge this gap, we present LookAnything, a foundation model that unifies diverse gaze-centric tasks for holistic gaze following. Benefiting from large-scale multi-task and cross-domain training, LookAnything flexibly adapts to various settings, generating diverse outputs (points, boxes, heatmaps, and language) and supporting broad gaze-centric localization, description, and reasoning. [116] Visual-State Graph Memory for Streaming Video Understanding cs.CVPDF

Ailar Mahdizadeh, Pritam Sarkar, Xiangteng He, Leonid Sigal

TL;DR: 论文提出 Visual-State Graph（VSG），一种无需训练的流式视频理解记忆机制，不存储原始帧序列，而是将视频流表示为对象及其关系不断演化的图，并记录事件日志。每个重复出现的对象对应一个持久节点，保存其带时间戳的状态变化历史，从而在固定输入预算下支持对过去内容的推理。

Details

Motivation: 流式视频中回答当前问题所需的证据可能已离开最近帧窗口；保留全部帧难以随视频长度扩展，而丢弃旧帧又会削弱对历史的推理能力。

Result: 在四个流式视频基准和七个 7B 到 32B 参数的开源视觉语言模型上评估，在匹配输入预算下，VSG 提升了对过去内容的推理能力，同时保持实时理解能力；摘要未给出具体定量指标或 SOTA 声明。

Insight: 创新点在于用动态场景图式记忆存储“流的状态”而非原始帧：以对象节点、关系、事件日志和带时间戳的状态历史构建记忆，并通过对象参与的关系判断新对象是否已存在；记忆在问题到来前随视频流构建，提问后按固定预算检索相关对象、事件和近期帧。

Abstract: In streaming video understanding, the evidence needed to answer a question may have already left the recent-frame window. Keeping all frames does not scale with video length, while dropping older frames limits the model’s ability to reason about the past. We propose Visual-State Graph (VSG), a training-free memory that stores the state of a stream rather than the raw sequence of frames. VSG represents the stream as a graph of objects and their relations, together with a log of events; similar to a dynamically evolving scene graph. An object observed repeatedly is represented by one persistent node, with a timestamped history of how its state changes. To determine whether a newly mentioned object already exists in the graph, we compare the relations it takes part in with those of stored objects. We build this memory as the video arrives, before the questions are known. When a question arrives, we retrieve relevant objects and events alongside recent frames under a fixed input budget. Our evaluation spans four streaming-video benchmarks and seven open-source vision-language models ranging from 7B to 32B parameters. Under matched input budgets, VSG improves reasoning about the past while preserving real-time understanding.

Details

Motivation: 论文关注超分辨率在目标检测等下游任务中的实际价值，指出仅优化重建指标可能不足，需要研究超分辨率处理对检测性能尤其是小目标检测的影响。

Abstract: Image super-resolution aims to reconstruct a high-resolution image from a low-resolution input and is relevant for surveillance, satellite imagery, medical imaging, and object detection. This paper investigates SRCNN-based single-image super-resolution for a scale factor of x4, starting from a classical baseline and progressively introducing residual learning, Charbonnier loss, and adaptive loss functions. The proposed approach includes a three-region adaptive loss, where image areas are divided into smooth, texture, and edge regions using gradient information. The models are evaluated using PSNR, SSIM, LPIPS, and EdgeMAE in order to measure both reconstruction quality and structural detail preservation. In addition to reconstruction evaluation, the paper analyzes the impact of super-resolution on object detection. The results show that improved reconstruction quality does not always lead to better detection performance. These results highlight the importance of task-aware evaluation when super-resolution is used in computer vision pipelines.

[118] Verify then Steer: Progress Hallucination-Aware Recovery for Continuous Vision-and-Language Navigation cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

TL;DR: 论文研究连续视觉语言导航（VLN）中测试时路线偏离后的恢复问题，提出“进度幻觉”现象：智能体将偏离路线的观测误判为任务进展，导致内部指令进度超前，难以依据早期子指令恢复。为此提出PHAR测试时恢复框架，采用“先验证再引导”范式：验证器检查VLM残差流表示以识别跨模态证据不匹配，并用分裂保形校准控制误报；报警后由引导模块用上下文bandit自适应选择指令进度回退程度进行在线恢复。

Details

Motivation: 现有VLN方法在测试时遇到路线偏离仍难以恢复，根源之一是智能体内部指令进度可能被错误推进，从而阻碍其回到较早子指令并重新导航。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE上，PHAR作为测试时框架提升了多种SOTA VLN方法的可靠性；摘要未给出具体数值提升，也未明确宣称达到新的SOTA。

Insight: 创新点在于提出“进度幻觉”这一失败模式，并利用VLM残差流中的跨模态证据不匹配进行检测，结合保形校准控制误报和bandit自适应回退指令进度，实现模型无关、可插拔的测试时恢复。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to follow language instructions to reach a target in an unseen 3D environment. However, existing methods still struggle to recover from route deviations at test time. We investigate this limitation through the lens of instruction progress, and trace it to a phenomenon we term progress hallucination: agents misinterpret off-route observations as evidence of task progress, causing their internal instruction progress to advance beyond the actual task stage and hindering recovery based on earlier sub-instructions. To address this failure mode, we propose PHAR (Progress Hallucination-Aware Recovery), a test-time recovery framework following a verify-then-steer paradigm. The verifier examines residual-stream representations of the Vision-Language Model (VLM) to identify mismatch of cross-modal evidence along the trajectories, and uses split conformal calibration to control false alarms. When an alarm is raised, the steering module uses a contextual bandit to adaptively select the rewind extent of instruction progress for online recovery. Through extensive experiments on R2R-CE and RxR-CE, our framework enhances the reliability of multiple state-of-the-art vision-language navigation methods at test time.

Details

Motivation: 长视频中简短且与问题相关的事件分散在大量冗余内容中，有限帧预算容易遗漏关键事件及其时间上下文。现有帧选择方法多强调语义相关性或时间覆盖，忽视了定位事件和建立时间关系所需的互补证据。

Abstract: Temporal localization in long videos is challenging because brief, question-relevant events are dispersed among extensive redundant content. Limited visual budgets increase the risk of omitting these events or their temporal context, undermining reliable reasoning. Existing frame selection methods emphasize semantic relevance or temporal coverage, often overlooking the complementary evidence needed to locate related events and establish their temporal relationships. To address this limitation, we propose TESS, a training-free framework for query-guided temporal event-structured frame selection. TESS selects frames that jointly show the reference event and the event asked about, in the required temporal order. Leveraging frozen vision-language representations, TESS integrates soft temporal gating, role-aware budget allocation, and within-role redundancy suppression to preserve complementary event evidence. Extensive experiments across four long-video understanding benchmarks and three multimodal backbones demonstrate that TESS consistently outperforms the strongest compared baseline by 4.0-5.5 percentage points under a fixed frame budget. Further analyses reveal greater improvements on longer videos and robust performance under tighter visual budgets, while independent evidence annotations confirm enhanced event and temporal-relation coverage. These findings establish explicit temporal evidence organization as an effective training-free strategy for preserving event relationships and supporting efficient temporal reasoning over long videos. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TESS_code.

[120] Factorized Context Attention: From Emulated to Native Grid Probing cs.CV | cs.CLPDF

Mohamed Eltahir, Manar Eyad, Yara Alzahrani, Fatimah Albesher, Duaa M. Tahir

TL;DR: 论文提出免训练的因子化上下文注意力（FCA），把此前通过提示在模型外部模拟的网格探测（grid probing）转化为模型原生的块稀疏注意力。FCA将输入一次性编码进KV cache，以批量掩码序列前向读取所有局部和跨步span，并用阶梯位置调度保证span处于训练位置范围。该方法在长视频理解、长上下文文本推理和图像grounding上以更低成本达到网格探测的精度和覆盖范围。

Details

Motivation: 长上下文单次前向会变慢、掉精度并受上下文窗口限制；稀疏注意力虽高效但仍存在精度和窗口限制。网格探测可免训练恢复精度并扩展范围，但每个局部/跨步span都要重复整段计算，成本高。

Result: 在长视频上比单次前向约提升10个点，在长小说上提升9到14个点；可在超过一千帧的视频和四倍窗口长度的文本中完成“大海捞针”，回答同一输入后续问题的耗时低至网格探测的十分之一；整体以更低成本匹配网格探测的精度和覆盖范围。

Insight: 核心创新是将网格探测重新解释为一种由提示从外部模拟的注意力模式，并将同一模式原生实现为块稀疏注意力：一次KV cache编码、批量掩码前向和阶梯位置调度，避免重复编码。可借鉴之处在于把外部多遍提示推理归纳为模型内部稀疏注意力机制，并保持免训练、兼容冻结语言与视觉语言模型。

Abstract: Models read a book, an hour of video, or an image in one forward pass. As the context grows, this pass slows down, loses accuracy, and stops at the context window. Sparse attention is efficient, but keeps the other two limitations. Grid probing, a recent training-free method, recovers the accuracy and extends the reach by asking a frozen model the same question about short local and strided spans of the input, but every question repeats the whole computation, since each span is encoded again. Our insight is that grid probing is emulated attention, an attention pattern reproduced from outside the model through prompts, since each probe only restricts which part of the input the question can see. We introduce Factorized Context Attention (FCA), a training-free block-sparse attention that runs the same pattern natively, as the attention of the model itself. FCA encodes the input once into a KV cache, reads all spans as masked sequences in one batched forward, and uses a stair position schedule to keep every span inside the trained position range. Across long video understanding, long-context text inference and image grounding, FCA matches the accuracy and the reach of grid probing at a fraction of its cost. It gains about 10 points over the single pass on long video and 9 to 14 points on long novels, finds needles in videos of over a thousand frames and in texts four times the length of the window, and answers a later question about the same input in as little as a tenth of the time of grid probing. Native attention thus keeps what emulated attention gains without its cost, all with existing frozen language and vision-language models.

Chenrui Fan, Qiyang Hu, Luca Scharr, Paolo Favaro

Abstract: Video generators now produce realistic clips of scenes and objects, which makes generated video an attractive source of 3D content. Yet generated video is not 3D consistent: static content slowly deforms or slides, 3D reconstruction degrades, and the errors are too subtle to label by hand. To make generators 3D consistent, or to build rewards and guidance that push them there, we need data that say where and by how much a generated video breaks rigidity, and these data must come from generated video itself. We obtain per-pixel ground-truth labels from real-generated twins: a real clip paired with its re-creation by a keyframe-conditioned video generator. After inserting real keyframes so that the twin camera roughly follows the real one, we measure per pixel, in degrees, how far the twin departs from a rigid scene seen along its own camera path. We release Non-Rigidly Generated Twins (NRG-Twins), 871 twins, the code that builds them for new generators, and a benchmark with an official test split. A detector trained on these data, the Non-Rigidly Generated Pixel Detector (NRG-PD), predicts the per-pixel angle from RGB alone. It separates inconsistent from consistent pixels with AUROC 0.85, against 0.40-0.74 for seven existing consistency and motion-segmentation methods, and its predictions improve 3DGS of video that contains generated content.

[122] Video Models are Excellent Motion Models cs.CV | cs.GRPDF

Details

Motivation: 论文重新审视专用运动模型的必要性，探究视频基础模型是否已蕴含更丰富、更通用的运动先验，并解决视频模型与专用运动模型之间缺乏统一比较和评估框架的问题。

Abstract: Video foundation models have emerged as powerful generators of dynamic visual content, exhibiting remarkable capabilities in synthesizing complex actions and interactions. We argue that these models have also become excellent motion generators, with motion priors that are richer and more general than those learned by dedicated motion models. Their advantage extends beyond visual realism and is manifested in two important aspects: motion naturalness, including coherent body coordination, physical plausibility, and realistic interactions with objects and the environment; and motion scope, encompassing a much broader range of activities and semantic coherence, particularly uncommon, complex, and compound actions. To investigate this position, we introduce a common evaluation framework that compares video and dedicated motion models through three complementary settings: text-only motion generation, reconstruction of video-generated motion from sparse keyframes, and video-based enrichment of motion-model outputs. By converting all outputs into a shared 3D skeletal representation, we evaluate motion naturalness, semantic alignment, action coverage, physical motion characteristics, and fidelity to motion constraints. Our results suggest that video foundation models can generate more natural and semantically meaningful movements, particularly for complex and compound actions. Moreover, we investigate motion-conditioned generation through loose skeletal control, finding that dedicated motion models do not consistently improve video-generated motion, while video models can enrich skeletal animations using sparse motion guidance. Our findings suggest a shift in the role of specialized motion models, from generating complete animations to guiding and controlling the richer motion capabilities already embedded in video foundation models.

Insight: 创新点在于将生成观测累积为在线点云记忆并重投影指导后续生成，利用 ERP 水平循环对称性做路径朝向规范化以对齐运动与朝向，并用时间步依赖的通道门控自适应调节不完美记忆引导，兼顾结构引导与伪影抑制。

Abstract: Long-horizon panoramic video wandering aims to continuously synthesize scene-consistent $360^\circ$ observations along user-specified camera trajectories from an initial equirectangular projection (ERP) panorama. Existing panoramic video models have achieved promising results in camera-controlled generation, while recent 3D-guided approaches further provide geometric guidance for improving scene consistency. However, stable long-horizon wandering remains challenging due to diverse motion–orientation relationships and imperfect memory guidance induced by accumulated generation artifacts and depth inaccuracies. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{PVWander}, a 3D-memory-guided framework that accumulates generated observations into an online point-cloud memory and reprojects it to guide subsequent generation, with two complementary designs: \textit{Path-Facing canonicalization} for motion–orientation alignment and \textit{Timestep-aware gating modulation} for adaptive memory guidance. \textit{Path-Facing canonicalization} exploits the horizontal cyclic symmetry of ERP panoramas to uniformly align each clip’s ERP orientation with its principal translation direction, yielding more consistent, forward-like relative motions while preserving the prescribed camera path. \textit{Timestep-aware gating modulation} exploits the coarse-to-fine nature of diffusion denoising to adaptively regulate reprojected memory guidance through timestep-dependent channel-wise gates, preserving its structural benefits while mitigating visual artifacts from imperfect projections. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PVWander achieves improved long-horizon generation quality, while ablations validate the effectiveness of both designs.

[124] A Mesh to Remember: Persistent Appearance for Interactive Video Worlds cs.CVPDF

Joonghyuk Shin, Alex Trevithick, Katarína Tóthová, Liyuan Zhu, Shengyu Huang

TL;DR: 论文提出 M2R（Mesh to Remember），一种少步因果视频渲染器，用于交互式视频世界模型。它利用网格作为像素所对应表面的“地址”，将渲染器自身特征存储在各物体坐标系下的表面上，并在表面重新可见时通过栅格化检索，从而让外观随物体移动、跨重访持久并可在多智能体间共享。

Details

Motivation: 现有视频世界模型难以在长交互中保持一致性；显式3D世界虽几何和状态持久，但外观不够真实。将粗代理渲染给视频模型时，代理只指定“在哪里”，外观需被重新生成且容易被遗忘。

Result: 仅用合成数据训练，M2R 可零样本迁移到真实室内扫描和编码智能体生成的世界；在静态、动态和多智能体基准上一致性优于SOTA，物体持久性提升一倍以上，跨智能体一致性接近翻倍，并以40 FPS实时渲染720p视频。

Insight: 核心创新是用网格建立跨帧、跨视角、跨物体运动的表面级外观地址，将生成特征绑定到物体表面而非帧缓存，实现持久且可共享的外观记忆，且内存随可见表面增长而非帧数增长。

Abstract: Video world models synthesize photorealistic observations in response to user actions but struggle to remain consistent over long interactions. Coding agents and text-to-3D models instead generate explicit 3D worlds whose geometry and state persist by construction, but whose appearance falls far short of photorealism. Recent methods combine the strengths of both by rendering such worlds into coarse proxies that guide a video model. However, since these proxies only specify where things are, the renderer must invent what they look like as the world is explored, and anything it forgets is reinvented when a surface reappears. Our key insight is that a mesh identifies which surface each pixel shows, across frames and views, even as objects move, allowing it to serve as an address for what the renderer generates. We propose M2R (Mesh to Remember), a few-step causal video renderer that stores its own features on the surfaces they depict, in each object’s frame, and retrieves them by rasterizing the mesh whenever those surfaces come back into view. Appearance thus travels with objects wherever they move, persists across revisits, and is shared by every agent that observes it, while memory grows with the surfaces seen instead of the number of frames. Trained only on synthetic data, M2R transfers zero-shot to real indoor scans and to worlds authored by coding agents. It outperforms state-of-the-art methods in consistency across static, dynamic, and multi-agent benchmarks, more than doubling object permanence and nearly doubling cross-agent consistency, while rendering 720p video in real time (40 FPS).

Details

Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型面对同一视觉输入下的多个问题时，需要逐个问题自回归生成答案，随着问题数量增加顺序解码开销累积，端到端完成时间变长；同时预测结果可能随问题顺序变化而不稳定。论文旨在实现更高效且对问题顺序更鲁棒的多问题视觉决策。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于将多问题视觉推理转化为单次前向的并行决策，通过统一序列组织共享视觉上下文、问题与决策槽，并用 Decision-Query Mask 精确控制每个决策查询的注意力范围，从而复用语言模型头同时产出所有答案分布。另一个关键点是采用案例平衡的多顺序训练提升对问题重排序的鲁棒性；从客观角度看，其价值在于把“多问题批处理”从外部调度层面推进到模型内部注意力与输出机制层面，兼顾效率、准确率和顺序稳定性。

Abstract: Visual decision-making demands accurate judgments and timely responses. Yet for multiple questions sharing the same visual input, autoregressive multimodal large language models generate answers token by token, accumulating sequential decoding costs as question count grows and delaying completion of the decision set. Their predictions can also vary with question order. We introduce ViDM, which transforms multi-question visual inference into parallel decisions in a single forward pass, improving both accuracy and inference efficiency. ViDM organizes shared visual context, multiple questions, and question-specific decision slots into one sequence, reusing the native language-model head to produce all answer distributions simultaneously. A Decision-Query Mask aligns each decision query’s direct attention with shared context and its corresponding question, while case-balanced multi-order training strengthens robustness to question reordering. Across nine benchmarks spanning image, multi-image, and video tasks, ViDM improves Qwen3.5-4B’s average accuracy from 73.49% to 77.55% while achieving 8.4x the baseline’s average end-to-end throughput. On GQA, where each evaluated image has 11.4 questions on average, the end-to-end speedup reaches 20.3x. ViDM also improves mean and worst-order accuracy while reducing order-induced variation across multiple benchmarks, with further validation on LLaVA-OneVision-7B and InternVL3.5-4B.

Details

Motivation: 现有基于裁剪视图的多模态on-policy蒸馏依赖为每个区域专门策划的问答对，导致标注成本高且难以扩展到无标签图像；同一区域的所有rollout只回答同一问题，监督覆盖不全面；问题预设目标存在，使学生模型容易在目标缺失时也强行作答。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often recognize fine-grained details more accurately in a relevant crop than in the full image. Multimodal on-policy distillation (OPD) methods exploit this by transferring the advantage of a crop-view teacher to a full-image student. However, their supervision relies on region-specific questions curated through an elaborate synthesis pipeline, which brings three weaknesses: (1) every region needs a question–answer pair written exclusively for it, locking training into costly QA curation that is hard to scale to unlabeled images; (2) all rollouts of a region answer the same question, so the teacher supervises only the details it asks about, leaving other visual elements in the region unsupervised; and (3) every question presupposes that its target exists, so the student becomes prone to answering even when the target is absent. In this paper, we study how to turn unlabeled images into broad visual supervision through multi-aspect self-description and privileged-view distillation, and propose CaptionOPD, which replaces tailored questions with a fixed set of image-agnostic caption prompts, ranging from general descriptions to focused captions of parts, colors, counts, text, and layout. The rollouts of a region receive different prompts, so the teacher supervises various aspects of the region. Moreover, because caption prompts mask the model to describe what is visible instead of querying a specific target, they avoid presupposing what the region contains. Using the model itself as the crop-view teacher, CaptionOPD requires no questions, no answers, and no larger model annotations: only images and region boxes. Experiments show that, trained on the same images and regions, CaptionOPD outperforms prior OPD methods across multiple fine-grained and general perception benchmarks.

Abstract: Underwater degradation changes image appearance and can compromise the semantic cues used to guide enhancement. Identifying scene content alone does not explicitly specify the appearance to recover or the input-dependent changes associated with that target. We propose target-discrepancy knowledge distillation (TDD) for underwater image enhancement, which organizes guidance around these two aspects of the same restoration problem. For each degraded-reference training pair, reference features from an underwater vision-language teacher provide target-appearance supervision, while paired feature residuals describe the observation’s discrepancy relative to the same target. Both representations are jointly distilled into a lightweight guidance network that predicts them from the degraded image alone; neither the teacher nor reference images are required at inference. To align their use with their supervisory roles, we introduce progressive knowledge fusion within a multi-scale enhancement network. The estimated discrepancy first conditions spatial feature updates, after which target-appearance guidance refines the updated features and channel interactions consolidate their responses. This correction-before-refinement design lets target-guided refinement build on discrepancy-conditioned features. Experiments on six underwater benchmarks demonstrate competitive performance across full-reference and no-reference metrics. Ablations and feature visualizations analyze the contributions of the knowledge source, joint distillation, and progressive fusion.

Abstract: Vision-language models often rely on global context and language priors while overlooking answer-determining local details. Yet our cross-scale diagnostics show that models retain over half of their full-image accuracy when local evidence is completely masked, indicating that single-image correctness does not guarantee reliance on local evidence, while the need for visual correction also varies across tokens with their spatial attention states. We propose STAR, a visual on-policy self-distillation framework combining independent counterfactual dual-world rollouts with spatial-attention-guided target reconstruction. To resolve this issue, we propose OmniPersist, a unified framework that couples perspective video generation with panoramic evolution within a single-stage diffusion model, without relying on auxiliary models or initial panoramic inputs. Abstract: Black-Box Universal Domain Adaptation (B$^2$-UniDA) aims to adapt a target model to an unlabeled target domain under arbitrary label-space relationships, while neither source data nor source-model parameters are accessible during adaptation. Under such restrictive information access, reliable target supervision is unavailable, while predictions from the black-box source model can be unreliable due to source bias and domain shifts. To address this challenge, we propose Progressive Reliable Supervision (PReS), which progressively establishes and refines reliable target supervision by coordinating complementary knowledge from the black-box source model, foundation models, and the evolving target model. PReS first identifies reliable known samples by exploiting semantic consistency under diffusion-based perturbations and cross-model consensus between the black-box source model and CLIP. It then estimates the sample-wise reliability of the two predictors and adaptively integrates their complementary predictions to construct reliable pseudo labels. The resulting supervision is exploited asymmetrically to encourage confident and discriminative predictions for reliable known samples while maintaining uncertain yet locally consistent predictions for potential unknown samples. As adaptation proceeds, the emerging target-domain knowledge is further leveraged to refine both the pseudo labels and the known/unknown sample partition, forming a closed-loop process in which supervision construction and target adaptation mutually reinforce each other. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets under diverse label-space relationships demonstrate that PReS achieves state-of-the-art or highly competitive performance under the restrictive B$^2$-UniDA setting.

Abstract: Both single-modal and multi-modal anomaly detection aim to identify deviations from normal patterns, providing a common foundation for unified detection. However, existing methods rely on modality-specific modeling and scoring, making it difficult to obtain comparable anomaly evidence with limited normal references. To that end, we propose UnifiedAD, which unifies single-modal and multi-modal anomaly detection through anomaly evidence calibration, requiring only one normal reference per target category and no gradient updates. Specifically, we first derive neuron-level discriminative priors from normal and anomalous samples in an independent auxiliary dataset and combine them with neuron response consistency under normality-preserving transformations of the target reference to select reliable neurons, termed anomaly neurons, and identify anomaly directions. Next, we construct a low-rank normal subspace for each layer and modality from the selected neurons’ spatial responses to the reference and quantify query feature deviations from normal structure using projection residuals. Motivation: 现有 Sol-Attn 采用块级稀疏路由，虽保留关键交互，但块内注意力往往只关注少数 token，导致仍需评估大量低响应成员。论文希望更精细地分配精确计算，以降低训练-free 视频扩散推理中的注意力开销。

Insight: 创新点在于无需训练的分层稀疏注意力路由：利用统计阈值从块到 token 逐步缩小精确计算范围，并通过块中心相关性与 Key/Value 块内变化等异构统计识别难近似块，再比较原始 token 相关性选择精确计算成员。客观上看，其价值在于细粒度、低开销地分配精确计算，减少低响应成员评估。

Abstract: Sol-Attn (Sparsifying online attention) combines sparse exact computation with low-cost representative approximation to accelerate training-free inference in diffusion video models. However, intra-block attention can focus on only a few tokens. While preserving these key interactions exactly, the block-level decision incurs the cost of evaluating many low-response members. We propose Sol$^{3}$, a training-free hierarchical sparse attention method that further refines how exact computations are allocated. First, we introduce Gaussian-guided hierarchical routing, which determines the scope of exact computation step by step, from blocks to tokens, using statistical thresholds at each level. Only a subset of members within Mixed blocks that require refinement is selected for exact computation, while representative terms approximate the remaining members. We further design heterogeneous routing statistics that combine block-center correlations with variations in Key and Value within blocks to identify blocks that are difficult to approximate. Among these Mixed blocks, we uniformly compare raw token relevance to select members requiring exact computation. Experiments on video generation, audio-video co-generation, and world simulation tasks demonstrate that Sol$^{3}$ achieves 86% attention sparsity and delivers a $3.07\times$ attention speedup while preserving quality. Our code is available at https://github.com/FofGofx/sol3.

Details

Motivation: 恶劣天气会导致LiDAR和4D Radar出现缺失回波与虚假回波，降低单车特征质量并影响协同检测；同时，修复后不同车辆特征的可靠性仍有差异，需要根据观测条件和质量进行合理融合。

Abstract: Multi-vehicle collaborative perception can use observations from different perspectives to alleviate the problems of occlusion and limited sensing range. However, missing echoes and false echoes caused by bad weather will reduce the quality of single-vehicle features and affect collaborative detection. LiDAR provides richer geometric information, and 4D Radar has strong weather adaptability. The complementarity of the two provides the possibility to improve perception under degraded observations. However, how to adjust cross-modal repair according to the current observation conditions and how to reasonably integrate multi-vehicle features whose reliability still differs after repair are still problems that need to be solved. To this end, we propose CoReQ, a condition-aware repair and quality-guided collaborative perception framework. For the former, the condition-guided bidirectional cross-modal repair module (CBCR) extracts vehicle-level observation conditions from point cloud occupancy, density and feature statistics, and adjusts the bidirectional correction of LiDAR and Radar features accordingly. For the latter, we combine local reliability assessment with collaborative weighting: first, the task-aware quality estimation module (TaQE) uses the classification and positioning information of the EMA teacher to construct a supervision target and predict the spatial quality of the repaired features; then, the condition and quality guided fusion module (CoQF) matches the quality information with the content, and the observation conditions of both parties are jointly used for multi-vehicle feature weighted fusion. Experiments on V2X-R and V2X-Radar-C show that the framework is competitive under a variety of observation conditions, achieving significant gains in AP@0.7 on V2X-R under snowy conditions, and achieving the highest AP among the compared V2X-Radar-C methods.

Abstract: We present VenusOCR2.0, a compact 1B-parameter vision-language recognizer designed for both high-accuracy and high-throughput document parsing across text, tables, and formulas. To continuously improve recognition quality without self-training collapse, we build a bounded recursive self-improvement (RSI) data engine where the model acts strictly as a weakness probe, while external multi-expert medoid consensus, VLM visual verification, and factorized synthesis supply reliable supervision across pretraining, curated SFT, and RLVR. VenusOCR2.0 couples a dynamic-resolution SigLIP2 encoder and 2x2 spatial token merging with a Qwen3-0.6B decoder, adopting compact atomic OTSL tokens to shorten table sequences and reduce structural errors. To further eliminate the autoregressive decoding bottleneck, we introduce PTPv2, a lightweight ($\sim$67M) decoupled parallel draft module that combines frozen multi-layer verifier conditioning, a standalone learned mask, and block-causal all-position next-token prediction. On OmniDocBenchv1.6, VenusOCR2.0 achieves an overall score of 96.45 and a state-of-the-art 98.57 formula CDM, substantially outperforming massive general-purpose VLMs such as Gemini-3-Pro (92.91) and Qwen3-VL-235B (89.78). Abstract: Given a reference asset and an instruction such as “remove one armrest,” a retriever must find variants that make the edit and preserve the other parts. ShapeQuest’s open-source pipeline edits textured meshes through part additions, removals, and material and colour changes. It controls edit granularity across five levels, defined by the fraction of the object’s surface area each edit affects. ShapeQuest has 87,824 queries in 50 categories and a 50,919-shape gallery with competing edits. Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language foundation models have enabled open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) without task-specific training, offering new opportunities for Earth Observation (EO) applications. However, their capabilities for cloud detection remain insufficiently explored. Abstract: Real-world single-image dehazing suffers from severe domain shifts, as paired real data is scarce and models trained on synthetic haze generalize poorly to real-world haze. While pre-trained generative editing models offer powerful image priors to bridge this generalization gap, applying them to image dehazing poses two major challenges: standard generative models prioritize distribution alignment over strict pixel-level fidelity, and operating in compressed latent spaces inherently discards fine-grained structural details. In this paper, we propose a novel two-stage framework that adapts the generative prior of a few-step flow-matching backbone to real-world dehazing. In the first stage, we fine-tune the latent backbone using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), supervised not only on a flow-matching objective, but also on pixel-level supervision, thus enabling direct hazy-to-clear translation. In the second stage, we introduce structure-preserving skip connections directly to the VAE decoder, bypassing latent compression artifacts to recover precise spatial edges. Experiments on four realworld benchmarks demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms existing dehazing methods in real conditions.

[143] ICLWorld: Benchmarking In-Context Learning of Agents from Digital to Physical Worlds cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.ROPDF

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) lets agents acquire new rules from context and adapt to user-specific requirements at inference time. The same frontier agents now operate both computers and robots, yet their ICL remains insufficiently assessed: existing benchmarks are largely offline, rely on text or static images, and risk training-data contamination, while robot evaluations are limited to method-level ablations. We present ICLWorld, an online benchmark that evaluates the ICL of agents from digital to physical worlds, covering computer use and robot use, together with UIverse, a unified harness that turns every environment into a user interface. We build 30 environments, record 630 demonstration videos (10+ hours), and design 160 tasks with customized, often counter-intuitive rules that cannot be satisfied without learning from the context. Each task comes with three rule versions, given as text or video, and we test generalization at three levels, from replaying the demonstration to new instances and unseen environments. Across six frontier models, the strongest agents, Claude Opus 5.5 and GPT-6 Astra, succeed on 82% and 76% of computer-use runs but only 27% and 26% of robot-use runs, and a no-rule control reduces success to at most 8%. Frontier agents learn rules comparably well from video and from text and transfer them across environments, whereas weaker agents rely on text and lose most of their video-based success once the demonstrated situation is resampled. Abstract: Modern video object segmentation (VOS) relies on a two-stage inference paradigm: target initialization and memory-based propagation. Motivation: 现有方法普遍把零样本文本分类器视为锚点，并设计复杂机制用少量图像进行校正；作者认为这一原则更多反映 CLIP 视觉特征特性而非任务本质，目标是给出更简单且有理论解释的少样本适配方案。

Result: 在标准 11 数据集基准上，TASP 使用冻结 PE-Core-L 特征在 1-shot 达到 87.1%、16-shot 达到 91.4%，比当时 SOTA 少样本视觉-语言适配方法高 4.6 至 11.0 个百分点。在匹配特征的受控比较中，现有冻结编码器适配方法并未显著优于该探针。

Insight: 创新点在于用贝叶斯视角把文本类名解释为 kappa 个加权样本，并将类名嵌入同时用于加权训练和正则先验，从而把适配简化为凸逻辑回归。摘要还指出文本权重比融合方式更重要，自监督视觉特征与视觉-语言表示结合对较弱编码器更有益，提示少样本适配进展更依赖表示质量与合理文本监督而非复杂机制。

Abstract: Few-shot adaptation of vision-language models has produced increasingly elaborate methods built on CLIP and on one shared principle: the zero-shot text classifier is the anchor, and labeled images only correct it. We argue that this principle reflects CLIP’s visual features rather than a property of the task. We provide a simple Bayesian interpretation, in which a class name is worth exactly kappa = sigma^2 / tau^2 labeled images, the ratio between the visual spread of a class and the misalignment of its name. The Bayes classifier of this model counts text as kappa more samples. With modern frozen encoders, few-shot adaptation then reduces to a well-regularised convex probe. Specifically, we introduce TASP, a simple convex logistic regression on frozen features that incorporates class-name embeddings as weighted training examples and as a regularization prior. On the standard 11-dataset benchmark, TASP with frozen PE-Core-L features achieves 87.1% accuracy at 1 shot and 91.4% at 16 shots, outperforming state-of-the-art few-shot vision-language adaptation methods by 4.6 to 11.0 percentage points. While stronger representations explain much of this improvement, controlled comparisons on matched features show that existing frozen-encoder adaptation methods do not materially improve on this probe. We further find that the weight assigned to text matters more than how it is incorporated, and that combining self-supervised visual features with vision-language representations is most beneficial for weaker visual encoders. Together, these findings suggest that progress in few-shot vision-language adaptation depends more on representation quality and the appropriate use of text supervision than on increasingly complex adaptation mechanisms.

[146] TokenMatte: Unlocking T2V Diffusion Priors for Pixel-Native Video Matting cs.CVPDF

Zanyi Wang, Dengyang Jiang, Fengfei Yu, Shilong Liu

TL;DR: 论文提出 TokenMatte，将预训练文本到视频扩散 Transformer（DiT）适配为单次、无需辅助输入的 RGB 到 alpha 预测器，并采用像素原生输出接口。其核心是用共享线性读出将每个时空 token 直接映射为对应的 alpha 像素块，再通过分块拼接生成全分辨率 alpha 序列，从而避免 VAE 潜在空间带来的细节损失和解码开销。

Details

Motivation: 高分辨率视频抠像需要准确的连续 alpha 值，包括细发丝、软边界和运动模糊，但密集 alpha 监督稀缺。现有生成式视频抠像通常通过 RGB 训练的 VAE 潜变量预测 alpha，容易衰减细节并引入额外解码开销，因此论文希望直接利用视频扩散先验并绕过 VAE 瓶颈。

Result: 在 VideoMatte、YouTubeMatte 和 V-HIM60 上达到无需辅助输入的新 SOTA；相比匹配的 latent-output 基线，像素原生接口提升了抠像精度，并实现 1.51 倍推理加速。

Insight: 创新点在于像素原生输出接口：共享线性读出将时空 token 直接映射为局部 alpha 像素块，无需卷积解码器或学习上采样；同时真值 alpha 不进入 VAE，预测 alpha 直接在像素空间监督，省去目标侧编码和输出侧解码。可借鉴之处是显式利用 DiT token 与局部视频块的对应关系，以更轻量方式保留高分辨率细节。

Abstract: High-resolution video matting requires accurate recovery of continuous alpha values, including fine hair, soft boundaries, and motion blur, yet dense alpha supervision remains scarce. Pretrained text-to-video diffusion transformers (DiTs) offer rich spatio-temporal priors, whereas existing generative video matting approaches typically route alpha predictions through RGB-trained VAE latents, which can attenuate fine details and introduce additional decoding overhead. We present TokenMatte, which adapts a pretrained video DiT into a single-pass, auxiliary-free RGB-to-alpha predictor with a pixel-native output interface. At its core is a single shared linear readout that maps each spatio-temporal token directly to its corresponding ($t \times p \times p$) block of alpha pixels, predicting spatial details and temporal variations within the same local video block. Tiling these blocks produces the full-resolution alpha sequence without a convolutional decoder or learned upsampling. The predicted alpha is supervised directly in pixel space, while the ground-truth alpha never enters the VAE, eliminating target-side alpha encoding and output-side VAE decoding. Leveraging the pretrained video prior, TokenMatte is trained using only existing public matting datasets, without additional data curation or auxiliary inputs at inference. Compared with a matched latent-output baseline, our pixel-native interface improves matting accuracy while achieving a 1.51$\times$ inference speedup. TokenMatte establishes a new auxiliary-free state of the art on VideoMatte, YouTubeMatte, and V-HIM60.

[147] Are We Rewarding the Wrong Module? Module-Specific Policy Optimization for Multimodal Reinforcement Learning cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

HuiSeong Cho, Jun Yeong Park, Yu Rang Park

TL;DR: 论文指出基于GRPO的多模态强化学习存在模块级信用纠缠问题，即视觉编码器、投影器和LLM共用同一优势信号优化，忽视感知正确性与最终答案正确性的差异。为此提出MSPO，将感知学习信号与结果学习信号按模块分离：视觉编码器使用基于感知清单的置信度感知感知奖励及其优势更新，投影器和LLM保留基于最终结果的GRPO更新。

Details

Motivation: 现有MLLM强化学习方法通常对所有模块使用同一优势信号联合优化，导致感知模块与推理模块的信用分配纠缠，无法针对视觉感知错误和最终答案错误分别优化。

Result: 在7个数学与通用推理基准上，MSPO在3B和7B规模下平均优于现有经过强化学习调优的模型。

Insight: 创新点在于提出模块特定策略优化，将感知奖励与结果奖励解耦，并为视觉编码器设计置信度感知感知奖励CaPR，结合Beta分布后验置信度和主任务成功率缩放优势，实现更细粒度的模块级信用分配。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has been widely adopted to enhance the multimodal reasoning of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, existing methods share a common limitation of Module-Level Credit Entanglement, in which the vision encoder, projector, and LLM modules are jointly optimized using the same advantage signals even when perceptual correctness and final-answer correctness differ. To address this limitation, we propose Module-Specific Policy Optimization (MSPO), a framework that separates perceptual and outcome-based learning signals across model modules. Specifically, MSPO uses responses to perception checklists comprising auxiliary questions about visual information to derive an advantage for training the vision encoder. From these responses, MSPO computes the Confidence-aware Perception Reward (CaPR), using a perception sign obtained by thresholding checklist accuracy to determine reward direction and posterior confidence from a Beta distribution over latent perception accuracy to scale reward magnitude. We then derive the vision advantage through group-relative normalization of CaPR and scale it using main-task success rates. This advantage guides updates to the vision encoder, while the projector and LLM retain outcome-based GRPO updates. Extensive experiments on seven mathematical and general reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that MSPO outperforms existing RL-tuned models on average at both the 3B and 7B scales.

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) enable simultaneous processing of egocentric video and textual target descriptions to produce action commands for embodied tracking platforms. However, these platforms rarely move exactly as commanded, in part due to unexpected environmental disturbances such as wind, unleveled terrain, and road bumps. Here we study how such models handle uncommanded egomotion. First, we explore performance across diverse disturbances by benchmarking three tracking VLAs on three datasets. We find that models differ in which environmental disturbances hurt them most, but all are sensitive to sudden target loss. Tracing egomotion from pixels to emitted actions, we identify two gaps. In representation, motion is reliably recovered through explicit relations between frames, but aggressive compression of past frames weakens small-motion readouts. In action, large rotations do reach the decision state, yet the decoded direction carries only a few percent of a mirrored rotation’s effect on the turn. Targeting both gaps, we fine-tune one model, Uni-NaVid, on data amounting to just 1.7% of its original training set in a ladder of analysis-motivated variants. These gain 5-22 points after target-loss without degrading clean success; after a smooth loss, the best one improves success by 72% (relative), recovering 76% of its gap to clean. We also show that targeted fine-tuning improves recovery partly via a text-mediated action dependency, without new architecture, large-scale data collection or training from scratch.

Details

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D segmentation methods rely on 2D foundation models applied to registered images captured alongside the 3D point clouds. However, such images may not be available in many cases, while direct 3D point cloud segmentation methods require semantic class supervision and are challenging to deploy in a zero-shot manner. To address these issues, we present SplatSeg3D, which segments a bare colored point cloud in an open-vocabulary manner without requiring the accompanying images, camera poses, class supervision, or instance mask proposals. To achieve this, we first surfelize the input point cloud and obtain oriented 2D Gaussians. We then render views of the scene by splatting, with a view selection strategy that maximizes scene coverage with as few views as possible. Next, we apply text-prompted segmentation on the rendered views, giving each 3D point a set of multi-view semantic cues, fused to produce a 3D segmentation of the scene. Abstract: 6D pose estimation of rigid objects is a fundamental and challenging task in computer vision. Nevertheless, the discontinuous nature of binary encoding hinders effective training at high resolutions, while its inherent discrete and quantization errors lead to significant localization uncertainty. Abstract: 3D perception is shifting from task-specific pipelines to geometric foundation models: monocular depth networks, feed-forward 3D/4D reconstruction models, and vision–language models that predict geometry. Abstract: Image-text alignment serves as a foundation for modern vision-language models (VLMs), yet prevailing training paradigms provide limited supervision for learning how semantic changes correspond across modalities. [154] PhysMend: Verification-Guided Program Refinement for Physics-Aware Video Generation cs.CVPDF

Meilong Xu, Ziyi Zhao, Bhagyashree Puranik, Shayda Moezzi, Yixiao Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出 PhysMend，一个无需训练的验证引导程序精化框架，用于物理感知视频生成。它将目标事件表示为可编辑的物理程序及可调执行参数，在仿真推演和作为生成条件的深度视频上进行物理违规验证与定向修复，只把通过验证的深度控制送入视频生成器，从而在合成前纠正物理错误并降低生成成本。

Details

Motivation: 现有视频生成模型常违反物理规律；事后VLM打分难以定位需修改的对象或参数且每次修正需重新合成，而基于仿真的固定流水线会把仿真误差带入视频，缺少可验证、可修复的合成前机制。

Result: 在Physics-IQ、PhyGenBench和VBench-2.0上，PhysMend在不同生成器骨干上均取得显著提升；例如将Wan-VACE的Physics-IQ总体分数从27.33提高到39.40。

Insight: 创新点在于把物理验证和修复前移到仿真rollout与深度控制上，实现定量违规测量、失败检查定位可编辑程序字段并有界更新参数；混合验证器限制VLM仅判断事件语义、不能覆盖物理测量失败，从而抑制VLM幻觉，且无需训练、每个输入只运行一次生成器。

Abstract: Video generative models synthesize realistic scenes, yet their interactions often violate physics. A ball dropped onto sand should displace grains and settle into a crater, whereas a generated video may show it passing through the surface or leaving the sand flat. Post-hoc methods typically score generated videos with a vision-language model (VLM), but such scores offer limited guidance on which object or parameter to modify, and each correction costs another synthesis. Simulation-based pipelines instead run fixed stages before synthesis, so simulation errors reach the video. To address these limitations, we present PhysMend, a training-free framework for verification-guided program refinement. PhysMend describes the target event as an editable physical program of grounded objects, events, and required relations, paired with tunable execution parameters. Our key insight is to verify and repair the event on its simulated rollout and on the depth video that conditions the generator (depth control) rather than on generated videos, enabling quantitative violation measurement, targeted editing, and fast re-verification before synthesis. A hybrid verifier evaluates semantic event consistency, physical plausibility, solver stability, and visibility in the depth control. VLM judgments are confined to event semantics and cannot override failed physical measurements, which limits the impact of VLM hallucinations. Each failed check localizes the program fields eligible for repair, and its measured violation scales a bounded update of their parameters. Only accepted depth control reaches the generator, which runs once per input. Experiments on the commonly used benchmarks Physics-IQ, PhyGenBench, and VBench-2.0 demonstrate substantial gains across different generator backbones; for example, PhysMend raises the overall Physics-IQ score of Wan-VACE from 27.33 to 39.40.

[155] Reliable and grounded reporting of any computed tomography scan with FORA cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Sezgin Er, Utku Turkbey, Alperen Gur, Omer Faruk Durugol, Suprosanna Shit

TL;DR: 论文提出通用CT模型FORA，可识别并空间定位1028个分层放射学概念，并针对28种CT协议生成报告。模型基于30余万例常规CT及其报告进行弱监督训练，无需病灶级标注，结合多示例学习与可解释概念瓶颈连接空间证据、放射学发现和报告生成。

Details

Motivation: 现有CT人工智能系统通常局限于特定解剖区域、检查类型或预设异常，难以提供全面且具有空间定位依据的解释。论文旨在构建一个跨多种CT检查的通用模型，实现广谱异常识别、空间定位和报告生成。

Result: 在36,619例留出CT上，概念识别宏AUROC达0.918，比18个现有CT模型中最强者高0.21；在三个公开基准排名第一，并在未使用该机构数据训练的情况下超过Merlin（AUROC 0.907 vs 0.826）。在肺栓塞、肾肿块和肋骨骨折定位基准上，最强空间证据落入标注病灶的比例最高为随机水平的348倍；报告生成宏平均发现召回率超过最强现有报告生成器3倍以上。

Insight: 创新点在于用常规报告弱监督实现无需病灶级标注的空间定位，并通过可解释概念瓶颈将空间证据、放射学概念和报告生成统一起来；客观上，其价值在于验证了单一通用CT模型跨12类CT检查同时完成广谱识别、定位与报告生成的可行性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems for computed tomography (CT) remain largely restricted to specific anatomical regions, examination types or predefined abnormalities, limiting their ability to provide comprehensive and spatially grounded interpretations. Here we present FORA, a general-purpose CT model that recognizes and spatially localizes 1,028 hierarchically organized radiological concepts and generates reports across the 28 CT protocols represented in our cohort. Trained on 304,962 routine CT examinations supervised by their accompanying reports, FORA learns to localize abnormalities without manually annotated lesions. Its architecture combines multiple-instance learning with an interpretable concept bottleneck that connects spatial evidence, radiological findings and report generation. We first developed and benchmarked the architecture on chest radiographs, achieving state-of-the-art performance, before training a separate model on three-dimensional CT examinations. On 36,619 held-out CT examinations, FORA achieved a macro area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.918 for radiological concept recognition, exceeding the strongest of 18 existing CT models by 0.21. FORA also ranked first on three public benchmarks, outperforming Merlin on its own test set without training on that institution’s data (AUROC 0.907 versus 0.826). On independent localization benchmarks for pulmonary emboli, renal masses and rib fractures, FORA’s strongest spatial evidence fell within annotated lesions at rates up to 348 times greater than chance. FORA achieved more than threefold higher macro-averaged finding recall than the strongest existing report generator. These results show that a single model can combine broad abnormality recognition, spatial localization and report generation across 12 CT examination types, using routine reports without lesion-level supervision.

[156] EEG-World: Context-Conditioned Dynamics of the Watching Brain cs.CV | q-bio.NCPDF

Junxiang Zhang, Jiayu Zuo, Wenhao Ye, Junfeng Xia, Xiang Chen

Details

Motivation: 传统脑编码模型通常忽略正在进行的脑活动对个体反应的影响，论文旨在建模持续神经动态，并区分状态驱动与刺激驱动的可预测性，以提升个体EEG预测的准确性。

Result: 在2584小时EEG数据上，EEG-World的预测相关性为0.261，超过最强历史+视频线性模型的0.233，并具有3.3秒的预测视野优势；在24名未见个体中，23人经校准后优于线性基线；使用V-JEPA 2特征使片段锁定预测比CLIP与DINOv2组合特征提升35%。

Insight: 创新点在于将循环世界模型用于EEG动态预测，通过轻量级受试者特定读出实现迁移，并分离状态驱动与刺激驱动成分；客观来看，强调持续脑活动历史的主导作用，并利用视频依赖成分评估视觉表示，为神经预测和表征评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: Brain encoding models predict neural responses from audiovisual input, but typically overlook how ongoing brain activity shapes an individual’s response. We introduce EEG-World, a recurrent world model that forecasts 10 seconds of EEG band-power dynamics from 6 seconds of preceding EEG and the subsequent audiovisual stimulus. Pretrained on continuous EEG, EEG-World learns shared neural dynamics and adapts to unseen individuals through lightweight subject-specific readouts. Using 2,584 hours of EEG from 78 participants, we show that nonlinear recurrent forecasting surpasses strong history-aware linear baselines at scale. EEG-World achieves a forecasting correlation of 0.261 versus 0.233 for the strongest history-and-video linear model, with a 3.3-second forecasting-horizon advantage. Its learned dynamics transfer to unseen participants, outperforming the linear baseline in 23 of 24 individuals after calibration. While ongoing EEG history accounts for most predictive performance, controlled stimulus analyses reveal an additional video-dependent component. This component also enables evaluation of visual representations, with V-JEPA 2 improving clip-locked prediction by 35% over combined CLIP and DINOv2 features. Our results demonstrate the value of transferable neural dynamics for individual EEG forecasting and highlight the importance of separating state-driven from stimulus-driven predictability.

[157] SAVERA: Bridging Frozen Vision and Language Models via Sparse Alignment and Visual Evidence Reuse cs.CVPDF

Jiajun Shen, Chuancheng Shi, Jiayi Lyu, Yanpeng Sun, Fei Shen

TL;DR: 论文提出SAVERA框架，通过共享稀疏空间中的双向对齐和提示引导的残差融合，将冻结的视觉模型与语言模型桥接起来。其核心包括SPARE共享稀疏编码、BEAM跨模态对齐和PRIVA残差融合，在保留原始与适应后视觉信息的同时增强多模态交互。

Details

Motivation: 独立预训练的视觉与语言模型可共享底层世界结构，但组合时难以对齐跨模态特征，同时保留语言适应前后的视觉信息。论文旨在解决冻结视觉语言模型桥接中的跨模态对齐与视觉证据保留问题。

Result: 实验表明SAVERA在图像描述和视觉问答任务上取得有竞争力的性能，同时减少物体幻觉；摘要未给出具体基准数值或SOTA水平。

Insight: 创新点在于共享软门控稀疏编码表示原始视觉、适应视觉和文本特征，并在样本与神经元级进行双向跨模态对齐，再以提示引导的残差融合整合原始与适应表示。客观看，其通过稀疏对齐与视觉证据复用缓解幻觉、保留视觉信息的设计具有借鉴意义。

Abstract: Independently pretrained vision and language models may learn shared structures of the same underlying world, providing a foundation for combining them to build multimodal capabilities. However, a key challenge remains in aligning cross-modal feature representations while preserving access to visual information before and after language adaptation. To this end, we propose sparse alignment with visual evidence-guided residual adaptation (SAVERA), a framework that combines frozen vision and language models through bidirectional alignment in a shared sparse space and prompt-guided residual fusion. Specifically, we first present shared-space projection, activation reconstruction and encoding (SPARE), which represents original visual, adapted visual, and textual features through shared soft-gated sparse encoding, with branch-specific reconstruction to preserve their respective information. Second, we devise bridge–text embedding and activation matching (BEAM), a bidirectional cross-modal alignment module that aligns adapted visual and textual features at both the sample and neuron levels, establishing fine-grained cross-modal correspondence. Finally, we introduce prompt-conditioned residual integration of vision and adapted features (PRIVA), a prompt-guided residual fusion module that models vision–language interactions through gating and attention and integrates complementary information from original and adapted representations through residual fusion. Extensive experiments show that SAVERA achieves competitive performance on image captioning and visual question answering while reducing object hallucination.

[158] DOPER: Deep Object Pose Estimation Revisited cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Hugo Hadfield, Alperen Degirmenci, Jonathan Tremblay

TL;DR: DOPER 是一个仅使用 RGB 的已知物体 6D 位姿估计框架，它通过共享视觉主干联合进行物体检测、分割和关键点预测，并利用 PnP 恢复位姿。方法从合成数据训练出发，结合部分监督和标定多视角几何约束适配真实观测；推理时可选热图细化，直接将刚性位姿拟合到关键点分布。

Details

Motivation: 论文旨在解决已知物体 RGB 6D 位姿估计中合成到真实域适应、欠定 CAD 模型利用、关键点对应歧义以及依赖深度输入或学习式位姿细化器等问题。

Result: 在 HANDAL、HOPEv2、HOT3D 和 T-LESS 等单视图/多视图配置上取得检测 AP 的 SOTA，同时仅用 RGB 并在单张 GB300 上达到每物体模型 35 Hz 实时运行；在 SPEED+ 和 SHIRT 上验证了光照变化适应和时序位姿估计效果。

Abstract: We present DOPER, an RGB pose estimation framework that shares a visual backbone across object detection, segmentation, and keypoint prediction to recover the 6D pose of known objects through Perspective-n-Point. Starting from synthetic training, DOPER adapts to real observations using partial supervision while retaining synthetic keypoint labels. Calibrated multi-view observations provide geometric constraints for keypoint supervision. During inference, optional heatmap refinement fits a rigid pose directly to the predicted keypoint distributions, resolving ambiguous correspondences without depth input or a learned pose refiner. Starting from underspecified CAD models, DOPER progressively refines the object representation and pose estimator. Results on SPEED+ and SHIRT further demonstrate effective adaptation to illumination changes and accurate temporal pose estimation. Across the evaluated single-view and multi-view configurations, DOPER achieves state-of-the-art detection AP results on HANDAL, HOPEv2, HOT3D, and T-LESS while using only RGB and running in real time (35 Hz per object model on one GB300). These results demonstrate the effectiveness of jointly learning detection and pose keypoints, with real observations and geometric constraints.

TL;DR: 论文提出 CityRecast，用于抑制生成式卫星三维重建中持续出现的重影、拖影和模糊等伪影。方法通过高斯场景修复与监督校准来修正产生伪影的高斯分布，并缓解多时相观测与生成视图间的冲突。在 DFC2019 和 IARPA 上，低仰角渲染伪影减少，同时保持卫星视角保真度。

Details

Motivation: 卫星三维重建受稀疏观测和跨采集日期变化限制，现有方法借助生成先验补全视觉细节，但高斯场景中仍存在重影、拖影和模糊，且在低仰角渲染中尤为明显。

Result: 在 DFC2019 和 IARPA 数据集上，CityRecast 减少低仰角渲染伪影并保持卫星视角保真度；相比 Skyfall-GS，FID-CLIP 分别降低 25.3% 和 51.8%，MUSIQ 分别提升 14.54 和 24.95 分。

Insight: 创新点在于面向高斯场景伪影的修复与监督校准：多角度预热约束高斯空间范围以减少初始重影，表面引导替换在保留外观的同时修复分布，观测校准缓解多时相干扰，联合细化减少生成视图冲突。

Abstract: Satellite-based 3D reconstruction is limited by sparse observations and changes across acquisition dates. Existing methods use generative priors to recover missing visual detail, but ghosting, streaking, and blur persist in their Gaussian scenes. These artifacts become particularly apparent in low-elevation renderings. We introduce CityRecast to suppress these artifacts through Gaussian scene repair and supervision calibration. To correct the Gaussian distributions that produce these artifacts, we first use multi-angle warm-up to constrain their spatial extent. This reduces the initial ghosting carried into progressive refinement. Surface-guided replacement then continues to repair the Gaussian distribution while preserving existing appearance. To provide more reliable supervision for this process, observation calibration mitigates interference from multi-temporal observations. Joint refinement further reduces conflicts among generated views. Experiments on DFC2019 and IARPA demonstrate reduced artifacts in low-elevation renderings while maintaining satellite-view fidelity. Compared with Skyfall-GS, CityRecast reduces FID-CLIP by 25.3% on DFC2019 and 51.8% on IARPA. MUSIQ increases by 14.54 and 24.95 points, respectively.

[160] MemHand: Memory-Enhanced World-Space Hand Motion Reconstruction from Egocentric Videos cs.CVPDF

Junyi Ma, Zhangshuo Qi, Erhang Zhang, Mingjie Li, Hesheng Wang

Details

Motivation: 现有方法多从固定长度窗口利用时间上下文，无法保证每一帧都具备准确位姿和度量尺度所需的信息，尤其在遮挡或手移出视野时。论文旨在解决这一限制。

Result: 在 ARCTIC 和 HOT3D 等包含严重遮挡和频繁出视野的 benchmark 上达到 SOTA；相对 StableHand，将 W-MPJPE 在 ARCTIC 上降低 57%、在 HOT3D 上降低 70%，并能泛化到未见域。

Insight: 创新点在于双记忆设计：短期事件记忆围绕遮挡/出视野事件保留良好观测上下文，长期尺度记忆在有限状态中保存全视频最可靠观测作为尺度锚点；二者协同提升世界坐标系手部运动的连续性和度量精度。

Abstract: Recovering the continuous world-space motion of both hands from egocentric video is essential for applications such as human-to-robot policy transfer and augmented reality. Achieving this goes beyond reconstructing the hands from each individual frame, as the context that a frame needs is often spread over time, especially around occlusions or when a hand moves out of sight. Recent works have explored how to exploit temporal context, yet they mostly draw it from fixed-length windows. Such windows cannot guarantee that every frame has the context required to reconstruct the hands in world space with accurate poses and metric scale. To address this limitation, we introduce MemHand, a memory-enhanced framework for world-space hand motion reconstruction. MemHand maintains two complementary forms of memory. The Short-Term Event Memory keeps the context of the well-observed hand around each occlusion or out-of-sight event and supplies it to frames with weak or missing visual evidence. In addition, since the metric scale holds over the whole recording, the Long-Term Scale Memory retains the most reliable observations across the video as scale anchors within a bounded state. Across benchmarks featuring severe occlusions and frequent out-of-sight intervals, MemHand achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and generalizes well to unseen domains. Benefiting from the synergy of both memories, it cuts W-MPJPE by 57% on ARCTIC and 70% on HOT3D relative to StableHand, delivering continuous and metrically accurate world-space hand poses and trajectories. We will release our code and models at the project page: https://irmvlab.github.io/memhand.github.io

Abstract: Self-attention’s quadratic complexity constitutes a core bottleneck for Vision Transformers (ViTs). Existing efficiency-oriented optimizations, including softmax-based and linear attention (LA) variants, generally compromise model representational capacity, leading to inevitable performance degradation. We propose a novel linear attention which alleviates this efficiency-capability dilemma. For the first time, we embed an Adaptive Channel Compression and Activation Purification (ACCAP) mechanism into the QKV projection of linear attention, which enables the model to retain high-dimensional feature information while performing computations with reduced channels. We further fuse a focused kernel function and a self-gating module into ACCAP to construct a lightweight linear attention module (ACCAP-LA). Our theoretical analysis shows that, compared to standard linear attention, ACCAP-LA reduces the attention module’s parameter count by 79.17% and FLOPs by 77.5%. Existing automated approaches derive hand-engineered features from two-dimensional keypoints, which describe image-plane motion rather than the three-dimensional behavior of individual joints. We present Baby Biomechanical 3D Body (BB3D), an infant-adapted, biomechanical pipeline that estimates joint-angle trajectories from single-camera video. Against a multi-view markerless motion capture reference in 17 infants, BB3D’s median Procrustes-aligned error was 17.5mm for joint centers and 20.4mm for marker sites. A spatio-temporal transformer, pretrained on BB3D joint-angle trajectories from 1k+ single-camera recordings, and fine-tuned on 550 videos from 350 infants, predicted each individual BabyOSCAR item with a mean within-site AUC of 0.79 and the BabyOSCAR total with a Spearman correlation of 0.86 on held-out recordings. Without being trained on diagnosis, the predicted total separated infants with CP from those without CP with an AUC of 0.86, exceeding a replicated two-dimensional feature classifier (0.67) but below the expert clinician rater’s total (0.97). Predicted scores also carried information about functional ability and body distribution, though less than expert clinician scores. These results show that biomechanically grounded representations of monocular video can support scalable, joint-level assessment of selective motor control in infancy

[163] Motion Conditioned World Modeling for Embodied Visual Tracking cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Details

Insight: 创新点包括：将预测的目标运动分布和机器人自我运动纳入潜在状态转换；几何-进度模块联合建模几何路径和单步移动距离，捕获路线选择与运动进度的不确定性及其依赖；动作诱导坐标变换将预测路径表达在下一机器人帧，提供几何基础条件；未来视觉特征重建监督运动预测器，链接显式运动表示与后续观察；联合优化运动似然、特征重建和专家动作监督。客观分析：这些创新点将运动预测与视觉特征重建结合，利用几何变换增强条件预测，并通过多任务监督提升跟踪策略。

Abstract: World models support embodied decision-making by predicting future environment states. In embodied visual tracking, uncertain target motion and robot-induced viewpoint changes jointly affect visual observations, making future-state prediction challenging. We propose Motion-Conditioned World Modeling, which incorporates predicted target-motion distributions and robot ego-motion into latent state transitions. A Geometry–Progress module jointly models geometric paths and single-step travel distances through shared motion modes and path-conditioned progress distributions. This formulation captures uncertainty in route selection and motion progress while preserving their dependence. Action-induced coordinate transformations express predicted paths in the next robot frame, providing geometrically grounded conditions for future visual-state prediction. Future visual feature reconstruction supervises the motion predictor through the conditioned transition branch, linking explicit motion representations to subsequent observations. The model is jointly optimized with motion likelihood, feature reconstruction, and expert-action supervision, enabling the tracking policy to combine visual context with predictive motion cues.Experiments on three embodied tracking benchmarks demonstrate improved tracking performance and cross-environment generalization with a lightweight $0.5$B backbone.

TL;DR: 论文提出 IMPACT-Seg，用共享网络参数在 CT、CBCT 和 MRI 上分割 126 个解剖结构，通过标签协调、伪标签补全、迭代精修和 2864 例基于物理的 CBCT 仿真构建 8290 个体积数据集，并用非配对图像联合训练 2.5D 和 3D 残差编码器网络。研究发现，密集解剖分割监督无需显式特征对齐，也能促进跨模态解剖对应，并同时服务于分割和可变形配准。

Details

Motivation: CT、CBCT 和 MRI 对同一解剖结构提供互补信息，但图像外观差异大，给自适应放疗中的一致分割和可变形配准带来困难；论文希望验证密集解剖分割监督能否在不做显式特征对齐的情况下建立跨模态对应。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT), cone-beam CT (CBCT), and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) provide complementary representations of the same underlying anatomy, yet substantial differences in image appearance challenge consistent segmentation and deformable registration in adaptive radiotherapy. We investigate whether dense anatomical segmentation supervision can promote cross-modality correspondence without explicit feature alignment. We introduce IMPACT-Seg, which segments 126 structures across CT, CBCT, and MRI using shared network parameters. A collection of 8,290 volumes was assembled from public cohorts through label harmonization, model-assisted pseudo-label completion, iterative refinement, and 2,864 physics-based CBCT simulations with voxel-aligned CT labels. Unpaired images were used to jointly train 2.5D and 3D residual-encoder networks. On 139 real CBCTs, IMPACT-Seg 3D achieved a mean Dice of 0.842 versus 0.671 for the strongest comparator. On 497 voxel-aligned CT-simulated-CBCT pairs, the CT-to-CBCT Dice decrease was 0.022 versus 0.153-0.178 for competing methods. These findings show that dense anatomical segmentation supervision can support consistent anatomical representations across modalities, with applications to both segmentation and registration. Code, datasets, and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/HEMONCedric/IMPACTSeg.

Abstract: Reasoning about human-object interaction from RGB cameras is important for gaming, VR environments, and robot learning. Yet 4D reconstruction from a monocular RGB camera is challenging, especially when articulated hands are involved. Prior full-body works neglect hands during reconstruction, causing them to penetrate into, or float from the object. We present HARI4D, a hand-articulation-aware method for reconstructing spatially and temporally consistent 4D human-object interaction from monocular RGB video. We introduce a history-aware object tracker that provides robust and stable tracking. Based on these tracks, we improve the interaction configuration through coarse-to-fine reasoning that follows the order of object manipulation: ReachNet first brings the full body and arms to the object during contact, and TouchNet then finalizes hand articulation according to temporal context and local object geometry. [166] Efficient Video Retrieval Augmented Generation via Token Allocation cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.LG | eess.IVPDF

Ayush Barik, Fan Lai, Francis Y. Yan

TL;DR: 论文提出一种即插即用的视频检索增强生成框架 Lembas，将长视频 RAG 建模为在帧与字幕之间、在共享 token 预算下的 token 分配问题。该方法利用 token 边际价值递减和相似证据重复惩罚，通过带收敛保证的迭代注水算法优化分配，并支持多轮对话中复用已处理 token。

Details

Motivation: 现有长视频 RAG 主要依赖帧选择和 token 压缩，却忽略了每个额外 token 的实际价值，导致在有限计算预算下难以高效定位关键证据并减少 VLM 处理开销。

Result: 在可比计算量下，Lembas 相比 SOTA 基线最高提升 7.2 个百分点准确率；在多轮视频理解中，以 1.7–17.3 倍更少 TFLOPs 实现最高 1.5 个百分点准确率提升。

Insight: 创新点在于将视频 RAG 统一为帧和字幕的 token 预算分配问题，并同时建模单个条目内 token 价值递减与相似证据重复导致的进一步贬值；此外，多轮场景可复用历史 token 并利用 VLM 注意力无额外成本地精炼相关性。

Abstract: Efficient long-video understanding requires identifying relevant evidence while minimizing the tokens processed by vision-language models (VLMs). Existing video retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods rely on frame selection and token compression, but overlook how much each additional token is worth. We introduce Lembas, a plug-and-play framework that formulates video RAG as a token allocation problem over frames and subtitles under a shared budget. Our key insight is that the value of a token diminishes within each item and decreases further as similar evidence receives more tokens. Lembas captures both effects in a single objective and solves it with an efficient iterative water-filling algorithm that has a provable convergence guarantee. For multi-turn conversations, Lembas reuses the tokens processed in earlier turns and refines relevance with the VLM’s attention at no extra cost. Our evaluations show that Lembas improves accuracy by up to 7.2 percentage points over state-of-the-art baselines at comparable compute. On multi-turn video understanding, Lembas achieves up to 1.5 percentage points higher accuracy with 1.7-17.3x fewer TFLOPs.

[167] Do Vision-Language Models Know Their Own Visual Weaknesses? Probing Capability-Level Metacognition cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Dohoon Kim, Taesup Moon

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）是否具备能力级元认知，即能否预先知道自己在大范围视觉任务类型和图像条件下的弱点。作者在多种开源与专有模型上测试，并区分所有模型共有的困难与模型自身特有弱点，发现VLM预先普遍不清楚自身视觉弱点，甚至误判整体准确率和退化图像影响。进一步训练模型预测自身准确率只能教会其在不同任务类型上的准确率水平，却不能让其知道哪些类型困难；但模型开始解题后，其答案概率会反映模型特有的弱点。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注VLM是否不仅能判断单个答案对错，还能预先知道自己擅长或薄弱的任务类型与图像条件。由于部分任务对所有模型都困难，研究动机还包括将共享难度与模型自身特有弱点分离，以更准确地评估VLM的元认知能力。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值或benchmark结果，但报告VLM普遍预先几乎不知道自身视觉弱点，会误判整体准确率，甚至不清楚哪些任务对所有模型都难，多数模型也误判退化图像对自身的影响。训练VLM预测自身准确率可在训练未见任务类型上教会其准确率水平，但不能教会其困难类型；模型开始解题后，在少量无标签问题上，其答案概率可反映模型特有弱点。

Insight: 创新点在于提出能力级元认知评估框架，将“所有模型共有的困难”与“模型自身特有弱点”分离，从而更客观地检验模型是否真正了解自己。另一个可借鉴之处是将模型对自身答案的置信度/答案概率作为潜在弱点信号，提示可通过解题过程中的概率信息而非仅靠显式预测来学习或探测VLM自身弱点。

Abstract: Metacognition includes knowing one’s own strengths and weaknesses across kinds of tasks, not only judging whether a single answer is correct. We test whether vision-language models (VLMs) know in advance which kinds of visual tasks and image conditions are hard for them, across a wide range of open and proprietary models. Because some tasks are hard for every model, we separate what a model knows about this shared difficulty from what it knows about itself. We find that VLMs know little in advance about their visual weaknesses. They misjudge their overall accuracy and do not even know which kinds of tasks are hard for every model, and most misjudge how degraded images affect them. We further train VLMs to predict their own accuracy, which teaches them their own level of accuracy on kinds unseen in training but not which kinds are hard for them. However, information about these weaknesses appears once a model starts solving. On a few unlabeled questions of a kind, the probability a model assigns to its own answers follows weaknesses particular to the model, across kinds of tasks and image conditions. VLMs thus hold information about their own weaknesses that their stated predictions do not yet reflect. By separating what is particular to a model from what holds for every model, our evaluation offers a test for methods that aim to teach VLMs their own weaknesses, and the answer probability offers them a signal to start from.
