Our approach makes limited instrumented measurements useful for interpreting human interactions through learned physical prototypes as a prior. The prototypes share learned force levels and temporal profiles across interactions of comparable effort, with visual and semantic cues determining their contribution to each observation. Geometric and vision-language features adapt this prior to the observed interaction, while a joint conditional flow refines an initial tracked trajectory together with the force profile. Paired human and instrumented-gripper recordings provide measured force references for supervising human video, and a separately trained torque model demonstrates that the formulation extends to other sensory quantities. We evaluate trajectory recovery and force estimation through baseline comparisons and controlled ablations on held-out interactions from the Hoi! dataset. On this evaluation subset, VidForce achieves 6.6 cm ATE, peak-force MAE and RMSE of 4.81 N and 5.92 N, respectively, and force-profile RMSE of 5.52 N. On real-world videos, VidForce infers motion and force reliably and consistently despite the variability in human execution. We further deploy VidForce on a Spot robot, where trajectories and force profiles estimated from human demonstrations drive real-world articulated-object manipulation, and report success rates across repeated trials. Project page: https://rafaymhddn.github.io/vidforce

[171] Teaching Time Without Losing General Retrieval: Adapting Multimodal Embeddings for Time-Sensitive Video Retrieval cs.CVPDF

Toya Oyama, Rainer Lienhart, Shin’ichi Satoh

TL;DR: 论文研究文本到视频检索中多模态嵌入模型在时间敏感检索（描述动作及其方向）上的失效问题，指出现有的TARA微调方法虽提升时间敏感检索却严重损害通用检索。作者提出三个简单组件：keep loss（约束输出嵌入不偏离基座模型）、pair loss（按两个文本嵌入的差异方向，拉大视频片段与其方向反转副本的视频嵌入间距）、middle-layer LoRA（仅在融合帧的中层训练LoRA），从而用单个模型、每个片段单个向量同时取得强通用检索与强时间敏感检索。

Details

Motivation: 基于MLLM的嵌入模型在场景描述式的通用检索上表现好，但在动作方向式的时间敏感检索上表现差；而针对后者微调（如TARA）会导致通用检索能力大幅下降（R@1 58.4→44.8），且其发布模型需与基座模型集成才能保住通用能力。论文希望在单一模型、单向量表示下同时兼顾两类检索。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B上，通用检索R@1保持58.4不降，画廊含方向反转副本时的时间敏感R@1从55.5升至63.2；增益可迁移到未见过的benchmark和InternVL2-8B-RA。keep loss作为通用补救用于TARA配方可将通用R@1从44.8恢复到57.8；与TARA集成后在RTime和未见CiA上取得对比模型中的最高分。

Insight: 创新点在于三点经验发现：通用检索能力取决于输出嵌入相对基座模型的漂移程度而非权重变化幅度，故用keep loss直接约束嵌入空间；文本嵌入能区分正反动作方向而视频嵌入不能，故用文本差异方向引导视频嵌入做对称性破除；时间信息集中在中层及紧随其后的层，故仅对该部分训练LoRA（仅0.15%参数即可接近全参微调效果）。这些发现将“灾难性遗忘”问题转化为嵌入漂移与层级选择问题，具有较好的可迁移性。

Abstract: In text-to-video retrieval, MLLM-based embedding models do well when the text describes the scene (general retrieval), but poorly when it describes actions and their direction (time-sensitive retrieval). TARA, a pioneering work, fine-tunes such a model on text triplets with temporal hard negatives; it improves time-sensitive retrieval but lowers general retrieval (mean R@1 over six benchmarks in our rerun on Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B: 58.4->44.8), and its released model keeps general retrieval only when ensembled with Qwen3-VL-Embedding. Our goal is strong general and time-sensitive retrieval with one model and one vector per clip. Three findings lead to three simple components. Keep loss: general retrieval follows how far the output embeddings drift from the base model, not how far the weights move; we therefore keep them near the base model, which keeps most general retrieval even without general-domain data. Pair loss: the text embeddings of an action description and its reversed-direction version are well separated, but the video embeddings of a clip and its reversed copy are close; we therefore widen the gap between the video embeddings of each clip and its reversed copy, in the direction of the difference between their two text embeddings. Middle-layer LoRA: frames are combined in the middle layers, and temporal information peaks in or just after them; we therefore train LoRA only on these layers, which nearly matches training all weights on most time-sensitive benchmarks with 0.15% of the parameters and keeps general retrieval better. General R@1 stays at 58.4, while R@1 with each clip’s reversed copy in the gallery rises from 55.5 to 63.2; the gains carry over to unseen benchmarks and InternVL2-8B-RA. The keep loss is also a remedy for the TARA recipe (general R@1: 44.8->57.8), and ensembling TARA with Ours gives the highest scores on RTime and unseen CiA among the compared models.

[172] When Repair Accuracy Misleads: Training a VLM on Its Own Failures cs.CV | cs.CLPDF

Ang Qi, Yue Wang

TL;DR: 论文研究”用VLM自身的失败样本微调来修复模型”这一范式，指出以held-out失败集上的准确率衡量成功具有误导性：人工审计发现100个失败中37个是标签错误，且失败样本存在措辞规律（仅看问题的分类器在73.2%的失败对上两个答案都能答对）。作者提出VFR（验证式失败驱动修复）：按类型挖掘最小对失败、由三个LLM裁判在不看存储标签的情况下作答来验证、采用答案优先的纠正目标、并用带replay的LoRA微调。VFR在held-out失败上修复率达82.8%（基线10.0%），在独立标注的无条件对上整体准确率由87.4%升至92.4%，但同时在否定、双图关系和真实图像上暴露出显著退化。

Details

Motivation: 直接用模型自身失败样本微调是修复VLM最直接的手段，但常用的held-out失败准确率会被标注噪声和措辞捷径污染，无法反映真实修复效果。论文旨在揭示这两类评估陷阱，并给出更可靠的失败驱动修复流程与评估视角。

Result: VFR修复82.8%的held-out失败（基线10.0%）；在独立标注的无条件对上整体准确率87.4%→92.4%，空间关系66.7%→96.8%。但否定类无条件对从88.7%降至79.7%，双图关系74.3%→49.6%，What’sUp真实照片的yes/no对84.5%→26.4%；去掉验证环节则降至80.8%（低于基线87.4%）。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新在于用最小对失败挖掘、多LLM裁判盲标验证、答案优先目标和replay微调来抑制捷径，并量化了”修复准确率”被标签错误与措辞规律双重污染的陷阱。客观来看，其最具价值的贡献是系统性揭示失败驱动修复的副作用，即捷径学习、任务间负迁移和分布外退化，提示该领域需要多维度、去捷径的评估体系。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a vision-language model on its own failures is a direct way to repair it, but accuracy on held-out failures, the obvious measure of success, misleads. Many mined failures are not model errors: a human audit of 100 failures of Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct on questions generated from COCO and GQA found label errors in 37. And mined failures share wording regularities: question-only classifiers get both answers right on 73.2% of held-out failure pairs. We study both traps with verified failure-driven repair (VFR): typed minimal-pair failure mining, verification by three LLM judges that answer without seeing the stored labels, answer-first corrective targets, and LoRA fine-tuning with replay. VFR repairs 82.8% of held-out failures (base model: 10.0%), and on independently labeled, unconditioned pairs it raises overall accuracy from 87.4% to 92.4% through spatial relations, whose accuracy rises from 66.7% to 96.8% even where the wording points to the wrong answer. Yet on negation, of which it repairs 88.9% of held-out failures, its accuracy on unconditioned pairs falls from 88.7% to 79.7%: it breaks negations about absent objects, rare in its training set. Without verification, accuracy on unconditioned pairs falls to 80.8%, below the base model’s 87.4%, through a negation shortcut our development benchmarks miss; without failure selection, it stays at 92.0% (one seed) while repair falls to 73.4%. Training also lowers two-image relation accuracy (74.3% to 49.6%; replay data alone: 48.4%); multi-image replay partly and unevenly reverses this. Paraphrasing the spatial questions keeps most of the gain (87.3% vs. the base model’s 62.0%), though rewording lowers spatial repair on held-out failures from 92.9% to 76.8%. On What’sUp’s independent photos of objects under others, caption choice stays perfect while accuracy on our yes/no pairs falls from 84.5% to 26.4%.

Abstract: Structured clinical decisions from volumetric computed tomography (CT) require anatomically grounded predictions and calibrated confidence estimates. However, existing 3D medical multimodal models often suffer from uncalibrated autoregressive decoding and shortcut reliance on irrelevant anatomical regions. We introduce Radev, a decision-native multimodal model that integrates anatomy-guided visual compression, direct candidate prediction, and counterfactual confidence supervision. To bridge global context with fine-grained slice details, a frozen 3D encoder extracts reusable organ-level features, which are dynamically selected by the query to attend to 2D slice features within predicted anatomical bounds. The resulting compact visual tokens enable the language backbone to estimate candidate probabilities in a single forward pass without autoregressive decoding. We further propose Counterfactual-Calibrated Training (CCT) to compare predictions under intact and intervened organ representations. CCT encourages confidence to decrease when removing target-organ evidence degrades decision quality, while penalizing excessive confidence drops under unrelated-organ removal. To facilitate rigorous evaluation, we construct CT-Verdict, a comprehensive benchmark spanning binary finding presence, multiple-choice questions, and ordinal severity assessment across both in-distribution and external cohorts. Abstract: Egocentric video question answering enables blind and low-vision (BLV) users to query their immediate surroundings through natural language. However, existing benchmarks and models primarily optimize for semantic agreement with reference answers, reducing visual assistance to a purely factual matching task. In real-world scenarios, a factually correct response remains practically unhelpful if it lacks the spatial orientation, actionable detail, or conciseness required by the immediate task of the user. Drawing upon real BLV egocentric videos and authentic user queries from EgoBlind, we construct the PA-EgoQA, a preference-aligned dataset that reconstructs reference answers according to empirical BLV communication criteria. Based on this dataset, we introduce Preference-Aligned Visual Egocentric Learning (PAVE), a unified learning and inference framework that couples structured answer-policy fine-tuning with deterministic question-conditioned routing to adapt response formulation directly to the surface form of the query at inference time. TL;DR: 论文针对状态空间模型中的视觉token压缩问题，提出ProbConv：在独立保留决策下精确计算保留位置激活前的期望卷积，并保持对保留概率可微。二值化后可与压缩Mamba块保持一致，从而让训练信号与推理时的压缩结构匹配。方法在图像、视觉语言和视频骨干网络中验证，强压缩条件下优势更明显。

Abstract: Visual token compaction reduces inference cost by passing only selected image patches or video tokens to later layers. In sequence models with local convolutions, each removal changes which tokens are neighbors. Learning which tokens to keep therefore requires a training signal that accounts for the new neighborhoods. We introduce ProbConv, which computes the exact expected convolution before activation at a kept position under independent keep decisions. The operator is differentiable in the keep probabilities. With binary decisions, ProbConv and step gating reproduce the compacted Mamba block. Across image, vision–language and video backbones, ProbConv is strongest under strong compaction: its advantage over matched training controls grows as more tokens are removed. Abstract: Can a simple mirror expose the limitations of today’s robotic manipulation policies? Inspired by the mirror self-recognition test in animal cognition, we investigate whether robot policies can reliably manipulate physical objects in scenes containing their reflections. Abstract: Physics-based soccer characters must perform diverse motor skills and transition smoothly between them, such as dribbling into a shot. However, skills that perform well in isolation may fail when executed sequentially, as preceding behaviors can leave the character and ball in unfamiliar states. We present MESSI (Multi-Expert Soccer Skill Integration), a framework that enables multiple soccer skill transitions through shared adaptation while preserving the original skill policy outside transitions. MESSI first trains eight task-competent experts using motion priors and task rewards, then distills them into a unified, skill-conditioned Transformer. Rather than retraining individual experts for different transitions, we train a shared, context-conditioned low-rank adapter on the frozen student through online reinforcement learning. Activated upon skill switching, the adapter helps the target skill handle unfamiliar entry states, while a learned exit mechanism determines when to return control to the original student. [178] Training Lightweight Gaze Models without Target Gaze Annotation: What Makes a Good Teacher? cs.CVPDF

Scott J. Lewis, Di Fu

TL;DR: 论文研究在视线目标估计（gaze target estimation）任务中，用模型生成标签训练轻量视觉模型时「什么样的教师模型才算好老师」，以及能否在学生训练前预判。作者固定学生模型、训练协议和36.1万训练样本，仅改变视线标签来源，在3个视频数据集、2种学生规模、3个随机种子上对比11种监督条件，并统一以人工标注评估。

Details

Motivation: 交互机器人需要既准确又高效的视线估计模型，但人工标注成本高昂；用模型生成标签是可扩展的替代方案，然而如何判断教师模型质量、能否在训练学生之前就识别出好教师，此前缺乏系统研究。

Result: 未在目标数据集标注上训练过的Gaze-LLE所教学生在ViT-Tiny上与人工标注训练的学生持平，在ViT-Small上显著更优（平均距离误差0.187 vs 0.196），且ViT-Tiny学生在笔记本CPU上比教师快32倍；验证集标签误差可预测学生测试误差（Spearman ρ=0.91），仅10个标注片段在95%的抽样中能挑出两个专家教师。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新在于：通过严格受控实验证明不完美的模型生成标签可替代人工标注、揭示表面能力会掩盖不可靠监督（如InternVL3/Qwen3-VL在提示给出他人脸部中心时以93.4%/81.1%比例直接复制坐标的捷径）、并提出训练前即可通过验证标签误差和小量标注样本筛选教师。客观来看，其价值在于给出一套可操作的监督信号评估与教师选择准则，并显式暴露VLM作标注器时的捷径行为，对「模型标注」类研究具有方法论借鉴意义。

Abstract: Model-generated labels offer a scalable route to training lightweight vision models, but what makes a model a good teacher, and can we tell before training a student? We study this in gaze target estimation, where interactive robots need models that are both accurate and efficient. In a controlled comparison of eleven supervision conditions, we vary only the source of gaze-point labels, keeping the student, training protocol and 361k training samples fixed, across three video datasets, two student sizes and three seeds; every student is evaluated against human annotation. Three findings emerge. First, imperfect model-generated labels can replace human annotation: Gaze-LLE, never trained on the target datasets’ gaze annotation, trains students that match annotation-trained ones on ViT-Tiny and are significantly more accurate on ViT-Small (average distance error 0.187 vs 0.196), and the ViT-Tiny student runs 32$\times$ faster than its teacher on a laptop CPU. Second, apparent competence can conceal unreliable supervision: given another person’s face centre in the prompt, InternVL3 and Qwen3-VL return it on 93.4% and 81.1% of test samples, and a copy-the-coordinate baseline matches InternVL3’s labels and students. Without that coordinate, both models fall far behind the specialist. Third, good teachers can be identified before training: validation label error ranks student test error (Spearman’s $ρ= 0.91$), and ten annotated clips single out the two specialist teachers in 95% of draws. These findings give practical principles for assessing supervision, exposing shortcuts and selecting teachers for lightweight models.

[179] RePix-VFI: Re-rendering Observed Pixels for Video Frame Interpolation with Generative Priors from One-Step Diffusion cs.CVPDF

Dayeon Kim, Jeonghyeok Do, Munchurl Kim

TL;DR: 论文提出 RePix-VFI，一个多帧视频插帧框架，将基于草稿条件的一步视频扩散与来自观测关键帧的像素重渲染相结合。预训练视频扩散模型单次前向精修整个窗口的流渲染草稿；随后通过生成式回读建立目标帧与前后关键帧的对应关系，在 VAE 外重渲染原始关键帧 RGB 像素，并由轻量外观融合头融合生成外观。

Details

Motivation: 多帧视频插帧在时间间隔增大时，流方法能保留观测纹理但易在模糊运动下错位，迭代潜扩散能合成合理内容但采样成本高且 VAE 重建可能改变细节。论文希望兼顾准确中间运动、忠实外观和高效生成。

Result: 在 SNU-FILM ×8 和 ×16 上，RePix-VFI 取得最高 PSNR 及最低 LPIPS、FloLPIPS、tOF，PSNR 分别超过最佳扩散基线 1.71dB 和 1.48dB；相比最先进多帧 VFI 扩散模型运行速度快 32–42 倍。

Insight: 创新点在于用一步视频扩散精修流渲染草稿，并通过生成式回读将原始关键帧像素在 VAE 外重渲染到目标帧，从而保留观测纹理、减少迭代采样成本并缓解 VAE 细节损失。

Abstract: Multi-frame video frame interpolation (VFI) aims to reconstruct dense video from sparse keyframes, requiring accurate intermediate motion and faithful appearance. As temporal gaps grow, flow-based methods can retain observed textures but misplace them under ambiguous motion, whereas iterative latent-diffusion models can synthesize plausible content but incur substantial sampling costs and may alter details during reconstruction with a variational autoencoder (VAE). We present RePix-VFI, named for Re-rendering observed Pixels, a multi-frame VFI framework that couples draft-conditioned one-step video diffusion with reconstruction from observed keyframes. A pretrained video diffusion model refines a flow-rendered draft of the whole window in a single forward pass. For each target frame, our generative readback matches its two bounding keyframes to the generated prediction using an optical-flow estimator. The resulting correspondences guide re-rendering of original keyframe RGB pixels outside the VAE. A lightweight appearance fusion head combines these renderings with generated appearance. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate significant improvements in reconstruction and perceptual quality. On SNU-FILM at $\times$8 and $\times$16, RePix-VFI achieves the highest PSNR and the lowest LPIPS, FloLPIPS, and tOF among the compared methods, exceeding the best evaluated diffusion-based baseline by 1.71 and 1.48dB in PSNR, respectively. It also runs 32–42$\times$ faster than the state-of-the-art multi-frame VFI diffusion model.

[180] E-MAPS: Event-Based Promptable Video Segmentation with Memory-as-Prompt cs.CVPDF

Adonay Demewez Gebremedhin, Mohamad Alansari, Wessam Shehieb, Muzammal Naseer, Hasan AlMarzouqi

TL;DR: 论文提出基于事件相机的可提示视频分割任务ePVS，并设计E-MAPS框架，将预训练可提示分割器迁移到事件流，通过事件到Token对齐和记忆感知提示实现任意窗口的初始化、纠正与传播。作者构建了大规模RGB-事件对齐数据集EViM及三个训练不相交基准，在提示一次和交互式ePVS上取得领先，并展现零样本目标跟踪能力。

Details

Motivation: RGB视频对象分割在快速运动与不良光照下容易失效；事件相机虽能应对模糊和曝光挑战，但慢运动时信息不足。现有可提示事件方法仅处理单一窗口，缺乏跨窗口传播、纠正和对象引入能力。

Result: 在三个训练不相交的ePVS基准上，E-MAPS在prompt-once和交互式设置下均领先，且更高效；同时在零样本目标跟踪基准上表现强劲。摘要未给出具体数值。

Insight: 创新点包括形式化事件可提示视频分割ePVS；提出E2T将循环事件特征与预训练表示对齐并保留提示条件先验；提出MAP将对象条件历史转为稀疏提示，使用户输入与记忆共享解码路径；构建EViM大规模对齐数据与基准。客观看，其核心贡献在于事件视频中的跨窗口可提示分割设定、记忆作为提示的机制以及跨模态预训练表示迁移。

Abstract: RGB-based video object segmentation (VOS) remains brittle under fast motion and adverse illumination. Event cameras preserve signal under blur and challenging exposure, but provide little evidence when motion slows. Interaction can restore object state for subsequent propagation, yet promptable event methods merely address single windows. We formalize event-based Promptable Video Segmentation (ePVS), where prompts at arbitrary windows initialize, correct, or introduce objects and predictions. We present Event Memory-As-Prompt Segmentation (E-MAPS), which transfers a pretrained promptable segmenter to event streams and learns propagation and correction. Event-to-Token (E2T) aligns recurrent event features with pretrained representations while preserving prompt-conditioning priors. Memory-Aware Prompting (MAP) converts object-conditioned history into sparse prompts, allowing memory and user inputs to share one decoder pathway efficiently. We construct Event-View Masklets (EViM) from 206K aligned RGB-event pairs and 620K masks for two-stage representation transfer and native event-video learning, and establish three training-disjoint benchmarks. E-MAPS leads prompt-once and interactive ePVS across all three benchmarks while being more efficient. It further demonstrates strong performance on zero-shot object tracking benchmarks. Project page: https://github.com/adonaydem/emaps-wacv

[181] GRAPE-Bench: When Vision Degrades, Does Video Reasoning Hold? cs.CVPDF

Ce Zhang, Junyi He, Tianshi Ming, Jinxi He, Jianshu Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出GRAPE-Bench，一个联合评估视频推理模型在视觉退化与问题难度下表现的基准。它结合合成退化数据（12种损坏类型，涵盖日常生活和驾驶视频）与真实夜间/雨雪雾驾驶视频，每段视频配三个难度递增的问题。实验发现合成与真实条件下的模型排名反转，以及整体准确率掩盖的难度层级弱点，并验证了微调和偏好引导可提升或控制退化条件下的视频推理。

Details

Motivation: 现有视频推理基准多通过模拟退化或高难度问题来评估模型鲁棒性，但未充分考察模型对自然退化视频的处理能力，以及安全驾驶场景中更简单但关键的判断能力。论文旨在填补这一空白，统一评估视觉退化与问题难度对视频推理的影响。

Result: 在开放权重和前沿模型上，合成与真实退化条件间出现排名反转，且整体准确率掩盖了不同难度层级的弱点。合成数据微调将Qwen3.5-9B的真实场景准确率从76.7%提升至81.1%，clean-privileged在线策略蒸馏在另一推理设置下达到80.4%；偏好引导可在整体准确率几乎不变的情况下连续控制冻结模型在安全相关判断与高难推理间的权衡。

Insight: 创新点在于联合建模视觉退化类型与问题难度，并融合合成退化数据与真实恶劣天气驾驶视频构建基准；揭示了合成与真实条件间的排名反转及难度层级弱点。客观看，其可借鉴之处包括分级问题设计、真实退化场景覆盖，以及通过微调和偏好引导实现退化条件下推理性能提升与安全-推理权衡控制。

Abstract: Despite strong performance on standard benchmarks, video reasoning models remain vulnerable to visual degradation, raising concerns about their reliability in safety-critical scenarios. Existing benchmarks address this concern in part by simulating visual degradation or probing model reasoning with challenging questions, leaving open how well models handle naturally degraded video and whether they can make the simpler judgments that matter for safe driving. To this end, we introduce GRAPE-Bench, which jointly evaluates video reasoning across visual degradation conditions and question difficulty. It combines a synthetic set spanning twelve corruption types across daily-life and driving videos with real-world footage of driving at night or in rain, snow, or fog. Each video is paired with three progressively challenging questions, from fundamental judgments with potential safety implications to demanding reasoning stress tests. Across open-weight and frontier models, we observe ranking reversals between synthetic and real-world conditions and level-specific weaknesses obscured by overall accuracy. Synthetic-data fine-tuning improves Qwen3.5-9B’s real-world accuracy from 76.7% to 81.1%, while clean-privileged on-policy distillation reaches 80.4% in a separate reasoning-enabled setting. Through preference steering, we further demonstrate continuous control over a frozen model’s trade-off between safety-relevant judgments and challenging reasoning, with little change in overall accuracy. Together, GRAPE-Bench provides a unified testbed for evaluating, improving, and controlling video reasoning under degraded visual conditions.

[182] SHARPnP: Self-Supervised and Plug-and-Play Hyperspectral Super-Resolution from Real Noisy Unaligned Camera Measurements cs.CV | eess.IV | eess.SPPDF

Mohamad Jouni, Brayan Monroy, Jorge Bacca, Mauro Dalla Mura

TL;DR: SHARPnP 提出一种面向真实噪声、未对齐相机测量的 RGB 引导高光谱超分辨率框架，将配准与重建耦合，无需干净高分辨率参考。方法通过跨视角损失适配预训练配准模型，并借助固定即插即用融合模块与无参考自监督进行训练，测试时还校准跨相机辐射并细化对齐。实验在 REAL benchmark 和多倍率及噪声条件下验证了优于监督融合基线的性能，并能从原生 RGB-HSI 快照重建 51 个 VIS-NIR 波段。

Details

Motivation: 低成本快照高光谱相机空间分辨率低且噪声大，RGB 相机可提供细节但存在跨相机空间变化错位；现有监督融合方法依赖大规模干净高分辨率高光谱数据，对噪声敏感且跨传感器泛化差。论文旨在从真实噪声、未对齐测量中实现无需干净参考的 RGB 引导高光谱超分辨率。

Result: 在 REAL benchmark 上，SHARPnP 在 4×、8×、16× 干净输入下优于监督融合基线，并在 4× 且高斯噪声 σ=5/255、10/255 时仍优于基线。在原生 RGB-HSI 快照测量上，方法无需高分辨率参考即可重建全部 51 个 VIS-NIR 波段，超过传统 31 波段 VIS 设置。

Insight: 创新点在于将预训练配准模型与固定即插即用融合模块耦合，通过跨视角损失反传实现配准适配，并利用时间与重新损坏配对在噪声输入上做无参考自监督；测试时辐射校准与对齐细化增强了真实场景鲁棒性。客观上看，其可解释、模块化和免干净参考的设计对真实跨相机高光谱融合具有借鉴价值。

Abstract: Affordable snapshot hyperspectral cameras capture spectral images in a single exposure but suffer from limited spatial resolution and sensor noise. Low-cost RGB cameras provide fine visible details to guide NIR reconstruction, but introduce spatially varying cross-camera misalignment. Supervised fusion methods require relatively large datasets of clean, high-resolution hyperspectral images, are sensitive to sensor noise, and generalize poorly across new sensors. We introduce SHARPnP, an interpretable, flexible framework coupling registration and reconstruction for RGB-guided hyperspectral super-resolution from noisy, unaligned measurements without clean references. We adapt a pretrained registration model via a cross-view loss backpropagated through a fixed plug-and-play fusion module. Temporal and recorrupted pairs enable reference-free self-supervision on noisy inputs. At test time, we calibrate cross-camera radiometry and refine alignment. Plug-and-play fusion employs sequential three-band denoising. On the REAL benchmark, SHARPnP outperforms supervised fusion baselines at $4\times$, $8\times$, and $16\times$ on clean inputs and at $4\times$ under Gaussian noise ($σ=5/255, 10/255$). On native RGB-HSI snapshot measurements, it reconstructs all 51 VIS-NIR bands, beyond the conventional 31-band VIS setting, without high-resolution references.

[183] Linear Probes Can Guide: Training-Free Control of Representation Generators cs.CVPDF

Pengwei Wang, José Morano, Botond Fazekas, Hrvoje Bogunović

TL;DR: 论文研究表征空间生成模型（如RAEs）中的免训练语义控制，发现预训练视觉基础模型的表征空间具有良好线性可读性，并据此提出Semantic Mean-Space视角：对理想去噪器预测施加线性探针可得到任意噪声水平下探针分数的精确后验均值。基于此提出LiSM统一框架，将语义引导转化为贝叶斯线性逆问题，并设计Fit-Path求解器实现全局与局部控制。

Details

Motivation: 现有表征空间生成模型主要利用视觉基础模型加速训练和提升样本保真度，但后验语义引导通常仍依赖训练时条件化或额外控制机制。论文希望利用VFM表征空间高度组织化、线性可读的特点，实现无需训练的语义引导。

Result: 论文在类别、属性、前景和关键点四类控制设置上验证LiSM及Fit-Path，结果表明其能提供有效且轻量的全局与局部控制，并优于噪声感知引导和像素空间引导方法；摘要未给出具体定量指标或SOTA声明。

Insight: 创新点在于观察到线性探针作用于理想去噪器预测时可由期望线性性得到精确后验均值，从而把语义引导形式化为贝叶斯线性逆问题，绕过训练时条件化；并统一刻画不同求解器如何估计探针空间不确定性及传播校正，提出兼顾全局和局部控制的Fit-Path。客观上看，核心可借鉴之处是将生成模型后验引导与线性探针/贝叶斯逆问题结合，实现免训练、轻量且可解释的控制。

Abstract: Representation-space generative models, such as Representation Autoencoders (RAEs), leverage pretrained vision foundation models (VFMs) primarily to accelerate training and improve sample fidelity. Yet VFMs are more than reconstruction tokenizers, as they feature rich representation spaces that are highly organized and linearly readable. We observe that this removes a key obstacle of post-hoc guidance: by linearity of expectation, a clean probe applied to an ideal denoiser’s prediction yields the exact posterior mean of the probe scores at any noise level. This regime, which we call Semantic Mean-Space, turns semantic guidance into a Bayesian linear inverse problem, bypassing the need for training-time conditioning. With this foundation, we propose LiSM, a unified framework for Linear Guidance in Semantic Mean-Space. To validate the framework, we developed and adapted different solvers, and characterized them based on how they estimate probe-space uncertainty and propagate the correction through the representation. In addition, we propose Fit-Path, a solver designed to achieve strong global and local control. Our results in four control setups (class, attributes, foreground, and keypoints) show that LiSM methods, and Fit-Path in particular, provide effective, lightweight control across global and local tasks, outperforming noise-aware and pixel-space guidance.

[184] Spectrify: Framework for Hyperspectral Image Generation Using Computer Graphics cs.CV | cs.GRPDF

Yasin Mamedov, Daniil Vladimirov, Sofiia Dorogova, Bakhtiyar Khalfin, Egor Ershov

TL;DR: 论文提出 Spectrify 框架，将 3D 场景数据集转换为高光谱图像，并提供按材质标注的反射光谱库。该方法让光谱跟随材质，为物体、光源和天空分配物理合理的光谱，并使用 Mitsuba 3 逐波长路径追踪完整光传输。实验表明合成高光谱图像可用于高光谱重建和颜色恒常性等任务训练。

Details

Motivation: 真实高光谱图像采集慢且昂贵，最大数据集仅约 1000 张；低层视觉任务需要物理正确的光谱，而现有光谱渲染覆盖场景少且成像模型较简化。

Result: 仅用 Spectrify 合成高光谱图像训练的高光谱重建网络，在多数测试集上比用最大真实高光谱数据集训练的网络更好地泛化到未见真实数据集；按材质类别而非仅按颜色分配光谱可在所有测试集上提升迁移；其投影 RAW 图像用于颜色恒常性训练时与现有合成 RAW 生成器相当。

Insight: 创新点在于构建材质标注反射光谱库，并按材质类别为场景对象分配实测反射光谱或光学常数、为光源和天空分配相应光谱，再通过逐波长路径追踪生成物理正确的高光谱图像；客观看，其核心价值是将图形学光谱渲染与低层视觉数据合成结合，并验证材质级光谱分配对真实数据迁移的有效性。

Abstract: Hyperspectral images (HSIs) record the full spectrum of light at every pixel, so projecting an HSI through a camera’s spectral sensitivities gives that camera’s RAW image, and one HSI can supply training data for hyperspectral reconstruction and camera-pipeline methods. Real HSIs, however, are slow and expensive to capture, and the largest datasets hold only about 1,000 images. High-level vision commonly replaces scarce real data with images rendered from 3D scenes. Low-level tasks, however, need physically correct spectra, which requires spectral rendering with a measured spectrum for every material in a scene, and existing spectral renderings cover only a handful of scenes, mostly with a simplified image formation model. We present Spectrify, a framework that converts 3D scene datasets into HSIs, together with a library of reflectance spectra labeled by material and compiled from close-range hyperspectral datasets and spectrophotometer measurements. In Spectrify, spectra follow the material: each object receives a physically based optical model and measured reflectance spectra or optical constants from its material category, each lamp an emission spectrum of its type and the sky a modeled daylight spectrum, and Mitsuba 3 path-traces the full light transport of each scene per wavelength. We show that a hyperspectral reconstruction network trained only on our synthetic HSIs generalizes to unseen real datasets better than networks trained on the largest real HSI datasets on most test sets, and that assigning spectra by material category instead of by color alone improves this transfer on every test set. RAW images projected from our HSIs are also useful training data for color constancy, performing on par with existing synthetic RAW generators. We release the 4,500 rendered HSIs, the code of Spectrify and its material-labeled reflectance library.

[185] EventDiffusion: Learning Native Event Stream Distribution from Videos cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Karthik Sundar Senthilkumar, Christopher Thirgood, Simon Hadfield

TL;DR: 论文提出EventDiffusion，一个分层生成框架，从真实视频/记录中直接学习原生异步事件流的条件分布，以缓解事件相机数据稀缺问题。方法将事件流生成分解为事件活动扩散（EAD）、事件空间分配（ESA）和事件时间扩散（ETD）三个概率过程，分别建模离散事件计数、像素级空间分配与基数保持、连续异步时间戳分布；同时提出事件流真实感指标CEFS。

Details

Motivation: 事件相机数据稀缺限制数据驱动研究，而稀疏RGB观测无法唯一确定中间事件流，相同帧可能对应不同活动、极性和时序的事件流；现有模拟器依赖预设传感器动态和估计亮度轨迹，限制了生成事件流的多样性与保真度。

Result: 论文引入CEFS事件流真实感指标；在所有数据集上，EventDiffusion的准确率达到SOTA方法的两倍，并展示出在不同相机和任意分辨率下的鲁棒性。

Insight: 摘要宣称的创新点在于直接从真实记录学习原生异步事件流分布，并将生成过程分解为活动计数、空间分配（保持基数）和时间戳三个扩散过程，同时提出综合活动、极性、多尺度时空结构和时间保真度的CEFS指标。客观来看，将事件流生成拆解为离散计数、空间基数保持和连续异步时间建模，比依赖预设传感器动态和亮度轨迹的模拟更贴近真实事件流统计，具有借鉴价值。

Abstract: Event cameras capture asynchronous brightness changes at high temporal resolution, but scarce recordings constrain data-driven research. Sparse RGB observations cannot uniquely determine intervening events since identical frames may correspond to streams with different activity, polarities, and timing. Existing simulators largely rely on prescribed sensor dynamics and estimated brightness trajectories, whose assumptions limit the diversity and fidelity of plausible event streams. We introduce EventDiffusion, a hierarchical generative framework that directly learns the conditional distribution of native asynchronous event streams from real recordings. EventDiffusion decomposes generation into three probabilistic processes: Event Activity Diffusion (EAD) learns stochastic discrete event counts; Event Spatial Allocation (ESA) distributes these counts to individual pixels while preserving cardinality; and Event Timing Diffusion (ETD) learns continuous asynchronous timestamp distributions. We also introduce CEFS, an event-stream realism metric combining event activity and polarity with multiscale space-time structure and temporal fidelity. Across all datasets, EventDiffusion achieves double the accuracy of SOTA methods and demonstrates robustness across different cameras at any resolution.

Abstract: We present Physicl Scenes, a high-quality artist-created and curated dataset of 6,499 3D scenes spanning household rooms, public interiors, and outdoor spaces. The dataset combines detailed geometry, physically based materials, and carefully composed environments with object- and part-level semantic annotations, providing rich, joint supervision for visual and embodied learning. We demonstrate its value as training data across inverse rendering, 3D reconstruction, scene understanding, and mobile manipulation. Across these tasks, training state-of-the-art methods with our scenes improves performance over alternative synthetic datasets. In addition, we introduce a mobile manipulation benchmark, evaluating vision-language-action models performing physical pick and place in diverse 3D scenes with realistic layouts, and using our part annotations to also score where each object is grasped. Our dataset will be released publicly to facilitate research in visual and embodied AI.

Zehan Zheng, Yanyuan Chen, Deming Li, Yutao Tang, Jianwen Xie

TL;DR: 论文提出 Lost on Campus，一个基于3D高斯泼溅重建的大规模真实户外3D环境具身空间推理基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在真实连续环境中的空间推理能力。该基准构建了统一的推理-行动评估框架，将孤立推理的诊断式问答与多模态指令下的闭环交互导航相结合，并从六个能力维度进行细粒度诊断。

Details

Motivation: 现有具身空间推理基准多基于静态图像或合成室内环境，模型可能依赖语义捷径，难以忠实评估真实野外环境中的空间推理能力，因此需要一个贴近真实户外连续3D环境的评估基准。

Result: 实验表明现有视觉语言模型在真实户外环境下表现较差，空间推理需求显著高于室内场景；主要瓶颈包括细粒度视觉对齐困难、空间前瞻和自我中心-异中心定位失败；论文未给出具体数值型SOTA结果。

Insight: 创新点在于使用3D高斯泼溅重建真实户外3D校园环境，并统一诊断式问答与闭环导航评估，从动作 grounding、空间前瞻、度量感知、目标导向规划、自我中心-异中心定位和时空一致性六方面细粒度诊断具身空间推理，强调感知、推理与行动在真实连续环境中的紧耦合评估。

Abstract: Embodied Spatial Reasoning (ESR) is central to deploying Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in real-world embodied tasks, yet remains inadequately evaluated. Existing benchmarks primarily assess spatial reasoning in static images or synthetic indoor environments, where semantic shortcuts often suffice, making it difficult to faithfully evaluate spatial reasoning capabilities in the wild. In light of this, we present Lost on Campus, a benchmark for evaluating ESR in large-scale real-world outdoor 3D environments reconstructed by 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our benchmark introduces a unified reasoning-action evaluation framework that seamlessly integrates diagnostic QA for isolated reasoning with closed-loop interactive navigation for active reasoning under multimodal instructions. To enable fine-grained diagnosis, we evaluate six capability aspects of ESR: action grounding, spatial foresight, metric awareness, goal-directed planning, ego-allocentric localization, and spatio-temporal consistency. Extensive experiments reveal that: (i) Compared to indoor settings, real-world outdoor environments impose substantially higher demands on spatial reasoning, where existing models exhibit poor performance; (ii) VLMs still struggle with fine-grained visual alignment, with failures in spatial foresight and ego-allocentric localization emerging as primary bottlenecks; (iii) Improving multimodal interaction capabilities and long-term spatial reasoning is crucial for advancing embodied intelligence. These findings highlight that faithful evaluation of ESR demands benchmarks tightly coupling perception, reasoning and action within realistic and continuous environments.

[188] One Take, Many Cameras: Audio-Grounded Shot Synthesis for Dialogue Re-Cinematography cs.CVPDF

Anthony Chen, Naomi Ken Korem, Matan Ben-Yosef, Tavi Halperin, Urska Jelercic

TL;DR: 论文研究对话视频重摄影，从单段广角对话视频合成多机位新镜头；方法利用语音音频的视角不变性约束任意视角下的唇动，基于预训练音视频扩散模型生成目标视角，并用说话人感知音频门控解决多人场景中静默听者被驱动口型的语音泄漏。在电影感配对视角基准上显著优于 Wan 3.0，并具野外鲁棒性。

Details

Motivation: 传统对话场景多机位覆盖拍摄成本高且常不可得；现有视频生成仅依赖视觉线索，源视频唇部不可见时生成唇动与语音不匹配。论文旨在从单视角广角视频合成音画同步的新机位镜头。

Result: 在覆盖多视角和景别的电影感配对视角基准上，相较最强基线 Wan 3.0，感知误差降低59%、关键点误差降低56%、语音泄漏降低97%；在野外场景也保持鲁棒。

Insight: 创新点在于将语音音频视为视角不变的唇动约束，改造预训练音视频扩散模型进行目标视角镜头合成；并提出无需训练的说话人感知音频门控，仅驱动目标镜头中真正说话的人脸，避免多人场景语音泄漏。

Abstract: Creating a dialogue scene typically requires cutting between multiple views (e.g., close-ups, medium shots, and over-the-shoulder shots) of the actors. Yet capturing such coverage is costly and in many settings unavailable. We study dialogue re-cinematography: synthesizing additional shots from a single wide-angle dialogue video. Existing video generators can synthesize new shots, but rely on visual cues alone, so when the source video barely shows the lips, the generated lip motion fails to match the speech. Our key observation is that the speech audio is view-invariant: it constrains lip motion in any target view. Building on this insight, we adapt a pretrained audio-visual diffusion model to generate the new shot from a target frame that specifies the view, a source video that contains the performance, and the speech audio that drives lip motion. In multi-person scenes, however, pretrained audio-visual models drive every visible face with the same speech, causing speech leakage, where silent listeners mouth the speaker’s words. We therefore introduce speaker-aware audio gating, a training-free module that lets the audio drive only the faces that speak in the target shot. On a cinematic paired-view benchmark spanning diverse viewpoints and framings, our method lowers perceptual error by 59%, landmark error by 56%, and speech leakage by 97% relative to the strongest baseline, Wan 3.0. Our method also remains robust on in-the-wild scenes, highlighting its potential for film post-production.

[189] BioLoom: Scaling Biomedical Vision-Language Models by Learning from the Structure of Scientific Articles cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Yuwei Yang, Xiaoxiao Sun, Alejandro Lozano, Yuan-Sen Ting, Yuhui Yuan

TL;DR: 论文提出 BioLoom，通过利用科学文献中图、子图、标题和正文之间的结构关系来增强生物医学视觉-语言模型的对比图文预训练。方法保留精确图文匹配，同时为相关图文对引入较弱正监督，对同内容类型的跨文章负样本加权，并利用视觉内容、原始图注和提及段落丰富子图描述，构建了包含 5240 万图文对的 Loom52M 数据集。基于该数据训练的 BioLoom（DFN-2B）在多个零样本分类和检索任务上显著优于现有生物医学 CLIP 基线。

Details

Motivation: 现有生物医学视觉-语言模型主要依赖扩大图文数据规模，但将文献中的图、子图和标题视为独立图文对，忽略了科学文章内部丰富的跨模态结构关系。论文希望从现有科学文献结构中挖掘额外监督信号，以学习更丰富的表示，而不仅依赖数据规模扩展。

Result: BioLoom（DFN-2B）在 43 个数据集、10 个领域的 67 个零样本分类任务上取得 56.82% 的领域平衡平均准确率，高于 MedPMC-CLIP 的 48.77%；在 BioLoomBench 简洁标题和 BioMedFlickr 上，双向平均 Recall@1 分别为 63.20% 和 10.59%，优于对应最强基线 BiomedCLIP 的 46.37% 和 MedPMC-CLIP 的 7.55%。

Insight: 创新点在于将科学文献的结构关系显式融入对比学习：用弱正监督建模相关图文对，用同内容类型跨文章负样本加权增强细粒度判别，并通过多源文本丰富子图描述，构建 Loom52M。客观来看，该工作把文献组织方式转化为低成本监督信号，兼顾数据构建与对比目标设计，有望提升生物医学多模态模型的细粒度理解和零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: The development of biomedical vision-language models (VLMs) with contrastive image-text pretraining has been driven by scaling to increasingly large and diverse image-text datasets. Beyond scaling, further progress can come from learning richer representations from existing data. We hypothesize that the structure of scientific literature itself provides an additional source of supervision: figures, panels, captions, and accompanying article text encode rich relationships across modalities. Yet, treating these as independent image-text pairs overlooks their relationships. To address this limitation, we incorporate these structural relationships into contrastive image-text supervision. We preserve exact image-text matches while introducing weaker positive supervision for related pairs, such as a panel and the caption of a sibling panel. We upweight cross-article negatives of the same content type to encourage finer discrimination. We also enrich panel descriptions using visual content, original figure captions, and mentioning paragraphs. Pairing panels with these descriptions and whole figures with their original captions yields Loom52M, a dataset of 52.4 million image-text pairs spanning scientific charts and biomedical images. Using Loom52M, BioLoom (DFN-2B) achieves 56.82% domain-balanced mean accuracy across 67 zero-shot classification tasks from 43 datasets in 10 domains, compared with 48.77% for MedPMC-CLIP. On BioLoomBench with concise captions and BioMedFlickr, it achieves bidirectional mean Recall@1 of 63.20% and 10.59%, versus 46.37% and 7.55% for the respective strongest evaluated baselines, BiomedCLIP and MedPMC-CLIP. Code, datasets, benchmarks, and models are available at https://bioloom-clip.github.io/.

TL;DR: 论文提出 DiaCap，一个用于综合对话字幕生成的视听 LLM，可为视频中每句话输出转写文本、时间戳以及说话人的视觉描述。模型将话语时间定位与视听说话人日志统一到单个模型中，并采用两阶段训练：先在大规模合成标注片段上预训练，再在人工校正片段上用强化学习细化时间、说话人分组和绑定。

Details

Motivation: 现有对话字幕需要同时回答“谁在说”和“何时说”，而话语时间定位与视听说话人日志高度耦合；论文希望用一个统一模型联合建模二者，生成更完整的对话字幕。

Abstract: We present DiaCap, an audiovisual LLM for comprehensive dialogue captioning, which produces the transcript, timestamps, and a visual description of the speaker for every utterance in a video. Considering that utterance timing and audiovisual diarization are tightly coupled, DiaCap unifies the tasks in a single model, trained in two complementary stages. First, we train DiaCap on 225K curated clips, using synthetic captions that densely label every utterance in detail. Second, we further refine the model via reinforcement learning on 2.9K human-corrected clips, using fine-grained feedback that directly evaluates utterance timing, speaker grouping, and speaker binding. On DiaDemBench, which we extend with utterance-level timestamps and subtitle redaction, both DiaCap with off-the-shelf transcripts and its end-to-end variant DiaCap-E2E achieve strong results. It also maintains strong diarization on multilingual and minute-long videos, and transfers to audiovisual question answering.

Abstract: Audio-video (AV) deepfakes can fake the face, the voice, or both. Most benchmarks only ask whether a clip is real or fake, so they cannot show whether a detector relies on the picture, the voice, or encoding traces. We present MOZAIC, a paired benchmark with a 4-way label in which every source clip is released in all four combinations of real or fake video and real or fake audio so each modality contribution can be measured directly. Fakes cover inner-face swapping, whole-frame video diffusion and voice conversion. MOZAIC has 137,745 clips from 15,014 source pairs and four tasks: T1 AV authenticity, T2 manipulation type, T3 identity verification and T4 explanation. We evaluate 12 AV multimodal LLMs (MLLMs), train four detectors and fine-tune MLLMs with LoRA. Overall, the best MLLM reaches only 35.6% on the four-way T1 and catches far more video-only fakes than voice-only fakes (43.6% vs 7.5%). Trained detectors reach 98.4% on T1, and LoRA fine-tuning raises explanation scores (OmniVinci 4.17 to 7.40, Qwen2.5-Omni 4.42 to 6.98), but the best T1 detector falls to 78.3% when the face-swap generator is held out of training, so generalization and explanation remain open. Understanding these behaviors requires reasoning about underlying facts, participant knowledge, and conversational context, making them difficult to evaluate reliably. To address this gap, we introduce InfoCon, a multimodal benchmark for fine-grained information manipulation in conversations. InfoCon identifies six fine-grained strategies and derives a coarse consistency label from their factual and interactional relations. We develop an evidence-grounded multi-agent annotation framework to construct and verify reliable labels from rich contextual evidence. We benchmark representative multimodal models and systematically analyze their fine-grained information manipulation understanding, providing a testbed beyond binary truth-deception judgments.

[194] Learning What vs. Learning How: Decomposing Task and Interface Competence in Agentic Training cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Chaeeun Lee, Hossein A. Rahmani, Aryo Pradipta Gema, Kexin Xu, Emine Yilmaz

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM智能体在不同harness（系统提示、动作格式、反馈循环等运行框架）下失败时，究竟是缺少任务知识还是缺少接口操作能力。作者将智能体能力拆分为任务能力与接口能力，分析多harness蒸馏和单harness训练对二者的影响，并提出harness rehearsal来单独锻炼目标接口。实验表明，跨harness任务监督与接口演练结合能显著提升性能，且on-policy RL对单harness训练更鲁棒。

Details

Motivation: LLM智能体可能具备解决任务的知识，却因不熟悉特定harness而无法表达该知识；论文希望区分任务能力与接口能力，从而诊断智能体失败来源并改进训练。

Result: 在四个推理与智能体基准上验证；在BFCL V3多轮、Qwen3-4B上，跨harness任务监督和harness rehearsal单独分别达8.5%和32.5%，二者结合达58.8%。此外，on-policy RL比蒸馏更不易因单harness训练而损害其他harness性能，且多harness蒸馏获得的接口能力可在后续RL中保持。

Insight: 核心创新是将智能体能力分解为任务能力与接口能力，并提出harness rehearsal：用携带最少任务知识但覆盖目标接口的轨迹进行蒸馏。论文还揭示任务监督与接口演练的互补性，以及RL在保持跨harness接口能力上的鲁棒性，为智能体训练数据设计和失败诊断提供了新视角。

Abstract: LLM agents operate through harnesses that define system prompts, action formats, feedback loops, and resource constraints. A model may possess the knowledge needed to solve a task yet fail to express that knowledge through a particular harness. This raises a basic question: when a policy fails under a given harness, is it missing task knowledge or the competence to operate the interface? We distinguish two components of agentic competence: task competence, the knowledge and reasoning needed to solve a task, and interface competence, the ability to act on that knowledge through a given harness. Across four reasoning and agentic benchmarks, we show that harness-conditioned training yields vastly different outcomes depending on how these interact. Multi-harness distillation aggregates task knowledge from trajectories collected through different harnesses into a shared policy, but this knowledge is realised only when training preserves or improves competence on the inference-time interface. Otherwise, single-harness training can induce interference in interface competence, sometimes catastrophically collapsing performance on a held-out harness despite improvements in underlying task knowledge. To disentangle task and interface supervision, we introduce harness rehearsal: distillation on traces that carry minimal task knowledge while exercising the target interface. Cross-harness task supervision or rehearsal alone yields little gain, but their combination yields gains far larger than either: on BFCL V3 multi-turn with Qwen3-4B, they achieve 8.5% and 32.5% respectively, versus 58.8% together. Finally, on-policy reinforcement learning (RL) is more robust than distillation to single-harness training: it does not substantially degrade performance on other harnesses, and interface competence acquired through multi-harness distillation persists through subsequent RL.

TL;DR: 论文提出 LimoBench，一个基于美国印第安纳州 West Lafayette 真实地图数据的街道级位置查询基准，包含 82 个自然语言查询，覆盖地理编码、逆地理编码、路径规划、方向性、范围搜索、k 近邻搜索和空间连接七类空间操作；同时提出基于 Model Context Protocol（MCP）的 Limo 方法，通过 PostGIS 空间数据和紧凑工具集增强 LLM 的位置感知能力。

Details

Motivation: LLM 正被用于地图问答、基于位置的服务和空间数据库自然语言接口等地理空间应用，但其是否真正具备街道级位置感知能力、能否可靠处理真实坐标、POI 和空间距离查询尚不明确，因此需要系统评测并提供增强方案。

Result: 无工具 grounding 的模型在各类空间操作上大多仅有个位数准确率，例如 GPT-6 Luna 为 3.1%；通过 MCP 接入 Limo 空间工具后，同一模型准确率接近完美，GPT-6 Luna 达到 99.3%，代价是增加 0.2 秒延迟、7.7 倍 token 和 2.2 倍成本。

Insight: 创新点在于构建街道级、多空间操作、按位置和语义复杂度分级的 LLM 位置感知基准，并利用 MCP 将 LLM 与 PostGIS 支撑的空间数据工具连接，以较低工程开销显著提升空间查询准确性，揭示了当前 LLM 原生空间知识不足而外部 grounding 有效的特点。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have transformed how humans interact with information, enabling natural-language interfaces across various domains. They are increasingly deployed for geospatial applications including map-based question answering, location-based services, and natural-language interfaces to spatial databases. But are LLMs truly location-aware, able to reliably interpret and precisely answer location queries involving real-world coordinates, points of interest (POIs), and geospatial distances? To answer this concretely, we present LimoBench, a street-level benchmark of 82 natural-language location queries over seven canonical spatial operations: geocoding, reverse geocoding, routing, directionality, range search, $k$-nearest-neighbor search, and spatial join. The queries are grounded in real map data of West Lafayette, Indiana, and are graded by location and semantic complexity. They are designed to probe LLMs’ street-level spatial awareness, from simple lookups to multi-step spatial reasoning. We also present Limo, an approach based on the Model Context Protocol (MCP) that enhances the location awareness of LLMs. Limo grounds LLMs in PostGIS-backed spatial data through a compact set of tools. We find that ungrounded models mostly achieve single-digit accuracy (3.1% for GPT-6 Luna) across different spatial operations. In contrast, the same models achieve near-perfect accuracy (99.3% for GPT-6 Luna) when given access to Limo’s spatial tools through MCP. Current LLMs thus have limited and unreliable street-level spatial knowledge, while grounding provides accurate answers at modest overhead (+0.2 s latency, 7.7$\times$ tokens, and 2.2$\times$ cost for GPT-6 Luna). We release the benchmark, Limo toolset, and evaluation code to encourage the community to evaluate their own location-aware LLM approaches.

[196] Compressible Representations: Functional Spines in Deep Neural Networks cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Niranjan Rajesh, Meenakshi Khosla

TL;DR: 论文提出一种识别深度网络各层中任务敏感子空间的框架，利用类似Fisher信息的隐藏表示矩阵对方向按下游影响排序并投影到最敏感子空间。实验表明预训练视觉网络表示高度可压缩，小比例任务敏感维度即可保留大部分性能，并可用于鲁棒性、持续学习和模型表示对齐分析。

Details

Motivation: 深层网络将输入嵌入高维表示，但其中多少容量真正驱动任务行为尚不清楚；论文希望识别功能上任务敏感的低维子空间，并利用它理解压缩性、鲁棒性和持续学习等问题。

Result: 在预训练视觉网络上，小比例任务敏感子空间即可保留大部分性能，且所需维度少于几何替代方法；压缩表示对全网络对抗攻击更鲁棒，持续学习约束优于基于方差的梯度投影方法，并揭示形状与纹理敏感方向基本不重叠及模型对齐中的任务相关不对称。

Insight: 创新点在于用类似Fisher信息的隐藏表示矩阵排序任务敏感方向，提出“功能脊柱”视角，将任务敏感子空间用于表示压缩、对抗鲁棒性、持续学习中的正交梯度约束以及更细粒度的跨模型表示比较。

Abstract: Deep networks embed inputs in high-dimensional representations, but how much of this capacity actually drives behavior? We introduce a framework for identifying, at each layer, the subspace of the representation that is functionally sensitive to the task. Using a matrix analogous to the Fisher information but defined over the hidden representation, we rank directions by their influence on downstream computation and project onto the most task-sensitive subspace. Across pretrained vision networks, representations are remarkably compressible: a small fraction of the task-sensitive subspace preserves most performance, often requiring far fewer dimensions than geometric alternatives. We find that this compressed representation is also more robust to adversarial attacks generated on the full network. We additionally show that this decomposition recovers functionally distinct subspaces within the same representation: on cue-conflict stimuli, shape-sensitive and texture-sensitive directions are largely non-overlapping, and ablating one selectively impairs the corresponding task while leaving the other intact. Task-sensitive subspaces also provide a principled foundation for continual learning: constraining gradient updates to lie orthogonal to the task-sensitive subspaces of prior tasks outperforms standard variance-based gradient projection methods. Finally, restricting representational comparisons to task-sensitive subspaces reveals that inter-model alignment is higher in task-sensitive directions than in task-insensitive directions at later layers, and that conditioning on different tasks over the same stimuli uncovers asymmetries that full-space similarity measures cannot detect. Together, these results establish that deep networks compute through a low-dimensional functional spine embedded within a much larger representational space.

[197] TableRouter: Query-Aware Evidence Construction for Long-Table Question Answering cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Anahita Bolourani, Xiaofeng Lin, Tung Sum Thomas Kwok, Guang Cheng

TL;DR: 论文提出 TableRouter，一个无需训练的查询感知证据构建框架，用于长表格问答。它先分析查询，再在“计算路线”（从完整表格计算问题相关统计量）和“覆盖路线”（检索相关行与列摘要）之间选择，并将选定上下文一次性交给冻结 LLM。方法在多个 LLM 和大表基准上验证，强调长表推理不仅依赖检索到哪些行，也依赖证据如何表示和使用。

Details

Motivation: 长表格超出 LLM 上下文预算时，仅检索相关行并不充分：检索子集可能遗漏精确聚合所需信息，即使证据完整，LLM 也可能难以直接利用。论文旨在解决长表问答中证据访问与证据使用脱节的问题。

Result: 在三个 LLM 和三个大表基准的九种设置中，TableRouter 均比六种单次调用基线中最强者高 1.2–18.3 个准确率点，且相比截断表格提示减少 77–95% 估计 FLOPs。在未见数据库长表上，Qwen2.5-7B 达到 35.8%，优于最强外部基线的 16.4%；控制实验显示显式提供计算统计量可提升最多 40.0 个点。

Insight: 创新点在于训练-free 的查询感知双路线证据构建，以及将“计算得到的统计量”作为一等证据表示。客观上，论文把长表推理问题从单纯行检索推进到证据表示与使用层面，说明精确聚合场景下预计算/查询条件化统计可能比扩大检索覆盖更有效。

Abstract: LLMs can reason over tables, but when tables outgrow the context budget, retrieving relevant rows is only part of the solution. A retrieved subset may omit information required for an exact aggregate; even complete evidence may remain difficult for an LLM to use. We introduce TableRouter, a training-free framework that analyzes each query, then chooses between two routes: a computation route, which derives question-conditioned statistics from the full table, and a coverage route, which retrieves relevant rows and column summaries. The chosen context is passed to a frozen LLM in one call. Across three LLMs and three large-table benchmarks, TableRouter outperforms the strongest of six single-call baselines in all nine settings by 1.2-18.3 accuracy points, using 77-95% fewer estimated FLOPs than truncated-table prompting, with indexing amortized across questions. The gains extend to long tables from databases not used in earlier experiments, where TableRouter achieves 35.8% with Qwen2.5-7B, versus 16.4% for the strongest external baseline. A controlled experiment shows that explicitly providing computed statistics improves accuracy by up to 40.0 points, even when all answer-determining rows are available. These results distinguish evidence access from evidence use: long-table reasoning depends on how information is represented, not only on which rows are retrieved.

TL;DR: 论文针对微调语言模型学习新事实时通用能力严重下降的问题，提出InstructReplay方法：让初始模型对一组通用指令生成答案，在微调新事实时约束模型在这些答案上的输出接近初始模型，从而在保持通用能力的同时有效学习新事实。

Details

Motivation: 标准微调会使模型指令遵循、推理和编码等通用基准平均下降35分，且与回答长度减半密切相关。论文旨在解决微调新事实与保留通用能力之间的冲突。

Result: 在三个模型家族和两个领域上，InstructReplay将五个通用基准的平均下降从28.1分降至2.4分，保持答案长度接近初始模型，学习新事实效果与标准微调相当，并在所有六个设置中优于七种适应方法。

Abstract: Fine-tuning an instruction-tuned language model on new facts often erodes its general abilities. We find that standard fine-tuning lowers instruction-following, reasoning and coding benchmarks by 35 points on average, and that these losses track a collapse in answer length: fine-tuned models at least halve their responses, and the further a response drifts from the initial model’s length, the more often it fails. We propose InstructReplay: the initial model answers a set of general instructions once, and during fine-tuning the model is kept close to the initial model on these answers. Across three model families and two domains, InstructReplay reduces the average decline on five general benchmarks from 28.1 to 2.4 points and keeps answer length close to the initial model’s, while learning the new facts as well as standard fine-tuning. It balances learning and retention better than seven adaptation methods in all six settings. Controlled comparisons attribute the gain to replaying the model’s own answers to general instructions rather than to the form of the loss.

Abstract: The use of AI agents among the general public has been increasing exponentially throughout 2026, though it is often hard for users to determine which configuration is most effective for their personal use. We find that model choice impacts performance more than harness choice, but the three leading models differ by only 1.1 percentage points. Abstract: As clinical AI agents begin to interact with electronic health records (EHRs), large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to evaluate their actions. But how reliably can these judges recognize clinically dangerous mistakes, especially when an agent appears to have completed its task successfully? We investigate this question using MedAgentBench by introducing controlled clinical errors into verified agent trajectories. The experiments include 416 faulty trajectories, eight error categories, and six open-weight LLM judges. By comparing each faulty trajectory with its correct counterpart, we measure whether judges genuinely recognize errors rather than simply flagging more actions as unsafe. The findings show that access to clinical evidence significantly improves judge reliability. When shown only the final answer, judges cannot distinguish errors that leave it unchanged, while flagging 56% of correct trajectories. With full trajectory information, the ability to distinguish unsafe actions from correct ones reaches a score of 0.75, and providing actual EHR responses improves discrimination by 0.39. Surprisingly, including the agent’s reasoning does not improve overall performance and reduces detection of incorrectly reported clinical values by 0.18, which remain the hardest errors to detect. MedGemma performed worse than the general-purpose model and was the only judge to miss potentially dangerous medication overdoses. We also find that the benchmark’s original grader accepts all 38 trajectories with an intentionally skipped laboratory check, while combining LLM judges with a rule-based checker informed by the full EHR record detects 97% of injected faults.

[202] SOARS: System One Assisted Reranking for Agentic Search cs.IR | cs.CLPDF

Amin Bigdeli, Liye Fu, Fattane Zarrinkalam, Joel Stremmel, Elizabeth Lingg

TL;DR: 论文提出SOARS，一个免训练的智能体搜索重排序框架，利用System One模型在每个搜索步骤中快速、校准地估计相关性，并单次调用完成长文档段落选择与候选重排序。方法同时考虑原始问题和智能体生成的子查询，在多个LLM骨干和两个基准上提升答案准确率与证据召回率，同时减少搜索调用、端到端延迟和推理成本。

Details

Motivation: 深度搜索智能体需要迭代检索与推理，检索证据的相关性直接影响下游表现；但现有重排序器若在搜索轨迹中反复调用，会带来显著延迟和成本。论文希望在不额外训练的情况下，实现更高效且有效的相关性估计与重排序。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus和WebShaper上，SOARS在六个LLM骨干上均一致提升答案准确率和证据召回率，并减少搜索调用、端到端延迟与推理成本。在BrowseComp-Plus上，将GPT-5.4 Mini的准确率从73.9%提升到84.7%，同时将搜索调用从23次降至14次。

Insight: 创新点在于用System One式快速相关性估计替代高成本重排序，并将长文档段落选择与候选重排序合并为单次调用，同时联合建模原始问题与智能体子查询。客观看，其免训练、低调用开销和面向智能体搜索轨迹的在线重排序设计，对提升深度搜索的效率与效果具有借鉴价值。

Abstract: Deep search agents address complex information needs through iterative retrieval and reasoning, where the relevance of retrieved evidence influences downstream performance. Existing rerankers improve retrieval effectiveness but introduce substantial latency and cost when invoked throughout the search trajectory. We introduce SOARS, a training-free framework leveraging a System One model for fast, calibrated relevance estimation. At each search step, SOARS selects query-relevant passages from long documents and reranks candidates in a single call, jointly considering the original question and agent-generated sub-query. Across six LLM backbones on BrowseComp-Plus and WebShaper, SOARS consistently improves answer accuracy and evidence recall while reducing search calls, end-to-end latency, and inference cost. On BrowseComp-Plus, it improves GPT-5.4 Mini’s accuracy from 73.9% to 84.7% while reducing search calls from 23 to 14, demonstrating the potential of System One reranking for more effective and efficient deep search.

[203] UniCon: Unified Framework for Efficient Contrastive Alignment via Kernels cs.LG | cs.CVPDF

Hangke Sui, Yuqing Wang, Minh N Do

TL;DR: 论文提出 UniCon，一个基于核方法的统一对比对齐框架，覆盖线性/非线性编码器以及一对多、多对多对齐场景。其核心是引入对比相似度权重矩阵 S(γ)，从而得到闭式全局解，并声称可用精确更新替代小批量反向传播。论文从 RKHS 视角统一对比对齐并建立其与谱方法的联系，在合成、单模态、多模态和零样本任务上验证效率与性能。

Details

Motivation: 现有对比目标虽支撑先进多模态模型，但训练缓慢且依赖长时间随机优化；论文希望在不牺牲通用性的前提下提升对比对齐的训练效率。

Result: 实验覆盖合成、单模态、多模态和零样本任务，结果表明 UniCon 在保持通用性和较强经验性能的同时取得显著效率收益；摘要未给出具体 benchmark 数值或 SOTA 对比。

Insight: 创新点在于用核化视角统一对比对齐，并通过 S(γ) 构造闭式全局解以替代小批量反向传播，同时揭示其与 RKHS 和谱方法的关系；客观看，其潜在价值在于为对比学习提供更高效且理论可解释的精确优化路径。

Abstract: Contrastive objectives power state-of-the-art multimodal models, but their training remains slow, relying on long stochastic optimization. We propose a Unified Framework for Efficient Contrastive Alignment via Kernels (UniCon), which spans linear and nonlinear encoders as well as one-to-one and many-to-many alignments. At its core, UniCon introduces the contrastive similarity weight matrix $S(γ)$, which enables closed-form global solutions that provably replace minibatch back-propagation with exact updates. Through the lens of reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHS), UniCon provides a kernelized perspective that unifies contrastive alignment and reveals its connection to spectral methods. To validate the theory, we conduct experiments on synthetic, unimodal, multimodal, and zero-shot tasks, demonstrating that UniCon achieves substantial efficiency gains while preserving generality and strong empirical performance.

[204] Verifiable On-Policy Distillation cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Juntong Shi, Stefano Ermon, Jure Leskovec, Minkai Xu

TL;DR: 论文提出可验证同策略蒸馏（VOPD），将强化学习中稀疏的验证器反馈转化为密集且基于验证结果的监督信号。方法在同策略蒸馏的隐式奖励视角下，把验证器偏好投影到教师隐式奖励方向，并对全词表进行Rao-Blackwell化，从而把可验证监督传播到未采样动作。实验表明，VOPD在多个数学推理基准上优于原始同策略蒸馏及其他基线。

Details

Motivation: RLVR的验证器奖励是序列级且稀疏的，只能通过采样轨迹传播，无法直接指导替代动作；而同策略蒸馏虽提供全词表密集教师反馈，却不以任务验证结果为根据。简单联合优化二者不能有效耦合稀疏验证信号与密集教师监督。

Result: 在两个教师-学生配置和八个数学推理基准上，VOPD相比原始同策略蒸馏及其他同策略蒸馏基线，一致提升最终性能和训练效率。

Insight: 核心创新是利用OPD的隐式奖励视角，将验证器反馈与教师监督统一到同一奖励空间，并把验证器偏好投影到教师隐式奖励方向后对全词表做Rao-Blackwell化，使监督覆盖未采样动作；该目标也可解释为根据教师与验证器一致性自适应外推蒸馏目标。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a powerful approach for improving language-model reasoning, but its supervision is inherently sparse: a sequence-level verifier reward is propagated only through sampled trajectories and provides no direct signal for alternative actions. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a complementary form of supervision by providing dense teacher feedback over the full next-token distribution, but this signal is not grounded in verified task outcomes. Simply co-optimizing the two objectives does not couple these signals: verifier feedback remains sparse and does not inform the dense teacher supervision. We introduce Verifiable On-Policy Distillation (VOPD), which transfers sparse verifier feedback into dense, verifier-grounded supervision. Our key observation is that the implicit-reward view of OPD places teacher supervision and verifier feedback in a common reward space while retaining a reward signal available for every next-token action. Under this view, VOPD projects verifier preference onto the teacher’s implicit-reward direction and Rao–Blackwellizes the resulting signal over the full vocabulary, propagating verifier-grounded supervision to actions that were never sampled. The resulting objective can also be interpreted as adaptively extrapolating the distillation target according to teacher–verifier agreement. Across two teacher–student configurations and eight mathematical reasoning benchmarks, VOPD consistently improves both final performance and learning efficiency over vanilla OPD and other on-policy distillation baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/juntongshi48/VOPD.

[205] SwiftLoop: Selective Refinement for Adaptive Implicit Reasoning in Looped Transformers cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Tianyi Zhao, Ke Wan, Chen Chen

TL;DR: 论文提出 SwiftLoop，一个面向循环 Transformer 隐式推理的推理时选择性精炼框架。它通过逐位置冻结机制，在循环深度和活跃宽度两个维度上自适应分配计算，避免固定深度循环中对所有隐状态位置的冗余更新。方法利用 K/V 可复用性和输出功能影响两类准则构造后见监督，学习轻量冻结策略，并在推理时根据隐藏状态自适应冻结。

Details

Motivation: 现有循环推理器通常采用固定深度递归，每次迭代都更新所有潜在位置，当进一步精炼不必要时会产生计算冗余。论文希望在不牺牲推理性能的情况下，实现更高效的动态计算分配。

Result: 摘要称大量实验表明 SwiftLoop 在准确率与计算量权衡上优于固定循环推理和自适应基线，并实现了可测量的推理加速；同时展示了其可扩展到一般循环模型中的递归 prompt prefilling。摘要未给出具体 benchmark 数值或 SOTA 声明。

Insight: 创新点在于将选择性精炼细化到循环 Transformer 的逐位置冻结，并同时从内部表示层面的 K/V 复用性和输出层面的功能影响学习冻结策略。客观上看，其可借鉴之处是推理时自适应计算不仅调节循环深度，还调节活跃宽度，并可推广到其他循环模型场景。

Abstract: Looped Transformers advance implicit chain-of-thought reasoning through recurrent latent refinement. However, existing looped reasoners typically employ fixed-depth recurrence, updating all latent positions at every iteration and incurring redundancy when further refinement is unnecessary. We introduce \textsc{SwiftLoop}, an inference-time framework for selective recurrent refinement through position-wise freezing, enabling adaptive computation allocation across both recurrent depth and active width. SwiftLoop learns lightweight freezing policies through hindsight-guided supervision derived from two complementary criteria: K/V reusability at the internal representation level and functional impact at the output level. At inference time, these policies guide adaptive freezing based on observed hidden states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SwiftLoop achieves favorable accuracy–computation trade-offs over fixed-loop inference and adaptive baselines, with measured inference acceleration. Beyond implicit reasoning, we further demonstrate its applicability to recurrent prompt prefilling in general looped models.

[206] Subliminal Learning Crosses Modalities cs.LG | cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Saraswathy Amjith, Kimia Hamidieh, Antonio Torralba, Yoon Kim

TL;DR: 论文研究语言模型中的潜意识学习（subliminal learning）能否跨模态迁移，发现通过数字序列等表面无意义数据传递的主题偏好，会在视觉语言模型中进一步影响图像生成与表示。作者用扩散模型验证学生模型的动物偏好会改变生成动物，但不影响颜色或风格，并从机制上发现学生模型变化中的恒定分量可解释大部分图像表示偏移，最后提出 weight lens 从模型权重识别潜意识特质。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注潜意识学习这一现象是否仅限于文本模态，以及主题/偏好如何跨模态影响视觉生成与表示，并试图揭示其作用机制。

Result: 实验表明，以学生模型为文本编码器的扩散模型比原始模型更频繁生成学生偏好的动物，但不改变颜色或风格；学生变化中的恒定分量可解释图像表示向该特质偏移的大部分，均匀加入该分量可改变生成动物；weight lens 能识别几乎所有学生模型的潜意识特质，包括未在生成图像中显现的特质。

Insight: 创新点在于证明文本中的潜意识学习可跨模态迁移到视觉语言模型和图像生成，并区分了依赖整体提示的主题概念与单 token 编码的颜色/风格概念；同时提出 weight lens 作为从模型权重诊断潜意识特质的方法，为理解无意义数据训练带来的隐性影响提供了新视角。

Abstract: Subliminal learning refers to a surprising phenomenon in language models wherein a prompt given to one model (e.g., preference for cats) can be subliminally distilled to another model via training on data that is ostensibly meaningless (e.g., number sequences). This paper shows that text-based subliminal learning transfers cross-modally, in particular, a topic/preference (e.g., animals) transferred through number sequences across vision-language models also changes how images are generated and represented. First, we show that a diffusion model using the student as its text encoder generates the student’s animal more often than with the original model as its text encoder, but not its color or style. We then investigate the mechanism behind this phenomenon and which concepts transfer across modalities. A constant component of the student’s change accounts for much of the shift of image representations toward the trait. Adding this component uniformly can change the generated animal, which depends on the whole prompt, but not concepts such as color or style, which are encoded in a single token. Finally, we introduce weight lens as an approach to identify a subliminal trait from the weights of a single trained model, and show that it identifies the trait of nearly every student, including traits that do not surface in generated images.

[207] Fix How It Acts, Not How It Thinks: Surgical Fine-Tuning of Frontier LLMs cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Sandeep Singh Sandha, Prann Bansal, Arvind Sundararajan

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向前沿LLM的“合并感知”微调方法，用LoRA对模型行为进行外科手术式修正，而不是改变其推理能力。作者指出小型LoRA更新在合并进BF16或FP8/NVFP4发布权重时会被严重丢失，因此在每个训练步都将基础权重与适配器更新按发布格式舍入，并在这些权重上计算损失，使训练模型与部署模型数值一致。该方法应用于Nemotron-3.5，在BF16和NVFP4版本上均提升智能体编码能力，最高在Terminal-Bench 2.1上提升8.4分，并公开释放模型。

Details

Motivation: 开放权重LLM虽具备软件工程能力，但在长轨迹任务中常因重复失败步骤、提前宣告完成等小动作失误而失败，而非推理能力不足。现有LoRA更新往往过小，合并进发布检查点后大量甚至绝大部分更新会消失，因此需要一种能让行为修正真正保留到部署权重中的微调方法。

Result: 在Nemotron-3.5上，该方法同时提升BF16和NVFP4发布版本的智能体编码表现，在Terminal-Bench 2.1上最高提升8.4分，并公开释放两个模型。

Insight: 创新点在于merge-aware fine-tuning：训练时每步将基础权重与适配器更新舍入到发布格式并保留厂商量化尺度，使前向数值匹配服务引擎且梯度可直通舍入；同时只优化模型动作、保持推理接近基座模型。客观看，其价值在于把“训练-合并-部署”数值鸿沟纳入优化目标，提升小更新在量化发布中的生存率。

Abstract: Open-weight LLMs are competent software engineers, but they sometimes fail at tasks they are otherwise able to do. Often it is not a failure of reasoning. It is a few small missteps over a long trajectory (repeating a step that has already failed, or declaring the task done while a requirement is still unmet) that make the difference. Correcting this calls for a surgical weight update that steers the model toward the right actions without interfering with its reasoning. Such updates, typically learned with LoRA adapters, are often too small to survive merging into a released checkpoint. In our BF16 release, the median weight change is only 1.5% of a single rounding step, and merging loses roughly two thirds of the update’s norm. When the same adapter is merged into the mixed FP8/NVFP4 release of the same model, 93% of the update disappears. Instead of increasing the size of the update, we directly train on the surviving part. We introduce merge-aware fine-tuning. Standard LoRA rounds the merged weights to the storage format only at export, so the loss never sees the rounded weights. We instead round the sum of base weights and adapter update to the release format at every training step, keeping the vendor’s quantization scales, and compute the loss on these weights. The forward pass matches the serving engine numerics, and the gradient passes straight through the rounding, so the model we train is the model we ship. The loss optimizes only the model’s actions and keeps its reasoning close to the base model. Applied to Nemotron-3.5, the recipe improves agentic coding in both the BF16 and the NVFP4 release, by up to 8.4 points on Terminal-Bench 2.1, and we publicly release both models.

[208] Calibrated Reading of Latent Reasoning and Communication cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Zixi Wang, Gaohao Zhou, Yubo Huang, Xin Lai, Yushe Cao

TL;DR: 论文提出校准潜在读取器CLR，用紧凑Transformer通过状态与候选答案的任务条件交互，读取潜在推理和键值通信中的中间信息并预测推理结果与消息内容；结合源适配器和共形校准实现跨模型迁移及覆盖/误报保证。实验在CODI模型和合成Qwen3-4B通信上验证了准确率、效率和消息替换检测能力。

Details

Motivation: 潜在推理与键值通信将中间信息存于连续表示，难以直接检查模型行为；现有激活读取器在低成本任务探针与灵活但昂贵的语言模型解码器之间取舍。论文旨在兼顾读取准确性、跨模型迁移和统计可靠性。

Result: 在两个CODI模型上，CLR较MLP和注意力探针在最终答案预测和参考值成员判断上提升4-18个百分点；在CODI-GPT-2上以少437-648倍推理FLOPs达到与1.7B LatentQA言语化器相当的准确率；在合成Qwen3-4B通信中，以名义5%假阳性率检测出93.1%的消息替换。

Abstract: Latent reasoning and key-value communication store intermediate information in continuous representations, limiting direct inspection of model behavior. Existing activation readers trade the low cost of task-specific probes against the flexibility of language-model decoders. We propose the Calibrated Latent Reader, CLR, a compact transformer that predicts reasoning outcomes and message content through task-conditioned interactions between states and candidate answers. Source adapters support transfer across models, while conformal calibration provides coverage and false-alarm guarantees under exchangeability. On two CODI models, CLR improves accuracy over MLP and attention probes by 4-18 percentage points for final-answer prediction and reference-value membership. On CODI-GPT-2, it achieves accuracy comparable to a 1.7B LatentQA-based verbalizer using 437-648 times fewer inference FLOPs. On synthetic Qwen3-4B communications, comparing decoded facts with public commitments detects 93.1% of message substitutions at a nominal 5% false-positive rate.

[209] See the Answer, Learn to Embed: Learning Universal Multimodal Embeddings from Hindsight cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CV | cs.IRPDF

Yoonki Cho, Youngju Na, Taegeun Yang, Jinhwan Seo, Sung-Eui Yoon

TL;DR: 论文提出Hindsight-Enhanced Multimodal Embeddings（HEME），探索在训练时利用配对候选（即“看到答案”）作为特权上下文来增强通用多模态嵌入学习，但在推理时不再依赖候选条件或额外推理。HEME先训练一个受约束的hindsight teacher，再将其hindsight表示和相似度分布蒸馏到独立student embedder中。实验在MMEB-V2以及MMEB-V3的agent和text任务上验证了方法有效性，并显示其推理比显式推理驱动方法更高效。

Details

Motivation: 现有推理驱动的通用多模态嵌入方法在推理时需要额外中间推理来生成上下文，计算开销较大。论文希望利用训练时可见的配对候选作为上下文来源，使模型“见答案学习嵌入”，从而在推理时无需答案或额外推理。

Result: 在MMEB-V2和MMEB-V3的agent与text任务上实验表明HEME有效，并比显式推理驱动方法具有更高效的推理；摘要未给出具体数值或SOTA声明。

Insight: 创新点在于将配对候选作为训练时特权上下文，通过hindsight teacher蒸馏到独立student，实现推理阶段解耦候选条件化。其客观可借鉴之处是用hindsight attention mask、错配候选条件化和query-token对齐约束教师，防止过度依赖候选信息及表示坍缩。

Abstract: Universal Multimodal Embedding (UME) models map heterogeneous multimodal inputs into a shared embedding space, where pairwise similarity supports diverse downstream tasks. Recent reasoning-driven UME approaches use intermediate reasoning to generate additional context for embedding, but often incur extra computation at inference. In this paper, we explore the matched candidate (i.e., the paired target) as an alternative source of context, asking whether a model can “see the answer” during training and “learn to embed” without it at inference. However, direct conditioning on this privileged context can introduce candidate-specific information unavailable from the query alone or cause the candidate-conditioned query representation to collapse toward the candidate embedding. To address this, we introduce Hindsight-Enhanced Multimodal Embeddings (HEME), which learns query-grounded hindsight representations from such candidate context. HEME first trains a hindsight teacher with a hindsight attention mask, mismatched-candidate conditioning, and query-token alignment to limit excessive reliance on the candidate. It then freezes the teacher and distills its hindsight representation and similarity distribution into a standalone student embedder. At inference, the student independently encodes queries and candidates without candidate conditioning or the hindsight teacher. Experiments on MMEB-V2 and the agent and text tasks of MMEB-V3 demonstrate the effectiveness of HEME and show that it enables more efficient inference than explicit reasoning-driven methods.

[210] Sample-Efficient In-Context Adaptation via Sleep cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Sangyun Lee, Zhang Enyan, Michael Xu, Hao Tang, Zinan Lin

TL;DR: 论文研究在Transformer语言模型中引入“睡眠”机制，即对一段上下文执行N次循环前向传播以精炼表示，并用最多1.2B参数、100B token预训练验证其能否提升通用非合成任务的上下文适应与样本效率。结果显示更长睡眠可加快few-shot算法和QA任务的上下文学习，降低OOD下一词预测损失，并在代码任务上以更少上下文token达到无睡眠基线损失。

Details

Motivation: 探索睡眠机制是否不仅对SSM-attention混合模型的长上下文推理有效，也能让Transformer语言模型从少量交互、陌生代码片段或实验结果中高效适应并泛化，从而改善样本效率以及上下文适应与解码延迟之间的权衡。

Result: 在多个few-shot算法和问答任务上，更长睡眠（更大N）使模型上下文学习更快；OOD下一词预测损失更低；在held-out代码上，N=2和N=4分别用2.10倍和2.39倍更少的上下文token匹配无睡眠基线的损失。

Insight: 创新点在于将睡眠/循环前向精炼用于Transformer语言模型的通用上下文适应，而不仅限于长上下文推理，并在较大预训练规模上验证。客观看，其核心是以额外循环计算换取更强的样本效率和更少上下文token，揭示了上下文适应与解码延迟之间的新权衡。

Abstract: The ability to learn and generalize from limited experience, or sample efficiency, is a cornerstone of intelligence. How can we build language models that turn a few user interactions, a few snippets of unfamiliar code, or a handful of experimental results provided in context into knowledge that generalizes? Prior work has found that sleep, a process where the model performs $N$ recurrent forward passes to refine its representations of a chunk of context, can improve long-context reasoning (specifically, within SSM-attention hybrid models). We study whether this method also improves in-context adaptation on general, non-synthetic tasks for transformer language models. To this end, we pre-train language models up to 1.2B-parameters on up to 100B tokens with sleep mechanisms. Notably, longer sleep (i.e., using a larger number of recurrent forward passes $N$) enables the model to learn faster in-context on multiple few-shot algorithmic, question-answering tasks, while also achieving lower next-token prediction loss on out-of-distribution evaluations. On held-out code, models with $N=2$ and $N=4$ match the no-sleep baseline’s loss using $2.10\times$ and $2.39\times$ fewer tokens in-context, respectively. More broadly, we view our work as a step toward building models that have better tradeoffs between in-context adaptation and decoding latency.

[211] Can a Frontier LLM Repair a Quantized Network by Tool-Free Weight-Space Programming? cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Steffen Urban

TL;DR: 论文将2万参数CIFAR-10 CNN的完整权重以文本形式输入Claude系列四个LLM，要求其在无工具、无数据、不能运行推理的条件下完成4-bit量化/修复。作者设计四种评测协议，分析LLM能否通过权重空间编程恢复量化损失；结果显示前沿LLM可在给定退化网络时显著恢复精度，但仅给浮点网络时多退化为普通RTN。

Details

Motivation: 关注4-bit RTN量化导致CNN精度大幅下降，探索前沿LLM是否能不依赖校准数据、工具和推理，仅通过阅读和改写权重文本进行后训练量化修复。

Result: RTN将精度从81.4%降至60.4%；Opus 5.5每层给一个答案，通过平衡每个3x3卷积核内舍入误差，将4-bit网络恢复到77.95%，比浮点网络低3.5个百分点，比AdaRound低2.8个百分点。仅给浮点网络时LLM多退回RTN，最多做轻微scale裁剪，仍使Fable 5.2的量化网络达到70.0%。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新是无工具权重空间编程：让LLM直接对整数权重进行推理并平衡卷积核内舍入误差。客观看，其价值更多在于系统评测LLM的量化修复能力与失败模式，而非提出超越数据驱动PTQ的方法；LLM需被告知精度下降才有效，自主优化能力仍有限。

Abstract: We give four large language models (LLMs) of the Claude family the complete weights of a 20k-parameter CIFAR-10 CNN as text and ask them to produce a 4-bit network without tools, data or the ability to run inference. Standard round-to-nearest (RTN) quantization to 4 bit lowers the test accuracy from 81.4% to 60.4%. We design four evaluation protocols that differ in the input and task description given to the LLMs and analyze their responses. In the first two protocols the LLMs are given the float and the 4-bit RTN network and are told that the accuracy has dropped significantly. In the last two they receive only the float network and the task to quantize it, which tests whether they apply an optimization on their own or fall back to plain RTN. Our analysis shows that a frontier LLM can recover most of the lost accuracy. Given one answer per layer, Opus 5.5 raises the accuracy of the 4-bit network to 77.95% by balancing the rounding errors within each 3x3 kernel, reasoning over the integer weights without tools. This is 3.5 pp below the float network and 2.8 pp below AdaRound, which learns the rounding from calibration data. Given only the float network, the LLMs name error-cancelling corrections but do not apply them and fall back to plain RTN, at most with mildly clipped scales, which still lifts Fable 5.2’s quantized network to 70.0%. We analyze in detail what the LLMs did and why it works. Code and prompts can be found at https://github.com/urbste/weight-space-programming-ptq

[212] A Bias in Disguise: Tiny Gradient Edits Stop a Small Language Model’s Thinking Loops and Complement a Frontier Model’s Weight Edit cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Hussamuddin Syed

TL;DR: 论文研究小型语言模型 Qwen3.5-0.8B 在 thinking 模式下难以结束推理循环的问题，提出只微调梯度最大的极少数权重（140–139,750 个）即可显著提升 MATH-500 准确率，并将该梯度编辑与 GPT-6 Astra 的无梯度权重编辑结合，发现二者互补，可在更少 token 下取得更高准确率。

Details

Motivation: 小模型在 thinking 模式中常陷入推理循环、难以闭合 think block，导致低 token 预算下准确率很低；作者希望补上缺失的梯度基线，并探索轻量权重编辑能否停止循环且与前沿模型的大规模权重编辑互补。

Result: 在 MATH-500 上，4k token 预算下梯度编辑将准确率从 15.4% 提升到 39.0–45.5%，仅需 50 个训练样例和单 GPU 2–9 分钟；4k 时优于 Astra 编辑（39.5% vs 6.7%），Astra 在 32k 协议下复现为 52.1%；两者结合达 62.2%，比单独 Astra 高约 10 点且 token 数减半。

Insight: 创新在于用梯度挑选极少数关键权重进行微小编辑，并揭示其本质等价于在前三层近似恒定通道上向残差流加入固定偏置向量，从而打断 thinking 循环；同时证明这种轻量梯度编辑可与前沿模型权重编辑互补。

Abstract: Qwen3.5-0.8B rarely finishes reasoning in thinking mode: at a 4,096-token budget only 1 or 2 of 500 MATH-500 answers close their think block (two seeds), and accuracy is 15.4%. A recent demonstration had GPT-6 Astra edit 131,072 of the model’s weights without gradients and reported MATH-500 rising from 7.9% to 48.1% at a 32,768-token budget. We provide the missing gradient baseline, then combine the two edits. Fine-tuning only the 140 to 139,750 weights with the largest gradient, on the model’s own correct answers to MATH training problems, raises thinking-mode MATH-500 at 4k tokens to 39.0-45.5% after 2-9 minutes of training on one GPU; 50 training examples suffice, and 1,398 random positions give only 16.3%. The gradient concentrates the edit on the most nearly constant channel of each of the first three layers. Because the model has no bias terms, an edit confined to those channels acts as a constant vector added to the residual stream: adding three fixed vectors to the unedited model matches the accuracy of such an edit. Under the Astra protocol (32k tokens, presence penalty 1.5), the Astra edit scores 52.1% (finished and correct) in our reproduction and our full-strength edits reach 45.6-45.7%, but ours win at every budget up to 16k tokens (39.5% against 6.7% at 4k). The two edits are complementary. The Astra edit plus our 139,750-weight edit at half strength scores 62.2% at 32k, 10 points above the Astra edit alone (p = 3e-9), with half as many tokens; our half-strength edit alone scores 46.7%.

[213] Residual Guidance for Real-Time Interactive Video Generation cs.MM | cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

NaHyeon Park, Kyeong-Hwan Moon, Youjin Jeon, Hyunjung Shim

TL;DR: 论文研究实时交互式视频生成中用户切换提示词后，流式模型仍受上一段视频上下文惯性影响而继续遵循旧提示词的问题。提出 Residual Guidance（RG），一种无需训练的方法：在新提示词下对重加噪的上一段做一次无缓存去噪，将得到的残差加到下一段早期去噪步骤中，以抵消上下文惯性。该方法可集成到现有流式视频生成流程，仅增加每段一次前向计算。

Details

Motivation: 现有按块生成的流式视频模型在用户切换提示词后，容易受上一块内容的强先验压制，导致新提示词失效；直接丢弃上下文又会破坏视觉连续性，因此需要在不破坏缓存连续性的前提下提升提示词切换能力。

Result: 在从单次切换到长序列多次切换的设置中，RG 显著提升了现有流式模型的提示词切换能力，包括已有专门切换机制的模型，同时基本保持视觉质量；无需训练和逐模型调参，每块仅增加一次前向传播。

Insight: 创新点在于将上下文惯性视为缓存内先验过强的问题，并用缓存外的校正信号来抵消：在新提示词下对上一块做无缓存去噪得到残差，再注入下一块早期去噪步骤。其客观可取之处是训练免费、模型无关、易集成，且针对提示词影响最大的早期步骤进行干预。

Abstract: Real-time interactive video generation allows users to change the prompt during generation to steer the evolving video. However, existing streaming models, which generate videos chunk by chunk, often keep following the previous prompt after a switch. We trace this failure to context inertia, where the previous chunk acts as a strong prior that suppresses the new prompt. Furthermore, we find that this inertia persists even when the cache retains only the previous chunk, yet discarding the context breaks visual continuity, which limits approaches that edit the cache. Rather than editing the cache, we propose Residual Guidance (RG), a simple, training-free method that counteracts context inertia with a correction measured outside the cache. Specifically, RG denoises the re-noised previous chunk once under the new prompt without the cache and adds the resulting residual to the early denoising steps of the next chunk, where its content is largely decided and the new prompt is most suppressed. Across settings ranging from a single switch to long multi-switch generation, RG substantially improves prompt switching capability on existing streaming models, including those with dedicated switching mechanisms, while largely retaining visual quality. As it requires no training or per-model tuning and adds only one forward pass per chunk, RG can be easily integrated into existing real-time video generation pipelines.
